<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1">
<title>OKPELLA Risk Assessment Generator</title>
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Oswald:wght@500;600;700&family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700&family=IBM+Plex+Mono:wght@500;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<style>
:root{
  --bg:#eef3fa; --surface-2:#e4eefb; --paper:#ffffff; --paper-dim:#f2f6fc;
  --ink:#14202e; --ink-dim:#5c7086; --line:#d7e3ee;
  --blue-deep:#123a73; --blue:#1d63c4; --blue-dark:#0f4694; --blue-light:#e4eefb;
  --red:#c6303e; --red-dark:#a2242f;
  --e:#c6303e; --h:#e0793a; --m:#d9b23a; --l:#4f9d63; --n:#4a7fc9;
  --radius:10px;
}
*{box-sizing:border-box;-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact;color-adjust:exact;}
html,body{margin:0;padding:0;}
body{background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font-family:'Inter',system-ui,sans-serif;-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;min-height:100vh;}
h1,h2,h3,.display{font-family:'Oswald',sans-serif;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.04em;}

.appbar{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:40;background:var(--blue-deep);border-bottom:3px solid var(--red);}
.appbar-inner{padding:13px 16px 11px;}
.appbar h1{color:#fff;font-size:18px;margin:0 0 2px;font-weight:700;}
.appbar .sub{color:#bcd2ee;font-size:11.5px;margin:0;text-transform:none;font-family:'Inter';}
.navbar{display:flex;background:var(--surface-2);}
.nav-btn{flex:1;background:none;border:none;color:var(--ink-dim);padding:9px 4px;font-size:11px;
  font-family:'Oswald',sans-serif;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.03em;font-weight:600;
  border-bottom:3px solid transparent;cursor:pointer;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:rgba(29,99,196,.15);}
.nav-btn:hover{color:var(--blue-deep);background:rgba(255,255,255,.5);}
button,label,select,input[type=checkbox],.acc-head,.preset-card,.reg-tab,.chip-sel,.settings-link{cursor:pointer;}
.acc-head:hover,.reg-tab:hover{color:var(--blue-deep);}
.preset-card:hover{border-color:var(--blue);background:var(--blue-light);}
.btn:hover{filter:brightness(1.07);}
.nav-btn.active{color:var(--blue-deep);border-bottom-color:var(--red);}

.wrap{max-width:680px;margin:0 auto;padding:14px 14px 130px;}
.card{background:var(--paper);border-radius:var(--radius);padding:16px;margin-bottom:14px;box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.08);}
.card h2{font-size:14px;margin:0 0 10px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;}
.card h2 .num{background:var(--blue-deep);color:#fff;width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:5px;display:inline-flex;
  align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:12px;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono';font-weight:600;flex:none;}
.hint{font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-dim);margin:-4px 0 12px;line-height:1.45;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;font-family:'Inter';}

label.field-label{display:block;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-dim);margin-bottom:5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.03em;}
select,input[type=text],input[type=password],textarea{width:100%;padding:11px 12px;border-radius:8px;border:1.5px solid var(--line);
  font-size:15px;font-family:'Inter';color:var(--ink);background:#fff;appearance:none;-webkit-appearance:none;}
input::placeholder,textarea::placeholder{color:#a7b2bd;font-style:italic;}
select{background-image:url("data:image/svg+xml;utf8,<svg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 20 20'><path fill='%235c7086' d='M5 7l5 6 5-6z'/></svg>");
  background-repeat:no-repeat;background-position:right 10px center;padding-right:32px;}
textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:60px;}
.field{margin-bottom:12px;} .field:last-child{margin-bottom:0;}
.two-col{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;}

.btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px;border:none;border-radius:8px;padding:13px 18px;
  font-size:14px;font-weight:700;font-family:'Oswald';text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em;cursor:pointer;width:100%;}
.btn-primary{background:var(--blue);color:#fff;} .btn-primary:active{background:var(--blue-dark);}
.btn-secondary{background:var(--blue-light);color:var(--blue-deep);}
.btn-ghost{background:transparent;color:var(--ink);border:1.5px solid var(--line);}
.btn-sm{padding:9px 12px;font-size:12px;width:auto;}
.action-bar{position:fixed;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;z-index:50;background:var(--blue-deep);border-top:1px solid #0b2c5c;
  padding:10px 14px calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));display:flex;gap:10px;}
.action-bar .btn{flex:1;}
.action-bar .btn-ghost{background:rgba(255,255,255,.08);color:#fff;border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.3);}

/* ---- Start screen: preset cards ---- */
.preset-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:8px;}
.preset-card{text-align:left;background:var(--paper);border:1.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:9px;padding:11px 13px;cursor:pointer;width:100%;}
.preset-card:active{background:var(--blue-light);border-color:var(--blue);}
.preset-card .pt{font-weight:700;font-size:13.5px;color:var(--ink);font-family:'Inter';}
.preset-card .pd{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-dim);margin-top:3px;line-height:1.4;}
.preset-card .pm{font-size:10.5px;color:var(--blue);margin-top:5px;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono';font-weight:600;}
.or-divider{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin:16px 0 12px;color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:11px;
  text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;font-family:'Oswald';}
.or-divider::before,.or-divider::after{content:"";flex:1;height:1px;background:var(--line);}

/* ---- Selected activity chips ---- */
.sel-summary{background:var(--blue-light);border-radius:9px;padding:11px 13px;margin-bottom:12px;}
.sel-summary .sl{font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--blue-deep);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em;margin-bottom:7px;font-family:'Oswald';}
.chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;}
.chip-sel{background:var(--blue);color:#fff;font-size:12px;padding:5px 10px 5px 11px;border-radius:14px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;border:none;}
.chip-sel .x{font-size:15px;line-height:1;opacity:.8;}
.chip-empty{font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-dim);font-style:italic;}

/* ---- Category accordion ---- */
.acc-item{border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);}
.acc-head{width:100%;background:none;border:none;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;
  padding:11px 2px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink);text-align:left;font-family:'Inter';cursor:pointer;}
.acc-head .cnt{font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-dim);font-family:'IBM Plex Mono';}
.acc-head .cnt.on{color:#fff;background:var(--blue);padding:2px 7px;border-radius:10px;}
.acc-body{display:none;padding-bottom:8px;}
.acc-item.open .acc-body{display:block;}
.acc-item.open .acc-head{color:var(--blue-deep);}
.tag-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:7px 4px;border-radius:6px;}
.tag-row input[type=checkbox]{width:19px;height:19px;flex:none;accent-color:var(--blue);}
.tag-row label{font-size:13.5px;flex:1;}

/* ---- Report ---- */
#report{display:none;} #report.visible{display:block;}
.report-banner{background:var(--blue-deep);color:#fff;padding:16px;border-radius:var(--radius) var(--radius) 0 0;text-align:center;}
.report-banner .company{font-size:16px;font-weight:700;}
.report-banner .form-title{font-size:13px;color:#bcd2ee;margin-top:2px;letter-spacing:.06em;}
.report-body{background:#fff;border-radius:0 0 var(--radius) var(--radius);padding:16px;}
.kv-row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-dim);font-size:13px;}
.kv-row .k{color:var(--ink-dim);font-weight:600;flex:none;width:40%;}
.kv-row .v{text-align:right;flex:1;font-weight:500;}
.summary-strip{display:flex;gap:6px;margin:14px 0;flex-wrap:wrap;}
.chip{font-family:'IBM Plex Mono';font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:#fff;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:20px;}
.chip.E{background:var(--e);} .chip.H{background:var(--h);} .chip.M{background:var(--m);color:#3a2f00;}
.chip.L{background:var(--l);} .chip.N{background:var(--n);} .chip.total{background:var(--blue-deep);}
.hazard-section{margin-top:18px;}
.hazard-section h3{font-size:12.5px;background:var(--blue-deep);color:#fff;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:6px;margin:0 0 8px;}
.hazard-card{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;margin-bottom:8px;}
.hz-top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:6px;}
.hz-title{font-size:13.5px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.35;font-family:'Inter';}
.lvl-badge{font-family:'IBM Plex Mono';font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#fff;border-radius:5px;padding:2px 7px;flex:none;}
.lvl-badge.E{background:var(--e);} .lvl-badge.H{background:var(--h);} .lvl-badge.M{background:var(--m);color:#3a2f00;}
.lvl-badge.L{background:var(--l);} .lvl-badge.N{background:var(--n);} .lvl-badge.blank{background:#c7ccd1;color:#555;}
.hz-control{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-dim);line-height:1.45;}
.hz-arrow{font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-dim);margin:5px 0;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;}
.hz-src{font-size:10px;color:#8b99a8;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono';margin-top:4px;}
.ppe-list{font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.6;background:var(--paper-dim);padding:10px 12px;border-radius:6px;}
.erp-box{background:var(--blue-light);border:1px solid var(--line);border-left:4px solid var(--red);border-radius:8px;
  padding:10px 12px;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.6;margin-top:14px;}
.erp-box strong{display:block;margin-bottom:4px;color:var(--red-dark);}
.sign-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-top:8px;}
.empty-note{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-dim);padding:10px;background:var(--paper-dim);border-radius:6px;text-align:center;}

/* ---- Register / Admin / AI ---- */
.reg-tabs{display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:12px;overflow-x:auto;}
.reg-tab{flex:none;background:var(--paper-dim);border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--ink-dim);padding:7px 12px;
  border-radius:20px;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap;}
.reg-tab.active{background:var(--blue-deep);color:#fff;border-color:var(--blue-deep);}
.reg-item{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;margin-bottom:8px;font-size:13px;}
.reg-item .reg-title{font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px;}
.reg-item .reg-meta{color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:12px;line-height:1.5;}
.reg-count{font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-dim);margin-bottom:10px;}
.mini-chip{font-size:10.5px;background:var(--paper-dim);color:var(--ink-dim);padding:2px 7px;border-radius:12px;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono';}
.gate-box{text-align:center;padding:24px 12px;}
.gate-box .lock{font-size:32px;margin-bottom:10px;}
.gate-box input{max-width:220px;margin:0 auto 10px;text-align:center;}
.gate-error{color:var(--e);font-size:12.5px;margin-top:6px;display:none;}
.admin-section{display:none;} .admin-section.unlocked{display:block;}
.upload-drop{border:2px dashed var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:20px 12px;text-align:center;color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:13px;margin-bottom:12px;}
.status-msg{font-size:13px;border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;margin-top:10px;display:none;}
.status-msg.success{background:#e7f5ec;color:#1f6b3a;display:block;}
.status-msg.error{background:#fbe9e7;color:#a63a2a;display:block;}
.data-summary{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-dim);line-height:1.7;background:var(--paper-dim);border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;margin-top:12px;}
.data-summary b{color:var(--ink);}
.chat-scroll{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;margin-bottom:12px;}
.chat-bubble{max-width:88%;padding:10px 13px;border-radius:14px;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.45;}
.chat-bubble.bot{background:var(--blue-light);align-self:flex-start;border-bottom-left-radius:4px;}
.chat-bubble.user{background:var(--blue);color:#fff;align-self:flex-end;border-bottom-right-radius:4px;}
.mini-note{display:block;font-size:10.5px;color:var(--ink-dim);margin-top:4px;font-style:italic;}
.chat-input-row{display:flex;gap:8px;}
.chat-input-row textarea{flex:1;min-height:44px;}
.chat-input-row button{flex:none;width:52px;}
.ai-mode-badge{font-size:10.5px;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono';padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:var(--blue-light);color:var(--blue-deep);font-weight:600;}
.ai-mode-badge.live{background:#e2f4e8;color:#1f6b3a;}
.ai-key-box{display:none;margin-top:10px;padding-top:10px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);}
.ai-key-box.open{display:block;}
.settings-link{background:none;border:none;color:var(--blue);font-size:12px;font-weight:600;text-decoration:underline;padding:0;margin-left:auto;}
.ai-disclosure{font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-dim);margin-top:6px;line-height:1.5;}

.log-row{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:9px 11px;margin-bottom:7px;font-size:12px;}
.log-row .ls{font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:10.5px;color:var(--blue-deep);font-weight:600;}
.log-row .lm{color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:11.5px;line-height:1.45;margin-top:3px;}
.log-empty{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-dim);text-align:center;padding:16px;}
.admin-sub{font-size:13px;margin:22px 0 8px;font-family:'Oswald';text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em;color:var(--blue-deep);}
.btn-row{display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:8px;}
.btn-row .btn{flex:1;}

/* ---- hazard verification: include / exclude ---- */
.hz-check{display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:9px;}
.hz-check input[type=checkbox]{width:19px;height:19px;flex:none;margin-top:2px;accent-color:var(--blue);}
.hazard-card.excluded{background:repeating-linear-gradient(135deg,#f6f7f9 0 10px,#eceff3 10px 20px);border-color:#c9d2db;}
.hazard-card.excluded .hz-title{text-decoration:line-through;color:#7d8996;}
.hazard-card.excluded .hz-control,.hazard-card.excluded .hz-arrow,.hazard-card.excluded .hz-src{color:#98a3ae;}
.hazard-card.excluded .lvl-badge{opacity:.45;filter:grayscale(.75);}
.excl-tag{display:none;margin-top:7px;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.03em;
  color:#5f6b77;background:#e6eaee;border:1px dashed #aab5c0;border-radius:4px;padding:3px 8px;}
.hazard-card.excluded .excl-tag{display:inline-block;}
.verify-bar{background:#fff8e6;border:1px solid #f0d9a0;border-left:4px solid #d9a441;border-radius:8px;
  padding:10px 12px;margin:12px 0;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.5;}
.verify-bar b{color:#8a6414;}
.verify-counts{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:7px;}
.vc{font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:10.5px;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:10px;}
.vc.inc{background:#e2f0e6;color:#1f6b3a;} .vc.exc{background:#e9edf1;color:#6b7783;}
.quick-chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin-top:8px;}
.qchip{background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--blue);color:var(--blue-deep);border-radius:14px;
  padding:5px 11px;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;}
.qchip:hover{background:var(--blue);color:#fff;}
.chat-select{width:100%;margin-top:8px;padding:7px 9px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;
  font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-size:12.5px;background:#fff;color:var(--ink);}
.chat-bubble.qcard{max-width:96%;}
.qcard-head{font-weight:600;color:var(--blue-deep);}
.qgroup{margin-top:10px;border-top:1px dashed #b3cae6;padding-top:8px;}
.qgroup-title{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;gap:8px;
  font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--blue-deep);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em;}
.qprog{flex:none;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:10px;background:#fff;
  border:1px solid var(--blue);color:var(--blue-deep);border-radius:9px;padding:1px 7px;
  text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;white-space:nowrap;}
.qitem{margin-top:8px;padding:7px 9px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.6);border-radius:8px;
  border-left:3px solid transparent;}
.qitem.active{background:#fff;border-left-color:var(--red);}
.qitem.waiting{opacity:.6;}
.qnum{display:inline-block;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:600;
  background:var(--blue);color:#fff;border-radius:8px;padding:1px 7px;margin-right:6px;}
.qitem.waiting .qnum{background:var(--ink-dim);}
.qqueue{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;margin-top:8px;}
.qmini{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-dim);
  background:rgba(255,255,255,.55);border-radius:9px;padding:3px 9px 3px 4px;}
.qmini .qnum{background:var(--ink-dim);font-size:9.5px;padding:1px 6px;margin-right:5px;}
.qnow{font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace;font-size:10px;color:var(--red-dark);margin-left:6px;
  white-space:nowrap;}
.qrecap{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;margin-top:8px;}
.qtick{font-size:10.5px;background:#e2f0e6;color:#1f6b3a;border-radius:9px;padding:2px 8px;}

.field-invalid input,.field-invalid textarea{border-color:var(--red)!important;background:#fff6f6;}
.val-banner{background:#fbe9e7;border:1px solid #f0b3ad;border-left:4px solid var(--red);border-radius:8px;
  padding:10px 12px;margin-bottom:12px;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.5;color:#8a2a22;display:none;}
.val-banner.show{display:block;}
.val-banner b{color:var(--red-dark);}
.req-star{color:var(--red);font-weight:700;}

.other-input{display:none;margin-top:6px;}
.other-input.show{display:block;}
.type-note{display:none;margin-top:6px;font-size:11.5px;line-height:1.45;color:#8a6414;background:#fff8e6;
  border:1px solid #f0d9a0;border-radius:6px;padding:7px 9px;}
.type-note.show{display:block;}
@media print{
  body{background:#fff;}
  .appbar,.navbar,.action-bar,#page-build,#page-register,#page-admin,#page-ai{display:none!important;}
  .wrap{padding:0;max-width:100%;}
  #report{display:block!important;}
  .report-banner,.report-body{border-radius:0;}
  .hazard-card{break-inside:avoid;}
  .hz-check input[type=checkbox]{display:none!important;}
  .verify-bar{display:none!important;}
  .hazard-card.excluded{background:#f1f3f6!important;border:1px dashed #aab5c0!important;}
  .hazard-card.excluded .excl-tag{display:inline-block!important;}
}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="appbar">
  <div class="appbar-inner">
    <h1>Risk Assessment Generator</h1>
    <p class="sub">OKPELLA Cement Limited — field edition</p>
  </div>
  <div class="navbar" id="navbar">
    <button class="nav-btn active" data-page="build">Build</button>
    <button class="nav-btn" data-page="ai">AI Assist</button>
    <button class="nav-btn" data-page="register">Register</button>
    <button class="nav-btn" data-page="admin">Admin</button>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="wrap">

  <!-- ============ BUILD PAGE ============ -->
  <div id="page-build">

    <!-- STEP A: start from a preset -->
    <div class="card" id="cardPresets">
      <h2>Start from a common task</h2>
      <p class="hint">Tap a job you're about to do. It fills in the activities and equipment for you — you can adjust everything afterwards.</p>
      <div class="tag-search" style="margin-bottom:10px;"><input type="text" id="presetSearch" placeholder="Search tasks…"></div>
      <div class="preset-grid" id="presetGrid"></div>
      <div class="or-divider">or build it yourself</div>
      <button class="btn btn-ghost" id="skipPresets">Start from scratch</button>
    </div>

    <!-- STEP B: refine -->
    <div id="refineArea" style="display:none;">
      <div class="card">
        <h2><span class="num">1</span>Job details</h2>
        <div class="val-banner" id="valBanner"></div>
        <div class="field">
          <label class="field-label">Job Type <span class="req-star">*</span></label>
          <input type="text" id="jobType" placeholder="e.g. Kiln refractory brick lining">
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label class="field-label">Description <span class="req-star">*</span></label>
          <textarea id="jobDesc" placeholder="e.g. Replacing refractory brick lining inside the rotary kiln during shutdown"></textarea>
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label class="field-label">Department / Section <span class="req-star">*</span></label>
          <input type="text" id="deptName" placeholder="optional — e.g. Production, Mechanical Maintenance">
        </div>
        <div class="two-col">
          <div class="field"><label class="field-label">Assessed By <span class="req-star">*</span></label><input type="text" id="assessorName"></div>
          <div class="field"><label class="field-label">Approved By <span class="req-star">*</span></label><input type="text" id="approverName"></div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card">
        <h2><span class="num">2</span>Location &amp; Equipment</h2>
        <p class="hint">Choose the area first — the equipment lists narrow to what is found there.</p>
        <div class="val-banner" id="valBanner2"></div>
        <div class="field">
          <label class="field-label">Location of work <span class="req-star">*</span></label>
          <select id="locArea" onchange="onLocChange()"></select>
          <input type="text" id="locOther" class="other-input" placeholder="Specify the area">
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label class="field-label">Equipment Type <span class="req-star">*</span></label>
          <select id="equipType" onchange="onTypeChange()"></select>
          <input type="text" id="equipTypeOther" class="other-input" placeholder="Describe the equipment type">
          <div class="type-note" id="typeNote">An unlisted equipment type has no equipment-specific hazards in the register, so only activity hazards will be generated. Ask an administrator to add this type if it is used regularly.</div>
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label class="field-label">Specific Equipment <span class="req-star">*</span></label>
          <select id="equipRef" onchange="onEquipChange()"></select>
          <input type="text" id="equipRefOther" class="other-input" placeholder="Name the equipment (e.g. tag number)">
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label class="field-label">Specific work area <span class="req-star">*</span></label>
          <input type="text" id="workArea" placeholder="e.g. kiln burner platform, level 2">
        </div>
        <div class="two-col">
          <div class="field"><label class="field-label">Motion</label>
            <select id="equipMotion"><option>Running</option><option>Static</option></select></div>
          <div class="field"><label class="field-label">Status</label>
            <select id="equipStatus"><option>Operational</option><option>Shutdown/Isolated</option></select></div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card">
        <h2><span class="num">3</span>Activities <span class="ai-mode-badge" id="tagCount">0</span></h2>
        <div class="sel-summary">
          <div class="sl">Selected activities</div>
          <div class="chips" id="selectedChips"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="tag-search" style="margin-bottom:6px;"><input type="text" id="tagSearch" placeholder="Search all activities…"></div>
        <div id="tagAccordion"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div id="report"></div>

  <!-- ============ AI PAGE ============ -->
  <div id="page-ai" style="display:none;">
    <div class="card">
      <h2>AI Assistant <span class="ai-mode-badge" id="aiModeBadge">BASIC</span></h2>
      <p class="hint">Describe the work in your own words. This only <b>selects</b> from the existing activity and equipment lists — it never invents hazards.</p>
      <div class="chat-scroll" id="chatScroll"></div>
      <div class="chat-input-row">
        <textarea id="chatInput" placeholder="e.g. We're entering the kiln to replace brick lining during the shutdown"></textarea>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" id="chatSend">Go</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ============ REGISTER PAGE ============ -->
  <div id="page-register" style="display:none;">
    <div class="card">
      <h2>Risk Register</h2>
      <p class="hint">Browse the hazard libraries and master lists loaded in this app.</p>
      <div class="reg-tabs" id="regTabs">
        <button class="reg-tab active" data-tab="activityHazards">Activity Hazards</button>
        <button class="reg-tab" data-tab="equipmentHazards">Equipment Hazards</button>
        <button class="reg-tab" data-tab="jobTags">Activities</button>
        <button class="reg-tab" data-tab="equipmentTypes">Equip Types</button>
        <button class="reg-tab" data-tab="locations">Locations</button>
        <button class="reg-tab" data-tab="equipment">Equipment</button>
        <button class="reg-tab" data-tab="presets">Presets</button>
        <button class="reg-tab" data-tab="toolRecognition">Tools</button>
      </div>
      <div class="tag-search"><input type="text" id="regSearch" placeholder="Search…"></div>
      <div class="reg-count" id="regCount"></div>
      <div id="regContent"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ============ ADMIN PAGE ============ -->
  <div id="page-admin" style="display:none;">
    <div class="card">
      <div class="gate-box" id="gateBox">
        <div class="lock">🔒</div>
        <h2 style="justify-content:center;">Admin Access</h2>
        <p class="hint" style="text-align:center;">Enter the admin password to manage app data.</p>
        <input type="password" id="gatePassword" placeholder="Password">
        <div><button class="btn btn-primary" id="gateSubmit" style="max-width:220px;">Unlock</button></div>
        <div class="gate-error" id="gateError">Incorrect password.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="admin-section" id="adminSection">
        <h2>Manage Data</h2>
        <p class="hint">Download the master workbook to edit offline, or upload an updated .xlsx with sheets named <b>JobTags</b>, <b>EquipmentTypes</b>, <b>Equipment</b>, <b>ActivityHazards</b>, <b>EquipmentHazards</b>, <b>TaskPresets</b>, <b>Locations</b> and <b>ToolRecognition</b>.</p>
        <div class="upload-drop">📄 Choose an updated workbook
          <input type="file" id="fileInput" accept=".xlsx,.xls" style="display:block;margin:10px auto 0;font-size:12px;"></div>
        <div class="status-msg" id="uploadStatus"></div>
        <div class="data-summary" id="currentDataSummary"></div>
        <button class="btn btn-secondary" id="downloadBtn" style="margin-top:12px;">Download Master Workbook</button>
        <p class="ai-disclosure">This export carries the current data in the correct column layout and re-uploads cleanly, but it cannot carry Excel dropdown validation. For a fully controlled editing template, use the issued master workbook.</p>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost" id="resetDataBtn" style="margin-top:10px;">Reset to built-in data</button>

        <h2 style="margin-top:22px;">AI Assistant Settings</h2>
        <p class="hint">Optional. With an API key the AI Assist tab uses a language model to interpret task descriptions. Without one it falls back to the built-in offline matcher.</p>
        <label class="field-label">LLM API Key</label>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;">
          <input type="password" id="aiApiKey" placeholder="sk-ant-…" style="flex:1;">
          <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="aiKeySave">Save</button>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="aiKeyClear" style="margin-top:8px;">Clear key</button>
        <p class="ai-disclosure">Stored only in this browser and sent directly to your chosen provider’s API — never to anyone else. Normal provider charges apply to your own account. Anyone with access to this device can read the key from browser storage, so use a key with a spending cap.</p>

        <div class="admin-sub">Admin Password</div>
        <p class="hint">Changes the password on this device only. The file itself still carries the original, so a fresh copy uses the built-in password again.</p>
        <div class="field"><label class="field-label">New password</label><input type="password" id="pwNew" placeholder="at least 8 characters"></div>
        <div class="field"><label class="field-label">Confirm new password</label><input type="password" id="pwNew2"></div>
        <div class="btn-row">
          <button class="btn btn-secondary" id="pwChangeBtn">Change password</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost" id="pwResetBtn">Revert to built-in</button>
        </div>
        <div class="status-msg" id="pwStatus"></div>

        <div class="admin-sub">Backup &amp; Restore (JSON)</div>
        <p class="hint">Export the full app configuration — hazard libraries, activities, equipment, presets and preferences — as a single JSON file. Use it to back up, or to copy an identical setup onto another device.</p>
        <div class="btn-row">
          <button class="btn btn-secondary" id="jsonExportBtn">Export JSON</button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary" id="jsonImportBtn">Import JSON</button>
        </div>
        <input type="file" id="jsonFileInput" accept=".json" style="display:none;">
        <div class="status-msg" id="jsonStatus"></div>
        <p class="ai-disclosure">The export deliberately excludes your API key, so the file can be shared safely. Re-enter the key on each device.</p>

        <div class="admin-sub">Assessment Log</div>
        <p class="hint">Every generated assessment is recorded here with its serial number for audit and usage tracking. Stored on this device only.</p>
        <div class="data-summary" id="logSummary"></div>
        <div class="btn-row">
          <button class="btn btn-secondary" id="logCsvBtn">Export CSV</button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary" id="logJsonBtn">Export JSON</button>
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost" id="logClearBtn" style="margin-top:8px;">Clear log</button>
        <div class="status-msg" id="logStatus"></div>
        <div id="logList" style="margin-top:12px;"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="action-bar" id="actionBar"></div>

Conveying"]}],"activityHazards":[{"tag":"Working at Height","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Fall from height to lower level causing serious injury or fatality","lv":"E","ct":"Guardrails or edge protection in place; full body harness with double lanyard and 100% tie-off to certified anchor; permit to work at height; pre-use inspection of all fall arrest equipment","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Double lanyard, Hard hat with chin strap, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working at Height","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Dropped tools or materials striking persons below","lv":"H","ct":"Barricade and sign the drop zone below; tool lanyards on all hand tools; toe boards on platforms; no loose items in pockets at height","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Tool lanyards","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working at Height","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Rescue delay after a fall leaving worker in suspension trauma","lv":"E","ct":"Documented rescue plan before work starts; rescue equipment staged at the work face; never work at height alone; suspension trauma straps fitted to harness","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Full body harness, Suspension trauma relief straps","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working at Height","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Adverse weather (wind, rain, lightning) destabilising work at height","lv":"H","ct":"Stop work at height in high wind or electrical storm; check forecast before permit issue; anti-slip surfaces on wet platforms","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Slip-resistant safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Climbing","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Loss of grip or footing while climbing causing fall","lv":"H","ct":"Maintain three points of contact at all times; no carrying items by hand while climbing (use hoist line or tool bag); inspect rungs/steps before ascent","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Grip gloves, Safety boots, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Climbing","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Fatigue or overexertion during prolonged vertical ascent","lv":"M","ct":"Use rest platforms on tall structures; rotate personnel on repeat climbs; heat stress monitoring where ambient temperature is high","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Drinking water","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Ladder Use","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Ladder slipping, sliding, or toppling during use","lv":"H","ct":"Secure ladder at top and bottom; 4:1 angle rule; level firm footing; second person to foot the ladder where tying off is not possible","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety boots, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Ladder Use","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Overreaching from ladder causing loss of balance","lv":"H","ct":"Keep belt buckle within the stiles; reposition the ladder rather than stretching; no two-handed work from a ladder above 3 m","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Scaffold Use","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Scaffold collapse from incorrect erection or overloading","lv":"E","ct":"Scaffold erected and tagged by competent scaffolder only; green tag verified before each use; posted load limits observed; never modify a scaffold without re-inspection","rd":"M","rp":"Scaffold Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Full body harness","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Scaffold Use","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Fall through incomplete or unboarded scaffold platform","lv":"H","ct":"Full boarding and guardrails before handover; red tag any incomplete scaffold; harness tie-off when working on partially boarded lifts","rd":"M","rp":"Scaffold Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Rope Access","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Rope failure, abrasion, or incorrect rigging during descent","lv":"E","ct":"IRATA/SPRAT certified technicians only; twin-rope system with independent backup; edge protection at all rub points; pre-use rope inspection logged","rd":"M","rp":"Rope Access Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Hard hat with chin strap, Descent / backup device","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working on Elevated Platform","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Platform structural failure or guardrail defect","lv":"H","ct":"Inspect platform and guardrails before access; report and tag out any defect; observe posted load rating","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working Near Unprotected Edge","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Fall over unguarded edge or through floor opening","lv":"E","ct":"Install temporary guardrail or cover all openings; harness tie-off where edge protection is not feasible; barricade and sign the area","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Man-lift/Aerial Platform Use","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Aerial platform tip-over or uncontrolled movement","lv":"E","ct":"Trained and authorised operators only; ground conditions assessed and outriggers deployed; harness clipped to basket anchor; never exceed basket load or reach limits","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Ascending Stairs/Ladders","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Slip or trip on stairs or fixed ladder","lv":"M","ct":"Three points of contact; keep stairways clear of tools and material; adequate lighting on all access routes; handrail use mandatory","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Descending Stairs/Ladders","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Missed footing when descending, often while carrying items","lv":"M","ct":"Face the ladder when descending; carry nothing by hand; use hoist line for tools and parts","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety boots, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Confined Space Entry","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Oxygen deficiency or enrichment inside the space","lv":"E","ct":"Confined space entry permit mandatory; pre-entry and continuous atmospheric testing (O2 19.5-23.5%); forced ventilation maintained throughout; entry stopped immediately on alarm","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer / Confined Space Attendant","pp":"Multi-gas detector, Full body harness, Retrieval line","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Confined Space Entry","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Carbon monoxide accumulation from combustion gas paths (kiln, preheater, cooler)","lv":"E","ct":"Continuous CO monitoring with personal alarms; exit immediately above 50 ppm and re-ventilate; isolate space from all combustion gas connections before entry","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Multi-gas detector, SCBA, Airline / air-fed respirator","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Confined Space Entry","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Carbon dioxide pooling in pits, tunnels, and lower vessel levels (heavier than air)","lv":"E","ct":"Test atmosphere at multiple depths, not just at the access point; ventilate from the lowest level; exit above 1.5% CO2","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Multi-gas detector, Full body harness","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Confined Space Entry","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Engulfment or burial by stored material, bridged material, or sudden collapse of buildup","lv":"E","ct":"Verify vessel is empty and material flow isolated; never enter beneath a bridge or rathole; break buildup remotely from above before entry; harness and retrieval line attached at all times","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer / Confined Space Attendant","pp":"Full body harness, Retrieval line, Tripod / davit","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Confined Space Entry","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Would-be rescuer becoming a second casualty (approx 1.5 additional deaths per confined space fatality)","lv":"E","ct":"Trained standby attendant stationed at entry at all times with no other duties; documented on-site rescue plan and equipment before entry; strict no-unplanned-entry rule enforced; entry log maintained","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer / Confined Space Attendant","pp":"Rescue harness, Retrieval winch and davit, SCBA","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Confined Space Entry","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Residual heat causing heat stress or burns inside the vessel","lv":"E","ct":"Verify internal temperature below 40 degrees C before entry; forced cooling and ventilation; work/rest rotation with hydration; continuous attendant observation for heat stress signs","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Heat-resistant coveralls, Cooling vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Confined Space Entry","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Unexpected energisation or material flow into the occupied space","lv":"E","ct":"Full LOTO on all drives, feeders, and inlets; blank or blind all connected pipework; independent verification of isolation by a second competent person before entry","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Lock and tag kit","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Confined Space Entry","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Flammable or explosive atmosphere from fuel residue or combustible dust","lv":"E","ct":"LEL testing before and during entry; no entry above 10% LEL; no ignition sources permitted; intrinsically safe equipment and lighting only; bonding and earthing applied","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Anti-static coveralls, Intrinsically safe torch, Multi-gas detector","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Crawling in Restricted Space","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Entrapment or inability to self-extract from a restricted opening","lv":"H","ct":"Assess opening size and egress route before entry; attendant maintains continuous voice or visual contact; no entry alone; retrieval line where geometry permits","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer / Confined Space Attendant","pp":"Full body harness, Knee pads, Helmet with lamp","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working in Trench/Excavation","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Trench wall collapse burying personnel","lv":"E","ct":"Shoring, benching, or sloping per soil assessment for any excavation over 1.2 m; spoil kept at least 1 m from edge; daily inspection by competent person; safe ladder access every 7.5 m","rd":"M","rp":"Excavation Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working in Trench/Excavation","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Striking buried services (power, water, gas) during excavation","lv":"H","ct":"Service drawings reviewed and cable/pipe locator survey before breaking ground; hand-dig trial pits to confirm; permit to excavate issued","rd":"M","rp":"Excavation Supervisor","pp":"Insulated tools, Hard hat, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working Below Grade","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Accumulation of heavier-than-air gases in below-grade areas","lv":"H","ct":"Gas test before and during entry to pits and tunnels; mechanical ventilation; attendant at grade level","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Multi-gas detector","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Hot Work (General)","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Ignition of combustible dust, coal residue, oil, or packaging causing fire or explosion","lv":"E","ct":"Hot work permit with gas test; clear or wet down combustibles within 11 m; fire blankets over openings; trained fire watch during work and for 60 minutes after; extinguisher at the work face","rd":"M","rp":"Fire Watch / Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Flame-resistant (FR) coveralls, Safety gloves (general), Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Hot Work (General)","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Burns from hot slag, sparks, or heated workpiece","lv":"H","ct":"Full FR clothing with no exposed skin; leather gauntlets and spats; allow parts to cool or handle with tongs; screens to contain spatter","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Flame-resistant (FR) coveralls, Leather welding gloves, Welding spats, Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Welding","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Arc eye (photokeratitis) and skin burns from UV radiation","lv":"H","ct":"Correct shade welding helmet for the process and amperage; welding screens to protect passers-by; full skin coverage for welder and helper","rd":"L","rp":"Welding Supervisor","pp":"Welding helmet (correct shade), Flame-resistant (FR) coveralls, Leather welding gloves","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Welding","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Inhalation of welding fume including manganese and hexavalent chromium from stainless","lv":"H","ct":"Local exhaust ventilation at the arc; respiratory protection appropriate to consumable and base metal; never weld in unventilated confined space without airline supply","rd":"M","rp":"Welding Supervisor","pp":"Welding fume respirator, Welding helmet (correct shade)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Welding","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Electric shock from welding return path or damaged cables","lv":"H","ct":"Inspect cables, holders, and return clamp before use; return clamp connected directly to the workpiece; dry insulating mat when welding in damp conditions; isolate before changing electrodes","rd":"M","rp":"Welding Supervisor","pp":"Leather welding gloves, Insulated boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Cutting (Oxy-Acetylene)","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Flashback or cylinder fire from oxy-fuel equipment","lv":"E","ct":"Flashback arrestors fitted on both regulator and torch ends; leak test with soap solution before use; cylinders upright, secured, valves closed when unattended; no oil or grease near oxygen fittings","rd":"M","rp":"Welding Supervisor","pp":"Flame-resistant (FR) coveralls, Leather welding gloves, Chemical goggles","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Cutting (Oxy-Acetylene)","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Falling cut section striking personnel","lv":"H","ct":"Plan and support the cut so the piece falls in a controlled direction; rig and tag lines on heavy sections; exclusion zone beneath cutting at height","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Grinding","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Abrasive wheel burst causing high-energy fragment injury","lv":"H","ct":"Inspect wheel for cracks and check rated RPM exceeds machine speed before mounting; guard fitted at all times; allow new wheel to run free for one minute before use; correct wheel for the material","rd":"M","rp":"Workshop Supervisor","pp":"Face shield, Safety glasses, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Grinding","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Eye injury from flying sparks, swarf, and abrasive particles","lv":"H","ct":"Face shield worn over safety glasses; screens to protect adjacent workers; never grind toward walkways","rd":"L","rp":"Workshop Supervisor","pp":"Face shield, Safety glasses","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Grinding","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Respirable crystalline silica and metal dust from grinding operations","lv":"H","ct":"On-tool dust extraction or water suppression; RPE where extraction is not practicable; rotate personnel to limit exposure time","rd":"M","rp":"Workshop Supervisor","pp":"P3 respirator, Safety glasses","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Brazing","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Fume exposure from flux and filler metals including cadmium","lv":"H","ct":"Use cadmium-free filler where possible; local exhaust ventilation; never braze in a confined space without forced ventilation","rd":"M","rp":"Workshop Supervisor","pp":"Welding fume respirator, Chemical goggles, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Soldering","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Burns and lead/flux fume exposure","lv":"M","ct":"Soldering iron in a stand when not in use; bench extraction for flux fume; hand washing before eating","rd":"L","rp":"Workshop Technician","pp":"Safety glasses, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Drilling","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Drill bit snatch or workpiece rotation causing hand and wrist injury","lv":"M","ct":"Clamp or vice the workpiece - never hold by hand; no gloves on rotating spindle machines; correct speed and feed for the material","rd":"L","rp":"Workshop Supervisor","pp":"Safety glasses, No gloves (rotating machinery)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Sawing","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Contact with saw blade or kickback of the workpiece","lv":"H","ct":"Blade guard in place; push stick and clamps used; stand out of the kickback line; never reach over a running blade","rd":"M","rp":"Workshop Supervisor","pp":"Safety glasses, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Chiseling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Flying chips and mushroomed chisel head fragments","lv":"M","ct":"Dress mushroomed heads before use; safety glasses mandatory; screens where others are nearby","rd":"L","rp":"Workshop Technician","pp":"Safety glasses, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Cold Cutting","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Sharp edges and stored stress release during cold cutting of pipe or plate","lv":"M","ct":"De-stress and support the section before the final cut; deburr all cut edges; cut-resistant gloves for handling","rd":"L","rp":"Maintenance Technician","pp":"Cut-resistant gloves, Safety glasses","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Lockout-Tagout Application","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Unexpected start-up or energisation during maintenance causing crush or amputation","lv":"E","ct":"Apply LOTO to every energy source identified on the isolation list; each worker applies a personal lock to a multi-lock hasp; try-out test to confirm zero energy before work; independent verification by second competent person","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Lock and tag kit","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Lockout-Tagout Application","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Incomplete isolation - a secondary or hidden energy source missed","lv":"E","ct":"Use equipment-specific isolation list and single-line drawings rather than memory; walk down all connections; account for gravity, springs, hydraulic, pneumatic, thermal, and stored electrical energy","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Lock and tag kit, Voltage tester","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Lockout-Tagout Application","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Stored energy release after isolation (gravity, spring, hydraulic, pneumatic, capacitive)","lv":"E","ct":"Block or chock suspended and gravity-loaded parts; bleed hydraulic and pneumatic pressure and verify at gauge; discharge capacitors; release spring tension under control","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Face shield, Blocks / chocks","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Equipment Isolation","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Process material or gas entering the work area from connected plant","lv":"H","ct":"Blank, blind, or physically disconnect connected pipework and ducting; double block and bleed where required; post isolation tags at every point","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Chemical goggles","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Mechanical Maintenance/Repair","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Crush injury from heavy components shifting or falling during removal","lv":"H","ct":"Assess component weight and plan the lift; use certified lifting gear and purpose-built stands; never work under an unsupported load; exclusion zone during component removal","rd":"M","rp":"Mechanical Maintenance Lead","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Mechanical Maintenance/Repair","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Hand injury from pinch points, sharp edges, and hand tool slip","lv":"M","ct":"Cut-resistant gloves; deburr edges; correct tool for the task and inspected before use; keep hands out of the line of force","rd":"L","rp":"Maintenance Technician","pp":"Cut-resistant gloves, Safety glasses","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Mechanical Maintenance/Repair","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Musculoskeletal injury from awkward or sustained posture in confined plant areas","lv":"M","ct":"Plan access and use platforms or creepers; rotate personnel on sustained awkward tasks; provide kneeling pads","rd":"L","rp":"Mechanical Maintenance Lead","pp":"Knee pads, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Fine Motor/Assembly Task","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Repetitive strain from sustained fine assembly work","lv":"L","ct":"Rotate tasks and take micro-breaks; adjust bench height to suit; use torque tools rather than sustained manual force","rd":"N","rp":"Workshop Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Machine Guarding Removal/Replacement","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Operating equipment with guards removed or defeated","lv":"E","ct":"Guards removed only under LOTO; never operate with an interlock bypassed; guard refit verified and signed off before returning equipment to service; disciplinary policy on guard defeat enforced","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety glasses","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Hand Tool Use","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Injury from defective, worn, or incorrect hand tools","lv":"M","ct":"Pre-use inspection and removal of defective tools from service; correct tool for the task; insulated tools for electrical work; tool lanyards at height","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety glasses","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Power Tool Use","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Electric shock or entanglement from portable power tools","lv":"H","ct":"110 V or RCD/ELCB protected supply; inspect leads and casing before use; no loose clothing or gloves near rotating tools; guards in place","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety glasses, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Power Tool Use","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Hand-arm vibration syndrome from prolonged power tool use","lv":"H","ct":"Limit trigger time per shift against tool vibration magnitude; anti-vibration tools and gloves; rotate operators; health surveillance for regular users","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Anti-vibration gloves","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Hammering","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Struck-by flying fragments or misdirected blow injuring hand","lv":"M","ct":"Inspect hammer face and handle security; safety glasses mandatory; keep the holding hand clear or use tongs","rd":"L","rp":"Workshop Technician","pp":"Safety glasses, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Machining (Workshop Lathe/Mill)","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Entanglement in rotating chuck, workpiece, or swarf","lv":"E","ct":"No gloves, loose clothing, rings, or long hair near rotating machinery; chuck guard and interlock in place; chuck key removed before start; stop machine before clearing swarf","rd":"M","rp":"Workshop Supervisor","pp":"Safety glasses, No gloves (rotating machinery)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Machining (Workshop Lathe/Mill)","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Eye and skin injury from hot swarf and coolant mist","lv":"M","ct":"Machine guard and screens in place; swarf hook to clear chips (never by hand); coolant mist extraction; skin care regime for coolant contact","rd":"L","rp":"Workshop Supervisor","pp":"Safety glasses, Barrier cream","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Live Electrical Work","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Electrocution from contact with live conductors","lv":"E","ct":"Live work only under a specific written permit where isolation is genuinely not feasible; competent authorised person only; insulated tools rated for the voltage; insulated matting; second person trained in rescue present","rd":"H","rp":"Licensed Electrician / Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Arc-rated suit and hood, Insulated electrical gloves, Face shield, Insulated mat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Live Electrical Work","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Arc flash causing severe burns, blast injury, and hearing damage","lv":"E","ct":"Arc flash risk assessment and incident energy study; arc-rated PPE selected to the calculated cal/cm2; maintain arc flash boundary and restrict access; remote racking and switching where available","rd":"H","rp":"Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Arc-rated suit and hood, Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"De-energized Electrical Work","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Working on apparatus assumed dead but still energised","lv":"E","ct":"Prove-test-prove with an approved voltage tester on all phases; apply earths where required; lock and tag at the isolation point; permit to work issued by authorised person","rd":"M","rp":"Licensed Electrician","pp":"Insulated electrical gloves, Voltage tester, Arc-rated suit and hood","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"De-energized Electrical Work","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Stored charge in capacitors, VSDs, or long cable runs","lv":"E","ct":"Observe manufacturer discharge times; apply earthing rod to discharge before touching; verify zero volts after discharge","rd":"M","rp":"Licensed Electrician","pp":"Insulated electrical gloves, Earthing rod, Voltage tester","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Electrical Panel Access","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Arc flash or shock on opening a live panel door","lv":"E","ct":"Isolate before opening where practicable; where live access is unavoidable use arc-rated PPE and maintain approach boundaries; no unqualified persons inside the electrical room","rd":"H","rp":"Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Arc-rated suit and hood, Insulated electrical gloves, Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Cable Pulling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Musculoskeletal injury and hand laceration during cable pulling","lv":"M","ct":"Use cable rollers, winch, and lubricant rather than manual force; coordinate the pull with clear signals; gloves for handling armoured cable","rd":"L","rp":"Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Cut-resistant gloves, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Cable Pulling","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Pulling into an energised or incorrectly identified circuit","lv":"H","ct":"Positive cable identification and tagging at both ends; isolation and proving before any work on existing routes","rd":"M","rp":"Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Voltage tester, Insulated electrical gloves","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Rigging/Slinging Loads","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Load drop from sling, shackle, or lifting point failure","lv":"E","ct":"Certified and in-date lifting gear only; pre-use inspection and correct WLL for the sling angle; verified lifting points; documented lift plan for heavy or complex lifts","rd":"M","rp":"Rigging Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Rigging/Slinging Loads","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Personnel struck by swinging or rotating load","lv":"E","ct":"Never stand or pass under a suspended load; tag lines to control rotation; barricaded exclusion zone; trained banksman controlling the lift","rd":"M","rp":"Rigging Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Rigging/Slinging Loads","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Hand crush between load and structure during landing","lv":"H","ct":"Use tag lines and push sticks rather than hands; plan landing area and packing before the lift; hands clear on final set-down","rd":"M","rp":"Rigging Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Crane Operation","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Crane overload or stability failure causing collapse","lv":"E","ct":"Verify load weight and radius against the load chart; ground bearing assessed and outriggers fully deployed; LMI functional; no lifting in wind above manufacturer limit","rd":"M","rp":"Crane Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Crane Operation","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Crane or load contacting overhead power lines","lv":"E","ct":"Survey for overhead lines before positioning; maintain statutory exclusion distance; isolate lines or use goal posts and spotter where work is unavoidable nearby","rd":"M","rp":"Crane Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Signaling/Banksman Duties","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Miscommunication between banksman and operator leading to uncontrolled movement","lv":"H","ct":"Single designated banksman with agreed standard signals; radio backup with clear call signs; stop the lift immediately if contact is lost","rd":"M","rp":"Rigging Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Two-way radio","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Use of Chain Block","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Chain block or anchor point failure under load","lv":"H","ct":"Inspect chain, hook, and latch before use; verify anchor point capacity; never exceed rated capacity or use cheater bars; do not side-load","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Use of Overhead Crane","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Load swing or collision with structure in confined bay","lv":"H","ct":"Slow travel speeds and controlled acceleration; clear travel path; audible warning during travel; no personnel in the travel corridor","rd":"M","rp":"Workshop Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Use of Forklift","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Forklift tip-over or load fall from forks","lv":"H","ct":"Trained and authorised operators only; load within capacity chart and stable on forks; mast tilted back and load low when travelling; seatbelt worn at all times","rd":"M","rp":"Warehouse Supervisor","pp":"Seatbelt, Hi-vis vest, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Use of Forklift","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Pedestrian struck by forklift in shared traffic areas","lv":"E","ct":"Segregated pedestrian walkways; horn at blind corners and intersections; speed limits enforced; eye contact protocol before crossing","rd":"M","rp":"Warehouse Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Tandem Lift","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Load transfer and overload between cranes during tandem lifting","lv":"E","ct":"Written tandem lift plan with engineered load sharing; single lift director in control; de-rated crane capacities applied; slow synchronised movements only","rd":"H","rp":"Lifting Engineer","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest, Two-way radio","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Operating Mobile Equipment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Pedestrian struck by mobile plant in blind spot","lv":"E","ct":"Segregated pedestrian routes and exclusion zones; reversing alarms and camera or proximity system functional; positive eye contact or radio confirmation before approach","rd":"M","rp":"Plant/Fleet Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Hard hat, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Operating Mobile Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Machine rollover on slope, soft edge, or uneven ground","lv":"E","ct":"Ground and slope assessment before operation; do not exceed rated gradient; ROPS fitted and seatbelt worn at all times; maintain safe distance from edges and berms","rd":"H","rp":"Plant/Fleet Supervisor","pp":"Seatbelt, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Operating Mobile Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Pre-use defects (brakes, steering, lights, alarms) going undetected","lv":"H","ct":"Documented pre-start inspection every shift; defects reported and machine tagged out until repaired; planned maintenance regime","rd":"M","rp":"Plant/Fleet Supervisor","pp":"","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Vehicle Driving","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Collision with other vehicles or plant on site roads","lv":"H","ct":"Site traffic management plan with enforced speed limits and right of way; headlights and beacons on; no mobile phone use while driving","rd":"M","rp":"Plant/Fleet Supervisor","pp":"Seatbelt, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Vehicle Driving","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Driver fatigue on long or repetitive haul cycles","lv":"H","ct":"Shift length and rest break policy enforced; fatigue awareness training; rotate drivers on repetitive circuits; report fatigue without penalty","rd":"M","rp":"Plant/Fleet Supervisor","pp":"","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Reversing Vehicle","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Person struck during reversing manoeuvre","lv":"E","ct":"Avoid reversing by design (one-way or drive-through layouts); banksman with agreed signals where reversing is unavoidable; reversing alarm and camera functional","rd":"M","rp":"Plant/Fleet Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Excavation/Loading Operations","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Face or stockpile collapse onto plant or personnel","lv":"E","ct":"Geotechnical assessment of face height and angle; never undercut the face; no personnel on foot in the loading zone; maintain safe standoff from stockpile toes","rd":"H","rp":"Quarry Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Excavation/Loading Operations","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Struck by bucket or material falling from bucket during loading","lv":"H","ct":"Exclusion zone within the machine slew radius; never load over an occupied cab; operator confirms clear before each cycle","rd":"M","rp":"Quarry Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Traffic Control/Flagging","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Flagger struck by vehicle in live traffic area","lv":"H","ct":"Position flagger with a protected escape route and clear sight lines; hi-vis clothing and signs in advance; never stand in the direct travel path","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis clothing (reflective), Hard hat, Stop/go board","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Manual Handling of Loads","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Back and shoulder injury from lifting heavy or awkward loads","lv":"H","ct":"Mechanical aid (trolley, hoist, vacuum lifter) as first choice; team lift above 25 kg; assess load before lifting; correct technique training and refreshers","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Manual Handling of Loads","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Cumulative musculoskeletal disorder from repetitive high-frequency lifting (e.g. packing plant, 200-400 bags per shift)","lv":"E","ct":"Automate or mechanise bag handling where possible; enforce job rotation and rest breaks; limit individual daily lift count; ergonomic assessment and health surveillance for packing operators","rd":"M","rp":"Packing Plant Supervisor","pp":"Back support belt, Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Lifting","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Acute strain from sudden or unbalanced lift","lv":"M","ct":"Test the load before committing; keep load close to the body; no twisting while loaded; clear the route before lifting","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Carrying","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Trip or loss of balance while carrying obscuring load","lv":"M","ct":"Keep sight line clear over the load; use trolleys for bulky items; ensure route is clear and lit before starting","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety boots, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Pushing","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Strain from pushing loaded trolleys or bins on poor surfaces","lv":"M","ct":"Maintain floor surfaces and trolley wheels; push rather than pull; two-person handling for heavy or sloped routes","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Pulling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Shoulder and back strain from pulling loads","lv":"M","ct":"Push in preference to pulling; use mechanical aids; avoid pulling on slopes or over thresholds","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Repetitive Motion","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Upper limb disorder from sustained repetitive work","lv":"M","ct":"Job rotation and scheduled micro-breaks; ergonomic tool selection; report early symptoms for assessment","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Anti-vibration gloves","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Gripping Tools","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Hand fatigue and grip-related strain","lv":"L","ct":"Ergonomic tool handles; power tools in preference to sustained manual force; rotate tasks","rd":"N","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Grip gloves","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Team Lifting","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Uncoordinated team lift causing sudden load transfer","lv":"M","ct":"Nominate one person to call the lift; brief the team on route and set-down point; matched heights where practicable","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Shoveling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Back strain and blistering from sustained shovelling","lv":"M","ct":"Correct shovel size for material density; rotate personnel; bend knees and avoid twisting; mechanical clearing where volume is high","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Digging","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Strain and struck-by from manual digging near services or in restricted space","lv":"M","ct":"Hand-dig only where services may be present; rotate personnel; maintain safe spacing between diggers","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety boots, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Sweeping","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Airborne dust generation from dry sweeping","lv":"M","ct":"Never dry-sweep cement or clinker dust; use industrial vacuum or damp sweeping; RPE where dust cannot be suppressed","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Dust mask (P2), Safety glasses","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Scraping","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Hand injury and dust release when scraping hardened buildup","lv":"M","ct":"Use long-handled tools to keep hands clear; cut-resistant gloves; dust suppression or extraction","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Cut-resistant gloves, Dust mask (P2), Chemical goggles","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Standing","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Lower limb and back fatigue from prolonged standing","lv":"L","ct":"Anti-fatigue matting at fixed workstations; rotate tasks; supportive footwear","rd":"N","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Walking","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Slip, trip, or fall on plant walkways","lv":"M","ct":"Keep walkways clear and well lit; immediate spill clean-up; designated walking routes only; no mobile phone use while walking","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Kneeling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Knee injury from prolonged kneeling on hard or uneven surfaces","lv":"L","ct":"Knee pads or kneeling mats; rotate posture regularly","rd":"N","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Crouching","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Lower limb strain and loss of balance from sustained crouching","lv":"L","ct":"Rotate posture; use low stools where practicable","rd":"N","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Bending","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Back strain from repeated or sustained bending","lv":"M","ct":"Raise the work to waist height where possible; rotate tasks; avoid bend-and-twist combinations","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Stooping","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Cumulative back loading from stooped working posture","lv":"M","ct":"Provide access platforms or adjustable supports; limit duration; rotate personnel","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Squatting","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Knee and hip strain from prolonged squatting","lv":"L","ct":"Use a low seat or creeper; rotate posture frequently","rd":"N","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Crawling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Abrasion and knee injury while crawling in plant spaces","lv":"M","ct":"Knee and elbow protection; clear the route of sharp debris; lighting provided","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Reaching Overhead","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Shoulder strain and loss of balance when reaching overhead","lv":"M","ct":"Use a platform or step to bring work to chest height; limit sustained overhead work; rotate personnel","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Twisting","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Back injury from twisting under load","lv":"M","ct":"Move the feet rather than twisting the spine; reposition the work; avoid twist-and-lift","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Balancing on Uneven Surface","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Fall or ankle injury on uneven or sloping ground","lv":"M","ct":"Improve or level access routes; ankle-supporting boots; adequate lighting; take time on rough ground","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Sitting for Prolonged Period","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Musculoskeletal discomfort and reduced alertness from prolonged sitting","lv":"L","ct":"Adjustable seating with lumbar support; scheduled breaks and movement; vibration-damped seat in mobile plant","rd":"N","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Handling Hazardous Chemicals","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Skin or eye burns from corrosive substances","lv":"H","ct":"Review SDS before use; chemical-resistant gloves, goggles and apron; eyewash and safety shower within 10 seconds travel; decant with spill tray and funnel","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Chemical goggles, Chemical-resistant gloves, Chemical apron","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Handling Hazardous Chemicals","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Inhalation of vapours or mists causing respiratory harm","lv":"H","ct":"Use in ventilated area or fume hood; RPE selected against the specific substance; monitor exposure where limits apply","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"P3 respirator, Chemical goggles","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Handling Hazardous Chemicals","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Incompatible chemical mixing causing violent reaction or toxic gas","lv":"H","ct":"Segregate incompatible classes in storage per SDS; never decant into unlabelled or food containers; clear labelling maintained","rd":"M","rp":"Stores Supervisor","pp":"Chemical goggles, Safety gloves (general), Chemical apron","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Handling Corrosive Substances","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Severe chemical burn from acid or alkali splash","lv":"E","ct":"Closed transfer systems where possible; full face shield over goggles; chemical suit for bulk handling; emergency shower tested weekly","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Face shield, Chemical suit, Chemical-resistant gloves, Waterproof / rain boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Handling Flammable Materials","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Fire or explosion from flammable liquid vapour ignition","lv":"E","ct":"Eliminate ignition sources within the hazardous zone; bond and earth during transfer; store in approved flammables cabinet; extinguisher at the point of use","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Anti-static coveralls, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Handling Compressed Gas Cylinders","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Cylinder falling, valve shear, or uncontrolled release","lv":"H","ct":"Cylinders stored and used upright and chained; valve caps fitted during transport and storage; trolley for movement; never lift by the valve or cap","rd":"M","rp":"Stores Supervisor","pp":"Safety boots, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Handling Compressed Gas Cylinders","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Asphyxiant or oxidising gas accumulation in enclosed storage","lv":"H","ct":"Store cylinders in ventilated external cages; segregate oxygen from fuel gases by at least 3 m or a fire wall; gas detection in enclosed storage","rd":"M","rp":"Stores Supervisor","pp":"Multi-gas detector","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Spray Painting","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Solvent vapour inhalation and fire risk during spraying","lv":"H","ct":"Spray in ventilated booth or well-ventilated area; air-fed or appropriate organic vapour respirator; eliminate ignition sources; earth equipment","rd":"M","rp":"Workshop Supervisor","pp":"Airline / air-fed respirator, Coveralls, Safety gloves (general), Chemical goggles","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Waste Handling/Disposal","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Exposure to unknown or mixed waste streams and sharps","lv":"M","ct":"Segregate waste at source with clear labelling; never hand-sort mixed waste; puncture-resistant gloves; licensed disposal contractor","rd":"L","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Puncture-resistant gloves, Chemical goggles, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Fuel/Oil Transfer","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Static ignition during fuel transfer","lv":"E","ct":"Bond and earth tanker to receiving tank before transfer; no plastic containers for fuel; transfer stopped during electrical storms","rd":"M","rp":"Utilities Operator","pp":"Anti-static coveralls, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Fuel/Oil Transfer","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Spill to ground or drainage causing environmental harm and slip hazard","lv":"H","ct":"Bunded transfer area and drip trays; spill kit at point of transfer; high-level alarm and attended transfer; drain valves kept closed","rd":"M","rp":"Utilities Operator","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Dust-Generating Activity","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Respirable crystalline silica exposure leading to silicosis","lv":"E","ct":"Water suppression or on-tool extraction as primary control; RPE (P3 minimum) where dust remains; exposure monitoring and health surveillance for regularly exposed workers; restrict access to dusty zones","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"P3 respirator, Chemical goggles, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Dust-Generating Activity","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Cement dust causing dermatitis and hexavalent chromium sensitisation (cement eczema)","lv":"H","ct":"Prevent skin contact with wet and dry cement; barrier cream and washing facilities; long sleeves and gauntlets; change out of contaminated clothing promptly","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Chemical-resistant gloves, Coveralls, Barrier cream","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Dust-Generating Activity","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Eye injury and irritation from airborne cement and clinker dust","lv":"M","ct":"Sealed goggles rather than open safety glasses in dusty areas; eyewash stations accessible; never rub eyes with contaminated hands","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Sealed dust goggles","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working in Extreme Heat","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Heat stress, heat exhaustion, or heat stroke","lv":"H","ct":"Work/rest cycles matched to WBGT; cool rest area and electrolyte hydration; acclimatisation for new workers; buddy system to spot early symptoms; reschedule heavy work to cooler hours","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Cooling vest, Drinking water","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working in Extreme Heat","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Contact burns from hot surfaces and radiant heat","lv":"H","ct":"Lag or shield hot surfaces within reach; allow cool-down before maintenance; heat-resistant PPE; exclusion zones around hot discharge points","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Heat-resistant gauntlets, Face shield, Flame-resistant (FR) coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working in Extreme Cold","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Cold stress and reduced dexterity","lv":"M","ct":"Layered clothing and warm break area; limit exposure duration; avoid skin contact with cold metal","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Insulated electrical gloves, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working in Noisy Environment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Noise-induced hearing loss from sustained exposure above 85 dB(A)","lv":"H","ct":"Engineering controls (enclosure, damping) first; mandatory hearing protection in marked zones; limit exposure time; audiometric health surveillance","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working in Noisy Environment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Failure to hear alarms, vehicles, or verbal
