warnings due to noise and PPE","lv":"H","ct":"Visual alarms in high-noise areas; radio communication; level-dependent hearing protection where situational awareness is critical","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Level-dependent hearing protection, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working in Dusty Environment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Chronic respiratory disease from prolonged mixed dust exposure","lv":"H","ct":"Dust suppression and extraction at source; RPE with face-fit testing; job rotation out of high-dust zones; respiratory health surveillance","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"P3 respirator, Chemical goggles","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working Near Moving Machinery","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Entanglement in unguarded or inadequately guarded moving parts","lv":"E","ct":"Fixed and interlocked guards maintained; emergency stops tested and accessible; no loose clothing, jewellery, or long hair; never reach past a guard on running plant","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Close-fitting clothing, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working Alone/Isolated","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Delayed discovery of injury or incapacitation when working alone","lv":"H","ct":"Lone worker check-in schedule with a nominated contact; man-down alarm or personal locator where available; prohibit lone working for high-risk tasks","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Two-way radio","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Night Shift Work","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Reduced alertness and impaired hazard perception on night shift","lv":"H","ct":"Adequate task lighting; fatigue management and shift rotation policy; limit high-risk work at night where possible; buddy checks","rd":"M","rp":"Shift Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis clothing (reflective), Intrinsically safe torch","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working Outdoors in Weather","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Exposure to sun, rain, wind, and lightning","lv":"M","ct":"Stop outdoor and elevated work during electrical storms; sun protection and shade; wet weather gear; monitor forecast","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Weather-appropriate clothing, Sun protection","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Working Near Water","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Drowning after fall into water body or tank","lv":"H","ct":"Edge protection and barriers; life buoy and throw line at the water edge; buoyancy aid where fall risk remains; never work alone near open water","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Buoyancy aid, Full body harness","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Wet Work/Slippery Surface","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Slip on wet, oily, or slurry-covered surfaces","lv":"H","ct":"Immediate spill clean-up and drainage maintenance; anti-slip flooring and matting; slip-resistant footwear; hazard signage while wet","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Slip-resistant safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Drilling (Quarry Blast Holes)","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Respirable silica dust from drill cuttings","lv":"H","ct":"Wet drilling or dust collection system on the rig; operator in closed pressurised cab; RPE outside the cab","rd":"M","rp":"Drill Rig Operator","pp":"P3 respirator, Safety glasses","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Drilling (Quarry Blast Holes)","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Drilling near unstable face or over previously charged holes","lv":"E","ct":"Maintain geotechnical standoff from the crest; survey and mark all hole positions; never drill into a misfired or charged hole; pre-drill face inspection","rd":"H","rp":"Quarry Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Blasting Operations","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Premature or unintended detonation during charging","lv":"E","ct":"Licensed shotfirer control of all charging; no radio transmitters or mobile phones within the exclusion zone; electrical storm suspension rule; approved initiation systems only","rd":"H","rp":"Licensed Shotfirer","pp":"Anti-static coveralls, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Blasting Operations","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Flyrock injuring persons or damaging plant beyond the exclusion zone","lv":"E","ct":"Engineered blast design with adequate burden, spacing, and stemming; exclusion zone sized to design and physically guarded; audible warning and all-clear procedure; blast mats where required","rd":"H","rp":"Licensed Shotfirer","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Blasting Operations","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Misfire handling after an incomplete detonation","lv":"E","ct":"Defined misfire waiting period before re-approach; only the shotfirer approaches; never drill or dig into a suspected misfire; documented misfire procedure","rd":"H","rp":"Licensed Shotfirer","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Explosives Handling/Storage","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Accidental initiation during transport, storage, or handling","lv":"E","ct":"Segregate detonators from bulk explosives in separate magazines; approved containers and vehicles only; no impact, friction, or heat sources; strict stock reconciliation and access log","rd":"H","rp":"Licensed Shotfirer","pp":"Anti-static coveralls, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Explosives Handling/Storage","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Lightning strike or external fire reaching the magazine","lv":"E","ct":"Certified lightning protection on magazines; vegetation-free and combustible-free zone around magazine; suspend handling during electrical storms; fire response plan","rd":"H","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Sample Preparation (Laboratory)","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Silica dust exposure during crushing, grinding, and pressing of samples","lv":"H","ct":"Enclosed or extracted sample preparation equipment; clean with vacuum not compressed air; RPE where extraction is insufficient","rd":"M","rp":"Laboratory Technician","pp":"P3 respirator, Safety glasses, Lab coat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Sample Preparation (Laboratory)","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Burns from furnace, oven, and hot crucibles during loss-on-ignition testing","lv":"H","ct":"Long-handled tongs and heat-resistant gauntlets; designated cooling area with warning signage; never handle crucibles by hand until verified cool","rd":"M","rp":"Laboratory Technician","pp":"Heat-resistant gauntlets, Face shield, Lab coat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Chemical/Reagent Handling (Laboratory)","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Acid or reagent splash to eyes and skin","lv":"H","ct":"Work in fume hood for volatile or exothermic reactions; always add acid to water; eyewash and shower tested and unobstructed; SDS accessible for every reagent","rd":"M","rp":"Laboratory Technician","pp":"Chemical goggles, Nitrile gloves, Lab coat, Chemical apron","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Chemical/Reagent Handling (Laboratory)","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Fire from flammable solvents in the laboratory","lv":"H","ct":"Flammables stored in approved cabinet; minimum working quantities at the bench; no open flame near solvents; extinguisher and fire blanket accessible","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Lab coat, Chemical goggles","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Sampling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Exposure to hot, dusty, or chemically active process material during sampling","lv":"H","ct":"Use designated sample points and long-handled samplers; allow hot samples to cool in a controlled area; never sample from a running stream by hand","rd":"M","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Heat-resistant gauntlets, Face shield, Dust mask (P2)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Calibration","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Incorrect calibration leading to unsafe process control or false safety readings","lv":"H","ct":"Calibrate against traceable standards on schedule; bump-test gas detectors before every use; tag out-of-calibration instruments; record all calibration results","rd":"M","rp":"Instrumentation Technician","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Gas Testing/Atmospheric Monitoring","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"False sense of safety from faulty, uncalibrated, or wrongly positioned detector","lv":"E","ct":"Bump test before each use and calibrate on schedule; test at multiple heights within the space; continuous monitoring during occupancy rather than pre-entry only; alarm set points verified","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Multi-gas detector","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Equipment Testing","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Unexpected movement or energisation during functional testing","lv":"H","ct":"Area cleared and barricaded before test; controlled re-energisation under permit; all personnel accounted for and clear; test controls from a safe position","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Visual Inspection","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Proximity to operating plant while inspecting","lv":"H","ct":"Use designated inspection walkways and viewing ports; maintain exclusion distance from moving parts; never open guards on running equipment","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety glasses, Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Visual Inspection","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Distraction-related slip, trip, or fall while focused on the inspection task","lv":"M","ct":"Stop walking when observing; keep routes clear and lit; use handrails on stairs","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Housekeeping/Cleaning","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Slip, trip, and fall hazards from spillage and material accumulation","lv":"M","ct":"Clean as you go; designated storage for tools and materials; immediate spill response; scheduled housekeeping audits","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety boots, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Housekeeping/Cleaning","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Dust cloud generation during cleaning creating inhalation and explosion risk","lv":"H","ct":"Industrial vacuum or wet methods only - never dry sweeping or compressed air blow-down of combustible dust; RPE during cleaning; control ignition sources","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"P3 respirator, Chemical goggles, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Housekeeping/Cleaning","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Contact with moving plant while cleaning near running equipment","lv":"E","ct":"Isolate and lock out equipment before cleaning within reach of moving parts; never clear material from a running conveyor or mill; use designated stop points","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Pressure Washing","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"High-pressure water injection injury to skin","lv":"H","ct":"Never direct the lance at any person; trigger guard fitted and functional; treat all injection wounds as a medical emergency regardless of appearance","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Waterproof gloves, Face shield, Waterproof / rain boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Pressure Washing","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Slip on wet surfaces and electrical hazard from water near equipment","lv":"H","ct":"RCD protection on supply; isolate and cover electrical equipment before washing; manage run-off and mark wet areas","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Slip-resistant safety boots, Waterproof / rain boots, Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Refractory Installation","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Refractory dust and ceramic fibre exposure","lv":"H","ct":"Wet cutting or extraction for refractory shaping; RPE mandatory for ceramic fibre work; skin cover to prevent fibre irritation; dispose of waste as controlled","rd":"M","rp":"Refractory Supervisor","pp":"P3 respirator, Coveralls, Safety gloves (general), Chemical goggles","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Refractory Installation","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Brick fall or lining collapse during installation","lv":"E","ct":"Follow the lining procedure for ring closure and keying; support and prop as work progresses; never work beneath an unsupported partially completed ring; evacuate before any kiln inching","rd":"M","rp":"Refractory Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Formwork/Casting","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Formwork failure or blowout during casting","lv":"H","ct":"Formwork designed and checked for the pour rate and head; propping verified before pour; controlled pour rate; no personnel beneath formwork during pour","rd":"M","rp":"Civil Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Concrete Pouring","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Cement burns from prolonged wet concrete skin contact","lv":"H","ct":"Prevent skin contact - waterproof gloves and boots, long sleeves; wash exposed skin immediately; never kneel in wet concrete without waterproof protection","rd":"M","rp":"Civil Supervisor","pp":"Waterproof gloves, Waterproof / rain boots, Chemical goggles, Long sleeves","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Barricading/Signage Setup","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Inadequate barricading allowing unauthorised entry to a hazard zone","lv":"M","ct":"Hard barriers for high-consequence hazards rather than tape alone; signage stating hazard and contact; daily check that barricades remain intact","rd":"L","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Fire Watch Duty","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Fire developing undetected after hot work ceases","lv":"H","ct":"Dedicated fire watch with no other duties during hot work and for at least 60 minutes after; check concealed and adjacent spaces including levels below; extinguisher and alarm access confirmed","rd":"M","rp":"Fire Watch","pp":"Flame-resistant (FR) coveralls, Fire extinguisher, Two-way radio","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"First Aid Response Standby","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Responder exposure to the originating hazard or to bloodborne pathogens","lv":"M","ct":"Scene safety assessment before approach; never enter a confined space or gas-affected area without the correct PPE; universal precautions for body fluids","rd":"L","rp":"First Aider","pp":"Nitrile gloves, Resuscitation face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Hydraulic System Work","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"High-pressure hydraulic fluid injection injury","lv":"E","ct":"Depressurise and verify zero pressure at the gauge before breaking any connection; never search for leaks with the hand - use cardboard; treat any injection wound as a surgical emergency","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Technician","pp":"Face shield, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Hydraulic System Work","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Uncontrolled movement of hydraulically held components on pressure loss","lv":"H","ct":"Mechanically block or prop suspended and gravity-loaded components before working; do not rely on hydraulic hold alone","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Technician","pp":"Hard hat, Safety gloves (general), Blocks / chocks","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Pneumatic System Work","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Stored compressed air release causing whipping hose or projectile","lv":"H","ct":"Isolate and bleed to zero before disconnection; whip-check restraints on all air hose couplings; never use compressed air for cleaning clothing or skin","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Technician","pp":"Safety glasses, Safety gloves (general), Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"High Pressure Grouting/Pumping","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Hose burst or coupling failure under pumping pressure","lv":"H","ct":"Inspect hoses and couplings before each use and pressure test to specification; whip-check restraints fitted; no personnel in line with couplings during pumping; relief valve verified","rd":"M","rp":"Refractory Supervisor","pp":"Face shield, Safety gloves (general), Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Operating Stacker/Reclaimer","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Struck by slewing boom or travelling machine","lv":"H","ct":"Audible and visual warning before slew and travel; travel path and slew radius barricaded; no personnel on the stockpile during operation","rd":"M","rp":"Stockyard Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Operating Stacker/Reclaimer","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Stockpile collapse or engulfment when personnel are on or near the pile","lv":"E","ct":"Prohibit walking on active stockpiles; assess pile stability and avoid undercut faces; remote inspection where possible","rd":"H","rp":"Stockyard Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Bulk Tanker/Wagon Loading","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Fall from tanker top during loading or hatch access","lv":"E","ct":"Fixed access gantry with guardrails and fall arrest; never climb on tanker without edge protection; loading from ground level where system permits","rd":"M","rp":"Dispatch Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Bulk Tanker/Wagon Loading","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Pressurised release or dust blowback during bulk loading","lv":"H","ct":"Correct seal of loading spout before start; dust extraction and filter on loading head; depressurise before disconnection; bond and earth the tanker","rd":"M","rp":"Dispatch Operator","pp":"P3 respirator, Chemical goggles, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Bulk Tanker/Wagon Loading","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Vehicle movement in loading bay striking personnel","lv":"H","ct":"Wheel chocks and keys held during loading; traffic light or interlock system; marked pedestrian routes; driver remains in designated safe zone","rd":"M","rp":"Dispatch Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Lubrication/Greasing","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Entanglement while lubricating near moving parts","lv":"E","ct":"Use extended remote greasing lines to keep hands clear; lubricate only with equipment stopped and isolated unless a designed remote point exists; guards remain in place","rd":"M","rp":"Lubrication Technician","pp":"Close-fitting clothing, Safety gloves (general), Safety glasses","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Lubrication/Greasing","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Skin contact and slip hazard from oil and grease","lv":"M","ct":"Wipe up spills immediately; barrier cream and gloves; dispose of oily rags in a lidded metal bin","rd":"L","rp":"Lubrication Technician","pp":"Oil-resistant gloves, Slip-resistant safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Operating Fixed Machinery","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Entanglement or crushing at an unguarded point on fixed plant","lv":"E","ct":"Confirm all guards and interlocks are in place before start-up; emergency stops tested and within reach; never operate with a guard removed or bypassed; trained and authorised operators only","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Close-fitting clothing, Safety glasses, Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Operating Fixed Machinery","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Unsafe start-up with personnel still in the machine zone","lv":"E","ct":"Pre-start warning alarm and visual check that all personnel are clear; start-up permit after maintenance; lockout removed only by the person who applied it","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Operating Fixed Machinery","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Noise and vibration exposure during sustained operation","lv":"H","ct":"Acoustic enclosure and damping; hearing protection in marked zones; limit continuous exposure time","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Kiln Burner Operation / Adjustment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Flame blowback or hot gas ejection from the burner hood during adjustment","lv":"E","ct":"Never stand directly in front of an open burner hood or inspection door; adjust from the designated side position; verify draught is negative before opening any port; aluminised heat protection worn","rd":"H","rp":"Pyro Section Supervisor","pp":"Aluminised heat suit, Face shield, Heat-resistant gauntlets","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"tag":"Kiln Burner Operation / Adjustment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Radiant heat burns and eye damage from flame observation","lv":"H","ct":"Use shaded viewing glass or camera rather than direct observation; limit exposure time at the hood; heat shields at the burner platform","rd":"M","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Shaded face shield, Heat-resistant gauntlets","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Burner Pipe Removal / Insertion","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Burner pipe swinging or dropping during withdrawal on the trolley","lv":"E","ct":"Use the designed burner trolley and rails only; verify trolley locks and stops engaged; exclusion zone along the withdrawal path; never manually restrain a moving burner pipe","rd":"H","rp":"Pyro Section Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Heat-resistant gauntlets, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"tag":"Burner Pipe Removal / Insertion","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Severe burns from residual heat in the burner pipe and refractory nose","lv":"E","ct":"Allow full cool-down and verify temperature before handling; use heat-resistant handling equipment; never touch the nose ring with unprotected hands","rd":"M","rp":"Pyro Section Supervisor","pp":"Heat-resistant gauntlets, Aluminised heat suit","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Kiln Feed / Process Control","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Incorrect process adjustment causing upset, blockage, or hot meal surge downstream","lv":"H","ct":"Follow operating envelope and change one parameter at a time; communicate all changes to field crews before making them; alarm limits maintained and not overridden","rd":"M","rp":"Control Room Operator","pp":"","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"tag":"Kiln Feed / Process Control","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Fatigue and reduced vigilance during long control room shifts","lv":"M","ct":"Shift rotation and break schedule; alarm rationalisation to prevent alarm flooding; second operator for abnormal operations","rd":"L","rp":"Shift Supervisor","pp":"","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Clinker Cooler Operation","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Red river or hot clinker avalanche from unstable cooler bed","lv":"E","ct":"Maintain bed depth control and grate speed within design; monitor for red river indications; no personnel at the discharge or drag chain during unstable operation","rd":"H","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Aluminised heat suit","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"tag":"Clinker Cooler Operation","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Burns from hot clinker spillage at the cooler discharge","lv":"H","ct":"Barricade spillage zones; allow material to cool before clearing; never clear hot clinker by hand","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Heat-resistant gauntlets, Heat-resistant boots and gaiters, Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Mill Start-up / Shutdown","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Personnel still inside or near the mill at start-up","lv":"E","ct":"Full headcount and permit close-out before start; pre-start warning alarm and visual sweep of the mill house; start-up only by the authorised operator after LOTO removal by each holder","rd":"H","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Mill Start-up / Shutdown","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Noise surge and dust release at start-up","lv":"H","ct":"Hearing protection before start; stand clear of vents and inspection doors; dedusting running before mill start","rd":"M","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Ear plugs, Dust mask (P2)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Silo Aeration / Fluidisation","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Sudden material collapse or blowback when aeration is applied to a bridged silo","lv":"E","ct":"Never apply aeration with personnel inside or at an open access point; clear and barricade discharge area; apply aeration progressively, not as a single surge","rd":"H","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Dust mask (P2), Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Air Cannon / Blaster Operation","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Unexpected air cannon discharge causing blast injury or hearing damage","lv":"E","ct":"Isolate and bleed air cannon receivers before any work in the vicinity; lockout the firing solenoid; audible warning and exclusion zone before test firing","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Ear plugs, Lock and tag kit","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Process Sampling (Hot Material)","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Burns from hot meal, clinker, or dust during sampling","lv":"H","ct":"Use designated sample points with long-handled scoops; allow sample to cool in a steel tray in a marked area; never sample from a live stream by hand","rd":"M","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Heat-resistant gauntlets, Face shield, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Process Sampling (Hot Material)","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Hot gas or dust ejection when opening a sample port","lv":"H","ct":"Verify negative draught before opening; stand to the side of the port; open slowly and in stages","rd":"M","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Face shield, Heat-resistant gauntlets","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Bypass Dust Handling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Exposure to alkali-rich, chloride-rich bypass dust causing burns and respiratory harm","lv":"H","ct":"Enclosed handling and conditioning of bypass dust; RPE and full skin cover; wash facilities immediately available; never handle dry bypass dust in open air","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"P3 respirator, Sealed dust goggles, Chemical-resistant gloves, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Kiln Inching / Barring","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Personnel inside or on the kiln during inching","lv":"E","ct":"Full evacuation and headcount before any inching; single authorised controller with radio confirmation from spotters at both ends; audible warning before movement; inching control locked when not in use","rd":"H","rp":"Mechanical Supervisor","pp":"Two-way radio, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"tag":"Alternative Fuel (RDF/SRF) Handling","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Self-heating and spontaneous combustion in RDF/SRF storage piles","lv":"E","ct":"Limit pile height and storage residence time; temperature and CO monitoring in the storage hall; first-in first-out rotation; fire detection and suppression in the hall","rd":"H","rp":"Fuel Handling Supervisor","pp":"P3 respirator, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Alternative Fuel (RDF/SRF) Handling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Biological and chemical exposure from waste-derived fuel (bioaerosols, sharps, contaminants)","lv":"H","ct":"Enclosed handling and negative pressure in reception areas; no manual sorting of unshredded waste; vaccination and hygiene regime; puncture-resistant gloves","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"P3 respirator, Puncture-resistant gloves, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Alternative Fuel (RDF/SRF) Handling","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Blockage clearing in AF feeding lines exposing personnel to moving parts","lv":"H","ct":"Isolate and lock the feed system before clearing; use rodding points rather than opening the line; never clear a running feeder by hand","rd":"M","rp":"Fuel Handling Supervisor","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Dust mask (P2), Lock and tag kit","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Tyre-Derived Fuel Handling","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Crush or impact injury from whole tyre handling and the midkiln feed mechanism","lv":"H","ct":"Mechanical handling of whole tyres; exclusion zone at the drop and gate mechanism; never enter the feed chute area while the system is armed","rd":"M","rp":"Fuel Handling Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Tyre-Derived Fuel Handling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Steel wire laceration from shredded tyre-derived fuel","lv":"M","ct":"Cut-resistant gloves for all TDF handling; avoid manual contact with shredded material; magnetic separation maintained","rd":"L","rp":"Fuel Handling Supervisor","pp":"Cut-resistant gloves, Arm protection","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Biomass Fuel Handling","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Dust explosion risk from fine biomass dust in enclosed handling","lv":"E","ct":"ATEX zoning and equipment selection; explosion venting and suppression on silos and mills; dust control and housekeeping; eliminate ignition sources","rd":"H","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Anti-static coveralls, P3 respirator","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Biomass Fuel Handling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Mould and fungal spore exposure from stored biomass","lv":"H","ct":"Limit storage time and keep material dry; RPE for handling degraded biomass; ventilation in storage and reclaim areas","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"P3 respirator, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Liquid Waste Fuel Handling","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Flammable and toxic liquid waste fuel splash or vapour exposure","lv":"E","ct":"Closed transfer with bonded and earthed connections; SDS review for every batch; gas detection at the pumping station; spill containment and emergency shower","rd":"H","rp":"Fuel Handling Supervisor","pp":"Chemical suit, Face shield, Chemical-resistant gloves, Organic vapour respirator","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Coal / Petcoke Handling","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Spontaneous combustion in coal and petcoke stockpiles","lv":"H","ct":"Compact and limit pile height; temperature monitoring; first-in first-out rotation; no smoking or hot work in the coal yard","rd":"M","rp":"Fuel Handling Supervisor","pp":"Dust mask (P2), Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Coal / Petcoke Handling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Coal dust inhalation and explosible dust accumulation","lv":"E","ct":"Dust suppression and extraction at transfer points; strict housekeeping to prevent layered dust; no dry sweeping or compressed air blow-down; CO monitoring in enclosed areas","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"P3 respirator, Anti-static coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Fuel Shredder Operation","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Entanglement or amputation at shredder rotor and infeed","lv":"E","ct":"Never approach the infeed while the rotor is turning; full isolation with rotor lock before clearing a jam; interlocked guards on all access; two-person rule for jam clearing","rd":"H","rp":"Fuel Handling Supervisor","pp":"Cut-resistant gloves, Hard hat, Lock and tag kit","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Fuel Shredder Operation","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Ejected fragments or foreign objects from the shredder","lv":"H","ct":"Ferrous and non-ferrous separation upstream; deflector guards at the infeed; exclusion zone during operation","rd":"M","rp":"Fuel Handling Supervisor","pp":"Face shield, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Belt Splicing / Vulcanising","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Burns from vulcanising press platens and hot splice","lv":"H","ct":"Allow platens to cool before handling; heat-resistant gloves; barricade the press area during heating cycle","rd":"M","rp":"Belt Technician","pp":"Heat-resistant gauntlets, Safety glasses","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Belt Splicing / Vulcanising","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Stored belt tension release during splice preparation","lv":"E","ct":"Isolate and lock the drive; secure the take-up and clamp the belt on both sides of the cut before cutting; controlled tension release","rd":"M","rp":"Belt Technician","pp":"Hard hat, Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Belt Splicing / Vulcanising","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Solvent and adhesive vapour exposure in a confined conveyor gallery","lv":"H","ct":"Forced ventilation during cold splicing; solvent-appropriate respirator; eliminate ignition sources; minimum quantity at the work face","rd":"M","rp":"Belt Technician","pp":"Organic vapour respirator, Nitrile gloves","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Chute / Liner Replacement","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Crush injury from heavy liner plates during removal and fitting","lv":"E","ct":"Mechanical handling or purpose-made liner manipulator; never support a plate by hand alone; tag lines and props; work from outside the drop line","rd":"M","rp":"Mechanical Maintenance Lead","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Chute / Liner Replacement","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Confined and awkward access inside chutes and transfer points","lv":"H","ct":"Confined space permit where applicable; gas test and ventilate; attendant at the access; limit duration and rotate personnel","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer / Confined Space Attendant","pp":"Full body harness, Multi-gas detector, Knee pads, Helmet with lamp","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Gunning / Shotcreting Refractory","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"High-pressure nozzle whip or rebound injury","lv":"H","ct":"Two-person nozzle control with deadman switch; whip-checks on all hose couplings; never point the nozzle toward any person; controlled pressure ramp-up","rd":"M","rp":"Refractory Supervisor","pp":"Face shield, Hard hat, Safety gloves (general), Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Gunning / Shotcreting Refractory","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Alkaline rebound material causing skin and eye burns","lv":"H","ct":"Full skin cover and sealed goggles; barrier cream; immediate washing of contact; eyewash at the work face","rd":"M","rp":"Refractory Supervisor","pp":"Sealed dust goggles, Chemical-resistant gloves, Coveralls, Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Hydro-Jetting / High Pressure Cleaning","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"High-pressure water injection injury penetrating skin","lv":"E","ct":"Never aim the lance at any person; deadman trigger verified; exclusion zone with hard barriers; treat any injection wound as a surgical emergency regardless of appearance","rd":"H","rp":"Cleaning Supervisor","pp":"Jetting suit, Face shield, Metatarsal boots, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Hydro-Jetting / High Pressure Cleaning","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Hose or lance failure under pressure","lv":"H","ct":"Pressure test hoses and lances to specification before use; whip-check restraints; bleed pressure before any adjustment","rd":"M","rp":"Cleaning Supervisor","pp":"Face shield, Jetting suit","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Bolt Torqueing / Tensioning","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Hydraulic torque wrench reaction arm crushing fingers or hand","lv":"H","ct":"Keep hands clear of the reaction arm and pinch point; verify reaction point is solid before applying pressure; never hold the tool by the reaction arm","rd":"M","rp":"Mechanical Maintenance Lead","pp":"Safety gloves (general), Safety glasses","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Bolt Torqueing / Tensioning","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Bolt or stud failure ejecting under tension","lv":"H","ct":"Inspect studs for damage before tensioning; do not exceed specified torque; stand clear of the bolt axis during tensioning","rd":"M","rp":"Mechanical Maintenance Lead","pp":"Face shield, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Alignment / Laser Survey","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Laser eye exposure during alignment work","lv":"M","ct":"Use Class 2 or lower alignment lasers where possible; never look into the beam; barricade the beam path and warn adjacent workers","rd":"L","rp":"Reliability Technician","pp":"Laser safety eyewear","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Vibration Analysis","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Proximity to running rotating equipment while taking readings","lv":"H","ct":"Use fixed measurement points and extension probes; never place hands near couplings or shafts; close-fitting clothing and no loose items","rd":"M","rp":"Reliability Technician","pp":"Ear plugs, Close-fitting clothing","mo":"Running","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Thermographic Survey","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Arc flash exposure when opening electrical panels for thermography","lv":"E","ct":"Use IR windows where fitted rather than opening live panels; arc-rated PPE if live panel access is unavoidable; qualified electrical escort","rd":"H","rp":"Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Arc-rated suit and hood, Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Non-Destructive Testing (NDT)","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Chemical exposure from penetrant, developer, and couplant","lv":"M","ct":"Ventilated work area; SDS reviewed; nitrile gloves and eye protection; no smoking near aerosol propellants","rd":"L","rp":"NDT Technician","pp":"Nitrile gloves, Chemical goggles","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Radiographic Testing","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Ionising radiation exposure to personnel near the exposure area","lv":"E","ct":"Radiography under permit by licensed personnel only; cordon and signage at the calculated boundary; dose rate survey before and after each shot; source accountability log; work scheduled out of hours where practicable","rd":"H","rp":"Radiation Protection Supervisor","pp":"Dosimeter badge, Radiation survey meter, Warning signage","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Nucleonic Gauge Work (Radioactive Source)","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Radiation exposure from an unshielded or stuck-open nucleonic source during maintenance","lv":"E","ct":"Confirm shutter is closed and locked, and verify with a survey meter before any work near the gauge; only licensed personnel may handle source housings; source register and leak testing maintained; never dismantle a source holder on site","rd":"H","rp":"Radiation Protection Supervisor","pp":"Dosimeter badge, Radiation survey meter","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Nucleonic Gauge Work (Radioactive Source)","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Source housing damage or loss of source during plant demolition or scrap handling","lv":"E","ct":"Identify and register all sources before any demolition or equipment removal; physically tag source housings; licensed removal and disposal only; scrap monitoring at the weighbridge","rd":"H","rp":"Radiation Protection Supervisor","pp":"Dosimeter badge, Radiation survey meter","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"CEMS / Analyser Calibration","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Calibration gas release causing asphyxiation in a small analyser room","lv":"H","ct":"Ventilate the analyser room; secure cylinders upright; O2 depletion monitor where inert gases are used; never calibrate alone in an enclosed room","rd":"M","rp":"Instrumentation Technician","pp":"Multi-gas detector, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"CEMS / Analyser Calibration","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Hot or acidic sample line contact during maintenance","lv":"H","ct":"Isolate and allow sample lines to cool and depressurise; drain condensate to a safe point; acid-resistant gloves","rd":"M","rp":"Instrumentation Technician","pp":"Chemical-resistant gloves, Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Insulation / Lagging Work","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Mineral fibre and potential legacy asbestos exposure","lv":"E","ct":"Presume legacy insulation contains asbestos until surveyed; asbestos survey and licensed removal where confirmed; enclosure and RPE for mineral fibre; wet stripping methods","rd":"H","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"P3 respirator, Disposable coveralls, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Insulation / Lagging Work","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Burns from hot surfaces during lagging removal or installation","lv":"H","ct":"Allow cool-down or work under a hot-surface permit with heat-resistant PPE; shield adjacent hot lines","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Heat-resistant gauntlets, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Painting / Surface Coating","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Solvent vapour inhalation and fire risk in enclosed areas","lv":"H","ct":"Forced ventilation and gas monitoring in enclosed spaces; eliminate ignition sources; appropriate vapour respirator; minimum quantity at the work face","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Organic vapour respirator, Nitrile gloves, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Painting / Surface Coating","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Surface preparation dust including lead or chromate from old coatings","lv":"H","ct":"Test old coatings before removal; contained blasting or needle gunning with extraction; P3 RPE; controlled waste disposal","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"P3 respirator, Disposable coveralls, Chemical goggles","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Scaffold Erection / Dismantling","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Fall during erection or dismantling when edge protection is incomplete","lv":"E","ct":"Competent scaffolders with advanced harness training; harness clipped to a secure anchor at all times during erection; sequence plan followed; no dismantling from below","rd":"H","rp":"Scaffold Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Hard hat with chin strap","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Scaffold Erection / Dismantling","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Dropped components striking persons below","lv":"E","ct":"Full exclusion zone below with hard barriers and spotter; never throw components down; use gin wheel or hoist; tool lanyards","rd":"M","rp":"Scaffold Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Demolition / Breaking Concrete","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Uncontrolled structural collapse during demolition","lv":"E","ct":"Engineered demolition method statement and sequence; temporary propping where required; no personnel beneath or inside the collapse zone; continuous structural monitoring","rd":"H","rp":"Demolition Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Demolition / Breaking Concrete","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Silica dust and noise from concrete breaking","lv":"H","ct":"Water suppression or on-tool extraction; hearing protection; rotate operators for HAVS control","rd":"M","rp":"Demolition Supervisor","pp":"P3 respirator, Ear plugs, Anti-vibration gloves","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Civil / Foundation Work","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Excavation collapse and contact with buried services","lv":"E","ct":"Permit to excavate with service drawings and locator survey; shoring or battering per soil assessment; safe access and spoil setback","rd":"H","rp":"Civil Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Roof Work / Fragile Surface","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Fall through fragile roof sheeting or rooflight","lv":"E","ct":"Presume all roof sheets are fragile; use crawl boards and edge protection; harness with running line; permit to access roof; never step on rooflights","rd":"H","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Crawl boards, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Stores / Warehouse Handling","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Racking collapse or falling stock from height","lv":"H","ct":"Racking inspection regime and load limits displayed; no climbing on racking; mechanical aids for high-level retrieval; damaged racking taken out of use","rd":"M","rp":"Stores Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Battery / UPS Room Work","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Hydrogen accumulation and explosion risk in battery rooms","lv":"E","ct":"Continuous ventilation interlocked and verified; hydrogen detection; no smoking, hot work, or sparking tools; insulated tools only","rd":"H","rp":"Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Face shield, Chemical-resistant gloves, Chemical apron","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Battery / UPS Room Work","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Acid burns and DC arc from battery banks","lv":"E","ct":"Acid-resistant PPE and eyewash at the room entrance; insulated tools and removal of metal jewellery; never bridge terminals; work on one cell at a time","rd":"M","rp":"Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Face shield, Chemical-resistant gloves, Chemical apron, Insulated tools","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Transformer Oil Handling","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Oil fire and potential PCB contamination in legacy transformer oil","lv":"E","ct":"Test legacy oil for PCB before handling; no hot work near oil-filled equipment; bunded handling and spill kit; fire protection in service","rd":"H","rp":"Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Chemical-resistant gloves, Chemical goggles, Flame-resistant (FR) coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Cable Jointing / Termination","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Induced voltage or back-feed on an apparently dead cable","lv":"E","ct":"Positive identification with a cable spiking gun where required; prove dead at the point of work; earth both ends; permit to work by authorised person","rd":"H","rp":"Licensed Electrician","pp":"Insulated electrical gloves, Arc-rated suit and hood, Voltage tester","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Earthing / Lightning Protection Work","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Working on earthing systems during storm conditions","lv":"H","ct":"Stop all earthing and lightning protection work during electrical storms; verify system isolation before disconnecting any earth conductor","rd":"M","rp":"Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Insulated electrical gloves, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Instrument Loop Checking","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Unintended plant movement triggered by loop forcing or simulation","lv":"E","ct":"Loop check under permit with field and control room coordination; physically isolate final elements before forcing; log and reverse all forces; confirm with field spotter","rd":"M","rp":"Instrumentation Technician","pp":"Two-way radio, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Weighbridge / Dispatch Operation","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Pedestrian struck by manoeuvring trucks in the dispatch yard","lv":"H","ct":"Marked pedestrian routes and refuges; drivers remain in cab or designated safe zone; mirrors and cameras at blind spots; speed limits enforced","rd":"M","rp":"Dispatch Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Palletising / Bag Stacking","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Entanglement or crush at palletiser and bag stacking mechanism","lv":"E","ct":"Interlocked perimeter guarding; never enter the cell to clear a jam without isolation; reset only from outside the guarded area","rd":"M","rp":"Packing Plant Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety gloves (general), Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Palletising / Bag Stacking","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Load instability and falling bags from stacked pallets","lv":"M","ct":"Stack within height limits and wrap securely; no walking beside unstable stacks; mechanical handling for pallet movement","rd":"L","rp":"Packing Plant Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Train / Wagon Loading","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Struck by rail movement or caught between wagons","lv":"E","ct":"Rail safety rules with derailers and blue flag protection applied before access; never cross between wagons; shunting controlled by a single competent person","rd":"H","rp":"Dispatch Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Hard hat, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Train / Wagon Loading","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Fall from wagon top during loading or hatch operation","lv":"E","ct":"Fixed access gantry with guardrails; harness where gantry not available; never walk on wagon tops unprotected","rd":"M","rp":"Dispatch Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Ship / Barge Loading","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Fall into water between vessel and quay","lv":"E","ct":"Edge protection and life rings along the quay; buoyancy aid when working at the vessel edge; controlled gangway access only; never work alone at the waterside","rd":"H","rp":"Marine Loading Supervisor","pp":"Buoyancy aid, Full body harness, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Waste Heat Recovery Operation","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Steam or hot water release from WHR boiler and pipework","lv":"E","ct":"Full isolation, depressurisation, and cool-down under permit before any work; never crack a flange under pressure; steam trace and drain points verified","rd":"H","rp":"Utilities Supervisor","pp":"Heat-resistant gauntlets, Face shield, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Waste Heat Recovery Operation","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Steam turbine overspeed or unexpected rotation during maintenance","lv":"E","ct":"Isolate and lock steam admission valves; engage turning gear lockout; verify zero speed before any rotor work","rd":"H","rp":"Utilities Supervisor","pp":"Lock and tag kit, Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Compressor Overhaul","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Stored air and residual oil pressure during compressor strip-down","lv":"H","ct":"Isolate, bleed, and verify zero pressure at all receivers and lines; allow cool-down; drain oil to a controlled container","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Technician","pp":"Safety glasses, Safety gloves (general), Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Dust Collector Bag Change","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Dust release and inhalation during bag removal","lv":"H","ct":"Isolate and allow settling before opening; remove bags into sealed containment; RPE with face-fit; wet or vacuum cleaning only","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"P3 respirator, Disposable coveralls, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Dust Collector Bag Change","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Pulse-jet cleaning air discharge during internal work","lv":"H","ct":"Isolate and bleed the compressed air header to the cleaning system; lock the solenoid supply; verify no residual pressure","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Ear plugs, Lock and tag kit","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Emergency Response / Drill","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Responder injury during drill or real emergency response","lv":"H","ct":"Scene safety assessment before entry; never enter a gas-affected or confined space without correct PPE and a rescue team; drills supervised with a safety officer and stop authority","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Task-appropriate PPE, SCBA","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Permit Issue / Area Handover","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Inadequate hazard communication at handover leading to an unsafe start","lv":"E","ct":"Face-to-face handover at the work location; walk the job with the receiving party; verify isolations physically rather than on paper; permit close-out and headcount before return to service","rd":"M","rp":"Permit Issuing Authority","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"tag":"Contractor Supervision","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Contractor unfamiliar with site hazards and emergency arrangements","lv":"H","ct":"Site induction before access; task-specific risk assessment reviewed and signed; nominated plant contact and continuous supervision for high-risk work; verify competence certificates","rd":"M","rp":"Contract Owner","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"}],"equipmentHazards":[{"et":"Rotating Thermal Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Unexpected kiln rotation or inching while personnel are inside or on the shell","lv":"E","ct":"Full LOTO on main and auxiliary drive with personal locks; kiln tyre physically chocked; all personnel accounted for and evacuated before any inching; inching controlled by a single authorised person with radio confirmation","rd":"H","rp":"Mechanical/Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Lock and tag kit, Two-way radio","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Rotating Thermal Equipment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Severe burns from residual heat inside the kiln shell after shutdown","lv":"E","ct":"Verify internal temperature below 40 degrees C with a temperature gun before entry; forced cooling and ventilation; work/rest rotation and hydration; continuous attendant monitoring","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Heat-resistant coveralls, Safety gloves (general), Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Rotating Thermal Equipment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Burns and radiant heat exposure from the rotating kiln shell and hot spots during operation","lv":"H","ct":"Maintain designated exclusion distance from the shell; infrared shell scanning rather than manual approach; never touch the shell; heat shields at fixed workstations","rd":"M","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Heat-resistant gauntlets, Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"et":"Rotating Thermal Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Falling refractory brick or coating from the kiln crown onto personnel below","lv":"E","ct":"Inspect and scale loose coating from a protected position before entry; barricade drop zones; hard hat mandatory; never work directly beneath unsupported lining","rd":"M","rp":"Refractory Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Rotating Thermal Equipment","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Oxygen deficiency and CO accumulation inside the kiln shell","lv":"E","ct":"Confined space permit with continuous multi-gas monitoring; forced ventilation maintained; standby attendant with rescue equipment at the inlet","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer / Confined Space Attendant","pp":"Multi-gas detector, Full body harness, SCBA","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Rotating Thermal Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Hot gas or flame blowback from the burner or inspection port","lv":"E","ct":"Never open inspection doors on the windward side while firing; use the designated viewing port with shield; burner isolation before front-end work","rd":"H","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Aluminised heat suit, Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"et":"Static Process Vessel","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Sudden release of hot meal at approximately 850 degrees C while clearing cyclone or duct blockages - fatal scalding","lv":"E","ct":"Clearing only by trained crew under permit using long-handled rodding equipment from a protected position; never stand in line with or below the poke hole; full aluminised heat suit and face shield; evacuate basement and lower levels before rodding; verify no personnel in the meal flow path","rd":"H","rp":"Pyro Section Supervisor","pp":"Aluminised heat suit, Face shield, Heat-resistant gauntlets, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Static Process Vessel","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Escaped hot meal accumulating unseen in basement and lower levels causing severe burns when stepped into","lv":"E","ct":"Barricade and inspect all lower levels after any meal escape; do not enter until material is confirmed cool; hot meal spill response procedure; area lighting to make accumulations visible","rd":"H","rp":"Pyro Section Supervisor","pp":"Heat-resistant boots and gaiters, Coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Static Process Vessel","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Confined space entry into cyclone or duct with residual heat and dust buildup","lv":"E","ct":"Cool-down verified below 40 degrees C; confined space permit with continuous gas testing; forced ventilation; standby attendant and rescue plan; buildup scaled from above before entry","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer / Confined Space Attendant","pp":"Heat-resistant coveralls, SCBA, Full body harness, Multi-gas detector","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Static Process Vessel","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Multi-level tower access with fall exposure at platforms, gratings, and openings","lv":"E","ct":"Guardrails and secured gratings verified; 100% tie-off when outside protection; permit for work at height; barricade openings","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Double lanyard, Hard hat with chin strap","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Static Process Vessel","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Collapse of heavy coating or buildup onto personnel during removal","lv":"E","ct":"Assess buildup extent remotely before starting; work from the top down; never position beneath the material being released; controlled removal in small sections","rd":"H","rp":"Pyro Section Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Heat-resistant coveralls","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Static Process Vessel","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Toxic process gas exposure (CO, SO2, NOx) from leaks at flanges and inspection ports","lv":"H","ct":"Personal multi-gas detector carried at all times in the pyro area; fixed detection with alarm at known leak points; maintain negative pressure; repair leaks under permit","rd":"M","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Multi-gas detector, Escape hood","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Rotating/Grinding Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Mill rotation or barring gear activation with personnel inside the mill","lv":"E","ct":"Full LOTO on main drive, auxiliary drive, and barring gear; personal locks by every entrant; mill physically secured against rotation; entry log and attendant at the manhole","rd":"H","rp":"Mechanical Maintenance Lead","pp":"Lock and tag kit, Full body harness","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Rotating/Grinding Equipment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Crush injury from grinding media, liners, or diaphragm plates shifting during relining","lv":"E","ct":"Purpose-designed liner handling equipment; never position body beneath a suspended liner; media secured or removed before liner work; controlled sequence of removal","rd":"H","rp":"Mechanical Maintenance Lead","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Rotating/Grinding Equipment","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Confined space conditions inside the mill with dust, poor ventilation, and restricted egress","lv":"E","ct":"Confined space permit, gas testing, forced ventilation; attendant at the manhole with entry log; rescue plan and retrieval equipment staged","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer / Confined Space Attendant","pp":"Full body harness, Multi-gas detector, P3 respirator, Helmet with lamp","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Rotating/Grinding Equipment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Noise exposure above 100 dB(A) during mill operation","lv":"H","ct":"Acoustic enclosure and lagging; mandatory double hearing protection in the mill house; limit occupancy time; audiometric surveillance","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Ear plugs plus ear muffs","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"et":"Rotating/Grinding Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Entanglement in trunnion, girth gear, pinion, or drive coupling","lv":"E","ct":"Fixed guards on all drive elements maintained; interlocked access gates; never approach the girth gear guard on running plant; lubrication via remote points only","rd":"M","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Close-fitting clothing","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"et":"Confined Space/Storage Vessel","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Engulfment when material bridge or rathole collapses under or onto a worker","lv":"E","ct":"Never enter a silo with material present or walk on a bridged surface; break bridges remotely with air cannon, vibrator, or lance from outside; full body harness with retrieval winch on a davit at all times; attendant with continuous visual contact","rd":"H","rp":"HSE Officer / Confined Space Attendant","pp":"Full body harness, Retrieval winch and davit, Multi-gas detector","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Confined Space/Storage Vessel","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Sudden material surge burying personnel who poke or lance a blockage from beneath the spout","lv":"E","ct":"Prohibit poking or lancing from below a clog; clear only from above or via designated external ports; barricade the discharge zone; no personnel on the receiving belt during clearing","rd":"H","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Full body harness","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Confined Space/Storage Vessel","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Silo structural failure from over-pressurisation, rathole collapse suction, or defective aeration","lv":"E","ct":"Maintain aeration and level indication systems in working order; pressure and vacuum relief verified; structural inspection regime for roof slab and beams; investigate and correct persistent ratholing","rd":"H","rp":"Plant Engineer","pp":"Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Confined Space/Storage Vessel","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Oxygen deficiency and CO2 accumulation inside the vessel","lv":"E","ct":"Continuous atmospheric monitoring at multiple depths; forced ventilation throughout occupancy; exit on any alarm","rd":"M","rp":"HSE Officer","pp":"Multi-gas detector, SCBA, Full body harness","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Confined Space/Storage Vessel","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Fall from height during vertical entry or roof access","lv":"E","ct":"Fall arrest from a certified anchor for all roof and vertical entry work; controlled descent equipment; guardrails on roof platforms","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Descent / backup device, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Confined Space/Storage Vessel","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Feeder or discharge system starting while personnel are inside","lv":"E","ct":"LOTO on all feeders, aerators, and discharge valves; personal locks by every entrant; independent isolation verification","rd":"H","rp":"Maintenance Supervisor","pp":"Lock and tag kit","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Rotating/Moving Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Entanglement at nip points between belt and pulley, idlers, or drive drums","lv":"E","ct":"Fixed nip guards at all in-running points; emergency pull-cord along the full belt length tested regularly; never clear material or adjust tracking on a running belt","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Close-fitting clothing, Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"et":"Rotating/Moving Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Stored belt tension or counterweight release during maintenance","lv":"E","ct":"Isolate and lock out drive; chock or land the take-up counterweight before releasing tension; controlled release procedure; never work beneath a suspended counterweight","rd":"M","rp":"Mechanical Maintenance Lead","pp":"Hard hat, Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Rotating/Moving Equipment","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Falling material from elevated conveyors and transfer points onto persons below","lv":"H","ct":"Skirting, covers, and catch trays at transfer points; barricade areas beneath elevated runs; hard hat mandatory in all conveyor corridors","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Rotating/Moving Equipment","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Fall from elevated conveyor gantry or transfer tower","lv":"E","ct":"Guardrails and secure walkway gratings; 100% tie-off outside protection; access permit for gantry work","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Full body harness, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Rotating/Moving Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Bucket elevator chain or belt failure causing falling buckets inside the casing","lv":"H","ct":"Isolate and secure the elevator; support head and boot sections before work; inspect chain and bucket attachment on schedule","rd":"M","rp":"Mechanical Maintenance Lead","pp":"Hard hat, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Rotating/Mechanical Equipment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Burns from hot clinker and radiant heat at the cooler","lv":"E","ct":"Verify bed temperature and allow cool-down before entry; exclusion zone at the discharge and drag chain; heat-resistant PPE for all near-cooler work","rd":"M","rp":"Pyro Section Supervisor","pp":"Heat-resistant coveralls, Heat-resistant gauntlets, Face shield","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Rotating/Mechanical Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Grate plate movement or hydraulic drive activation during maintenance","lv":"E","ct":"LOTO on hydraulic power pack and grate drive; bleed and verify zero hydraulic pressure; mechanical blocking of grate frames","rd":"M","rp":"Mechanical Maintenance Lead","pp":"Lock and tag kit, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Rotating/Mechanical Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Red river or avalanche of hot clinker from the cooler during operation","lv":"E","ct":"Maintain cooler bed depth control and monitor for red river conditions; no personnel at the discharge during unstable operation; heat shields at inspection points","rd":"H","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Aluminised heat suit","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"et":"Rotating/Mechanical Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Entanglement in rotating separator rotor, packer spouts, or roller press nip","lv":"E","ct":"Interlocked guards on all access points; never reach into a running feed zone; isolate before clearing blockages","rd":"M","rp":"Process Operator","pp":"Close-fitting clothing, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"et":"High Voltage Electrical Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Electrocution from contact with high voltage conductors","lv":"E","ct":"Isolate, lock, prove dead, and apply earths before any work; authorised HV person and permit to work; approach distances maintained; no unaccompanied HV access","rd":"H","rp":"HV Authorised Person","pp":"Arc-rated suit and hood, Insulated electrical gloves, Insulated mat, Voltage tester","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"High Voltage Electrical Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Arc flash during switching, racking, or panel access","lv":"E","ct":"Incident energy study and arc flash labelling; remote racking and switching where available; arc-rated PPE to the calculated rating; restrict access behind the arc flash boundary","rd":"H","rp":"Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Arc-rated suit and hood, Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"High Voltage Electrical Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Residual charge in capacitors, cables, and ESP transformer-rectifier sets","lv":"E","ct":"Apply earthing rod and verify zero volts after the manufacturer discharge period; earths remain applied while work is in progress","rd":"M","rp":"HV Authorised Person","pp":"Insulated electrical gloves, Earthing rod, Voltage tester","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"High Voltage Electrical Equipment","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Confined space entry into ESP casing with combustible dust and residual charge","lv":"E","ct":"Confined space permit plus electrical permit; discharge and earth all internal HV sections; clear combustible dust before entry; continuous gas and LEL monitoring","rd":"H","rp":"HSE Officer / HV Authorised Person","pp":"Anti-static coveralls, SCBA, Multi-gas detector, Full body harness","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"High Voltage Electrical Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Rapping gear or internal mechanism activation during internal work","lv":"H","ct":"LOTO on all rapper and internal drive motors; verify isolation before entry","rd":"M","rp":"Electrical Supervisor","pp":"Lock and tag kit","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Pressurized Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Stored compressed air release during maintenance","lv":"H","ct":"Isolate, bleed to atmosphere, and verify zero pressure at the gauge before breaking any joint; lock isolation valves; whip-checks on hose couplings","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Technician","pp":"Safety glasses, Safety gloves (general), Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Pressurized Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Pressure vessel or receiver failure from corrosion or defective relief valve","lv":"E","ct":"Statutory pressure vessel inspection regime; relief valve tested and certified on schedule; drain receiver condensate routinely; never isolate or adjust a relief valve","rd":"M","rp":"Plant Engineer","pp":"","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Pressurized Equipment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Hearing damage and hot surface burns in the compressor house","lv":"H","ct":"Acoustic enclosure maintained; hearing protection mandatory; lag hot piping and aftercoolers; limit occupancy time","rd":"M","rp":"Utilities Operator","pp":"Ear plugs, Heat-resistant gauntlets","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"et":"Lifting Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Load drop from hoist brake failure, rope failure, or overload","lv":"E","ct":"Statutory thorough examination and load testing current; pre-use inspection of rope, hook, and latch; overload protection functional; never exceed SWL","rd":"M","rp":"Lifting Equipment Inspector","pp":"Hard hat, Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Lifting Equipment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Personnel struck by load or crane travel in the bay","lv":"E","ct":"Exclusion zone beneath the load and along the travel path; audible warning during travel; trained banksman; never pass under a suspended load","rd":"M","rp":"Rigging Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Hi-vis vest","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Lifting Equipment","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Fall from height during crane maintenance on elevated runway or bridge","lv":"E","ct":"Fall arrest anchored to the structure; crane isolated and chocked; access permit; rescue plan for high-level work","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Engineer","pp":"Full body harness, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Lifting Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Unexpected crane movement during maintenance","lv":"E","ct":"LOTO on crane supply and travel motors; wheel chocks applied; warning signage at floor level and controls","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Engineer","pp":"Lock and tag kit","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Mobile Plant","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Collision with pedestrians or other vehicles in shared traffic areas","lv":"E","ct":"Traffic management plan with segregated routes; reversing aids and alarms functional; speed limits enforced; positive communication before approach","rd":"M","rp":"Plant/Fleet Supervisor","pp":"Hi-vis vest, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Mobile Plant","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Rollover on slopes, soft edges, or uneven ground","lv":"E","ct":"ROPS/FOPS fitted and seatbelt worn; operate within rated gradient; ground Hazard","hz":"Unexpected movement or attachment drop during maintenance","lv":"E","ct":"Park on level ground, apply park brake, chock wheels, lower or prop all attachments, isolate battery and apply LOTO before work","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Technician","pp":"Lock and tag kit, Hard hat","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Mobile Plant","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Explosive tyre failure during inflation or hot-work near a wheel assembly","lv":"E","ct":"Use a tyre cage or remote inflation with the operator out of the trajectory; never weld on an inflated wheel assembly; trained tyre fitters only","rd":"M","rp":"Tyre Fitter","pp":"Face shield, Ear plugs","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Rotating/Impact Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material before entering the feed or discharge zone","rd":"M","rp":"Area Supervisor","pp":"Hard hat, Safety gloves (general), Safety boots","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Rotating/Impact Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Stored energy release from flywheel, springs, or hydraulic accumulators","lv":"E","ct":"Verify zero energy state; mechanically block the flywheel; bleed accumulators and verify at gauge before disassembly","rd":"M","rp":"Maintenance Engineer","pp":"Hard hat, Safety gloves (general)","mo":"Any","st":"Shutdown/Isolated"},{"et":"Rotating/Impact Equipment","cat":"Job Related Hazard","hz":"Flying rock fragments and dust from the crushing operation","lv":"H","ct":"Screens hat","mo":"Any","st":"Operational"},{"et":"Static/Confined Space Equipment","cat":"Job Location Related Hazard","hz":"Confined space entry into bag filter or duct with combustible discrepancy","rd":"H","rp":"Security Manager","pp":"","mo":"Any","st":"Any"},{"et":"Water Treatment Equipment","cat":"Equipment and Material Related Hazard","hz":"Chlorine or treatment gas
