<script>const ADMIN_CRED = {"salt": "c940c3a99686573d9a2268d958a71e14", "hash": "pbkdf2:e3566f5991d368cf4543f57be0bab987be22662b5a6d2a2d97f460762464fd8c", "iters": 150000};
// =====================================================================
// ADMIN AUTHENTICATION
// The password is never stored in this file. Only a random salt and a
// PBKDF2-derived hash are embedded, so the source reveals nothing usable.
//
// HONEST LIMITATION: this is a static file. A determined person can read
// the code and remove the gate entirely. Hashing stops the password being
// *read and shared*; it does not make the admin area tamper-proof. For
// field distribution use the FIELD build, which contains no admin code.
// =====================================================================
const PBKDF2_ITERS = 150000;

// ---- pure-JS SHA-256 (fallback where crypto.subtle is unavailable) ----
function _sha256Bytes(bytes){
  const K=[0x428a2f98,0x71374491,0xb5c0fbcf,0xe9b5dba5,0x3956c25b,0x59f111f1,0x923f82a4,0xab1c5ed5,
  0xd807aa98,0x12835b01,0x243185be,0x550c7dc3,0x72be5d74,0x80deb1fe,0x9bdc06a7,0xc19bf174,
  0xe49b69c1,0xefbe4786,0x0fc19dc6,0x240ca1cc,0x2de92c6f,0x4a7484aa,0x5cb0a9dc,0x76f988da,
  0x983e5152,0xa831c66d,0xb00327c8,0xbf597fc7,0xc6e00bf3,0xd5a79147,0x06ca6351,0x14292967,
  0x27b70a85,0x2e1b2138,0x4d2c6dfc,0x53380d13,0x650a7354,0x766a0abb,0x81c2c92e,0x92722c85,
  0xa2bfe8a1,0xa81a664b,0xc24b8b70,0xc76c51a3,0xd192e819,0xd6990624,0xf40e3585,0x106aa070,
  0x19a4c116,0x1e376c08,0x2748774c,0x34b0bcb5,0x391c0cb3,0x4ed8aa4a,0x5b9cca4f,0x682e6ff3,
  0x748f82ee,0x78a5636f,0x84c87814,0x8cc70208,0x90befffa,0xa4506ceb,0xbef9a3f7,0xc67178f2];
  let H=[0x6a09e667,0xbb67ae85,0x3c6ef372,0xa54ff53a,0x510e527f,0x9b05688c,0x1f83d9ab,0x5be0cd19];
  const l=bytes.length, bitLen=l*8;
  const withPad=new Uint8Array((((l+9)+63)>>6)<<6);
  withPad.set(bytes); withPad[l]=0x80;
  new DataView(withPad.buffer).setUint32(withPad.length-4, bitLen>>>0, false);
  new DataView(withPad.buffer).setUint32(withPad.length-8, Math.floor(bitLen/4294967296), false);
  const w=new Uint32Array(64), dv=new DataView(withPad.buffer);
  const rr=(x,n)=>(x>>>n)|(x<<(32-n));
  for(let i=0;i<withPad.length;i+=64){
    for(let t=0;t<16;t++) w[t]=dv.getUint32(i+t*4,false);
    for(let t=16;t<64;t++){
      const s0=rr(w[t-15],7)^rr(w[t-15],18)^(w[t-15]>>>3);
      const s1=rr(w[t-2],17)^rr(w[t-2],19)^(w[t-2]>>>10);
      w[t]=(w[t-16]+s0+w[t-7]+s1)>>>0;
    }
    let [a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h]=H;
    for(let t=0;t<64;t++){
      const S1=rr(e,6)^rr(e,11)^rr(e,25), ch=(e&f)^(~e&g);
      const t1=(h+S1+ch+K[t]+w[t])>>>0;
      const S0=rr(a,2)^rr(a,13)^rr(a,22), maj=(a&b)^(a&c)^(b&c);
      const t2=(S0+maj)>>>0;
      h=g; g=f; f=e; e=(d+t1)>>>0; d=c; c=b; b=a; a=(t1+t2)>>>0;
    }
    H=[(H[0]+a)>>>0,(H[1]+b)>>>0,(H[2]+c)>>>0,(H[3]+d)>>>0,
       (H[4]+e)>>>0,(H[5]+f)>>>0,(H[6]+g)>>>0,(H[7]+h)>>>0];
  }
  const out=new Uint8Array(32), odv=new DataView(out.buffer);
  H.forEach((v,i)=>odv.setUint32(i*4,v,false));
  return out;
}
function _enc(s){ return new TextEncoder().encode(s); }
function _concat(a,b){ const o=new Uint8Array(a.length+b.length); o.set(a); o.set(b,a.length); return o; }
function _hex(b){ return Array.from(b).map(x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join(''); }

// Iterated salted SHA-256 - slow by design, mirrors PBKDF2 intent for the fallback path
function _slowHashJS(pw, saltHex, iters){
  let cur = _concat(_enc(saltHex), _enc(pw));
  for(let i=0;i<iters;i++) cur = _sha256Bytes(cur);
  return _hex(cur);
}

async function hashPassword(pw, saltHex, iters){
  iters = iters || PBKDF2_ITERS;
  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.subtle) {
    try{
      const salt = Uint8Array.from(saltHex.match(/.{2}/g).map(h=>parseInt(h,16)));
      const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', _enc(pw), 'PBKDF2', false, ['deriveBits']);
      const bits = await crypto.subtle.deriveBits(
        {name:'PBKDF2', salt:salt, iterations:iters, hash:'SHA-256'}, key, 256);
      return 'pbkdf2:' + _hex(new Uint8Array(bits));
    }catch(e){ /* fall through */ }
  }
  // fallback: fewer iterations so low-end phones stay responsive
  return 'sha256x:' + _slowHashJS(pw, saltHex, Math.min(iters, 20000));
}

function randomSaltHex(){
  const a = new Uint8Array(16);
  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.getRandomValues) crypto.getRandomValues(a);
  else for(let i=0;i<16;i++) a[i]=Math.floor(Math.random()*256);
  return _hex(a);
}

// Credential in use: an admin-set override (this device) takes precedence
// over the credential built into the file.
const CRED_STORE = 'okpella_admin_cred';
function currentCred(){
  try{ const r = localStorage.getItem(CRED_STORE); if(r) return JSON.parse(r); }catch(e){}
  return ADMIN_CRED;   // injected at build time
}

async function verifyPassword(pw){
  const c = currentCred();
  const h = await hashPassword(pw, c.salt, c.iters);
  // constant-time-ish compare
  const a = h, b = c.hash;
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let diff = 0;
  for (let i=0;i<a.length;i++) diff |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);
  return diff === 0;
}
</script>
<script>const BUNDLED = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('app-data').textContent);
const STORE = 'okpella_v2_data';
const AI_KEY = 'okpella_llm_key_v1';

let DATA = (function(){ try { const r = localStorage.getItem(STORE); return r ? JSON.parse(r) : BUNDLED; } catch(e){ return BUNDLED; } })();
function persist(d){ try { localStorage.setItem(STORE, JSON.stringify(d)); } catch(e){} }

const CATS = ['Job Related Hazard','Job Location Related Hazard','Equipment and Material Related Hazard'];
const CAT_LABEL = {'Job Related Hazard':'1. Job Related Hazard','Job Location Related Hazard':'2. Job Location Related Hazard','Equipment and Material Related Hazard':'3. Equipment and Material Related Hazard'};
const LEVEL_ORDER = {E:0,H:1,M:2,L:3,N:4};

const selectedTags = new Set();
// How the current assessment was assembled - used for the audit log
let genMethods = new Set();
function noteMethod(m){ genMethods.add(m); }
function esc(s){ return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c])); }
function badge(l){ return l ? '<span class="lvl-badge '+l+'">'+l+'</span>' : '<span class="lvl-badge blank">—</span>'; }

// =====================================================================
// CORE: union-based generation. Activity hazards ∪ Equipment hazards.
// Never returns empty as long as one activity or equipment type is set.
// =====================================================================
function collectHazards(){
  const et = isTypeListed() ? document.getElementById('equipType').value : null;
  const mo = document.getElementById('equipMotion').value;
  const st = document.getElementById('equipStatus').value;
  const okM = v => v === 'Any' || v === mo;
  const okS = v => v === 'Any' || v === st;

  const out = [];
  DATA.activityHazards.forEach(h => {
    if (selectedTags.has(h.tag) && okM(h.mo) && okS(h.st)) out.push(Object.assign({}, h, {src:'Activity: '+h.tag}));
  });
  DATA.equipmentHazards.forEach(h => {
    if (h.et === et && okM(h.mo) && okS(h.st)) out.push(Object.assign({}, h, {src:'Equipment: '+h.et}));
  });

  // de-duplicate identical hazard text, keeping the highest severity
  const seen = new Map();
  out.forEach(h => {
    const k = h.hz.toLowerCase().trim();
    const prev = seen.get(k);
    if (!prev || (LEVEL_ORDER[h.lv] ?? 9) < (LEVEL_ORDER[prev.lv] ?? 9)) seen.set(k, h);
  });
  return Array.from(seen.values()).sort((a,b)=>(LEVEL_ORDER[a.lv]??9)-(LEVEL_ORDER[b.lv]??9));
}


// =====================================================================
// ASSESSMENT SERIAL NUMBER + AUDIT LOG
// Format: RA-YYMMDD-HHMMSSCC-AAA
//   YYMMDD   date
//   HHMMSSCC time to centiseconds (sorts chronologically as plain text)
//   AAA      3-char code derived from the specific equipment, else equipment type
// =====================================================================
const LOG_STORE = 'okpella_assessment_log';
const AREA_STOP = new Set(['the','and','for','of','a','an','with','on','in','to']);

function areaCode(txt){
  if(!txt) return 'GEN';
  var words = String(txt).toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9 ]/g,' ').split(/\s+/)
                .filter(function(w){ return w && !AREA_STOP.has(w.toLowerCase()); });
  if(!words.length) return 'GEN';
  var code;
  if(words.length >= 3)      code = words[0][0] + words[1][0] + words[2][0];
  else if(words.length === 2) code = words[0][0] + words[1][0] + (words[1][1] || words[0][1] || 'X');
  else {
    var w = words[0];
    var cons = w.replace(/[AEIOU]/g,'');
    code = (cons.length >= 3 ? cons.slice(0,3) : (w + 'XXX').slice(0,3));
  }
  return (code + 'XXX').slice(0,3).toUpperCase();
}

// Excel date serial: whole days since 1899-12-30 (matches Excel's own numbering)
function excelDateSerial(d){
  var utcMidnight = Date.UTC(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate());
  var epoch = Date.UTC(1899, 11, 30);
  return Math.round((utcMidnight - epoch) / 86400000);
}

function makeSerial(equipRef, equipType){
  var d = new Date();
  var p = function(n,w){ return String(n).padStart(w||2,'0'); };
  var cs   = Math.floor(d.getMilliseconds()/10);
  var time = p(d.getHours()) + p(d.getMinutes()) + p(d.getSeconds()) + p(cs);
  // Single unbroken token: <excel date serial><HHMMSSCC><3-char area code>
  return { serial: String(excelDateSerial(d)) + time + areaCode(equipRef || equipType), iso: d.toISOString() };
}

function readLog(){ try{ var r=localStorage.getItem(LOG_STORE); return r?JSON.parse(r):[]; }catch(e){ return []; } }
function writeLog(a){ try{ localStorage.setItem(LOG_STORE, JSON.stringify(a)); }catch(e){} }
function appendLog(entry){
  var a = readLog(); a.unshift(entry);
  if(a.length > 1000) a = a.slice(0,1000);   // cap storage growth
  writeLog(a);
}

// =====================================================================
// BUILD PAGE
// =====================================================================
let openPresetCats = {};
function renderPresets(filter){
  filter = (filter||'').toLowerCase().trim();
  const g = document.getElementById('presetGrid'); g.innerHTML='';
  const list = DATA.presets.filter(p => !filter || (p.n+' '+p.d+' '+(p.cat||'')+' '+p.tags.join(' ')).toLowerCase().includes(filter));
  if (!list.length){ g.innerHTML = '<div class="chip-empty">No matching tasks — try "start from scratch" below.</div>'; return; }

  // group by category, preserving first-seen order
  const groups = {}; const order = [];
  list.forEach(p => { const c = p.cat || 'Other';
    if(!groups[c]){ groups[c]=[]; order.push(c); } groups[c].push(p); });

  order.forEach(cat => {
    const item = document.createElement('div'); item.className = 'acc-item';
    const isOpen = !!filter || openPresetCats[cat];
    if(isOpen) item.classList.add('open');
    const head = document.createElement('button'); head.className='acc-head';
    head.innerHTML = '<span>'+esc(cat)+'</span><span class="cnt">'+groups[cat].length+'</span>';
    head.addEventListener('click', ()=>{ item.classList.toggle('open');
      openPresetCats[cat] = item.classList.contains('open'); });
    const body = document.createElement('div'); body.className='acc-body';
    groups[cat].forEach(p => {
      const b = document.createElement('button'); b.className='preset-card';
      b.innerHTML = '<div class="pt">'+esc(p.n)+'</div><div class="pd">'+esc(p.d)+'</div>'+
                    '<div class="pm">'+p.tags.length+' activities · '+esc(p.loc || 'choose area')+'</div>';
      b.addEventListener('click', ()=>applyPreset(p));
      body.appendChild(b);
    });
    item.appendChild(head); item.appendChild(body); g.appendChild(item);
  });
}

function applyPreset(p){
  noteMethod('Preset');
  selectedTags.clear();
  p.tags.forEach(t => { if (DATA.jobTags.some(j=>j.t===t)) selectedTags.add(t); });
  setEquipmentContext({loc: p.loc||'', et: p.et||'', ref: p.ref||'', mo: p.mo, st: p.st, workArea: ''});
  document.getElementById('jobType').value = p.n;
  document.getElementById('jobDesc').value = p.d;
  openRefine();
}
// Department is deliberately NOT auto-filled. The equipment record gives an
// area/section (already shown as "Location of work"), which is not the same thing
// as the owning department - inferring one from the other would fabricate data on a
// safety document. Left blank unless the user supplies it, and omitted if empty.
function suggestDept(){ /* intentionally no-op */ }

function setVal(id, v){ const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el && v) el.value = v; }

function discardReport(){
  var r = document.getElementById('report');
  if(r){ r.classList.remove('visible'); r.innerHTML=''; r.style.display=''; }
}
function openRefine(){
  discardReport();                                   // a stale report must not hide the builder
  document.getElementById('page-build').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('cardPresets').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('refineArea').style.display = 'block';
  renderChips(); renderAccordion(''); updateCount(); renderActionBar('build');
  window.scrollTo(0,0);
}
function backToPresets(){
  // Full state reset so a previous preset never bleeds into a fresh start
  selectedTags.clear();
  genMethods = new Set();
  if(typeof clearQCard==='function'){ clearQCard(); setChatPrompt(null); }
  pending = {tags:new Set(),et:null,mo:null,st:null,ref:null,jt:'',jd:'',dept:'',assessor:'',approver:'',toolsAsked:false,toolsDetected:[],awaiting:null,loc:'',locOther:'',locCandidates:null,etOther:'',refOther:'',workArea:'',otherMode:{}};
  ['jobType','jobDesc','deptName','assessorName','approverName'].forEach(function(id){
    var el=document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.value='';
  });
  setEquipmentContext({workArea:''});
  var moEl=document.getElementById('equipMotion'); if(moEl) moEl.selectedIndex=0;
  var stEl=document.getElementById('equipStatus'); if(stEl) stEl.selectedIndex=0;
  var ts=document.getElementById('tagSearch'); if(ts) ts.value='';
  renderChips(); renderAccordion(''); updateCount();
  document.getElementById('report').classList.remove('visible');
  document.getElementById('report').innerHTML='';
  document.getElementById('presetSearch').value='';
  renderPresets('');
  discardReport();
  var vb=document.getElementById('valBanner'); if(vb) vb.classList.remove('show');
  var vb2=document.getElementById('valBanner2'); if(vb2) vb2.classList.remove('show');
  document.querySelectorAll('.field-invalid').forEach(function(w){ w.classList.remove('field-invalid'); });
  document.getElementById('page-build').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('cardPresets').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('refineArea').style.display = 'none';
  renderActionBar('start');
  window.scrollTo(0,0);
}

function updateCount(){ document.getElementById('tagCount').textContent = selectedTags.size; }

function renderChips(){
  const c = document.getElementById('selectedChips'); c.innerHTML='';
  if (!selectedTags.size){ c.innerHTML = '<span class="chip-empty">None yet — pick from the list below.</span>'; return; }
  Array.from(selectedTags).forEach(t=>{
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className='chip-sel'; b.innerHTML = esc(t)+'<span class="x">×</span>';
    b.addEventListener('click', ()=>{ noteMethod('Manual edit'); selectedTags.delete(t); renderChips(); renderAccordion(document.getElementById('tagSearch').value); updateCount(); });
    c.appendChild(b);
  });
}

function renderAccordion(filter){
  filter = (filter||'').toLowerCase().trim();
  const host = document.getElementById('tagAccordion'); host.innerHTML='';
  const byCat = {};
  DATA.jobTags.forEach(t=>{ (byCat[t.c] = byCat[t.c]||[]).push(t.t); });
  Object.keys(byCat).forEach(cat=>{
    const tags = byCat[cat].filter(t => !filter || t.toLowerCase().includes(filter));
    if (!tags.length) return;
    const nSel = tags.filter(t=>selectedTags.has(t)).length;
    const item = document.createElement('div');
    item.className = 'acc-item' + ((filter || nSel) ? ' open' : '');
    const head = document.createElement('button');
    head.className='acc-head';
    head.innerHTML = '<span>'+esc(cat)+'</span><span class="cnt'+(nSel?' on':'')+'">'+(nSel?nSel+' / ':'')+tags.length+'</span>';
    head.addEventListener('click', ()=>item.classList.toggle('open'));
    const body = document.createElement('div'); body.className='acc-body';
    tags.forEach(t=>{
      const row = document.createElement('div'); row.className='tag-row';
      const id = 'tg-'+t.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi,'');
      row.innerHTML = '<input type="checkbox" id="'+id+'"><label for="'+id+'">'+esc(t)+'</label>';
      const cb = row.querySelector('input'); cb.checked = selectedTags.has(t);
      cb.addEventListener('change', ()=>{ noteMethod('Manual edit'); cb.checked?selectedTags.add(t):selectedTags.delete(t); renderChips(); updateCount();
        const h = item.querySelector('.cnt'); const n = tags.filter(x=>selectedTags.has(x)).length;
        h.className = 'cnt'+(n?' on':''); h.textContent = (n?n+' / ':'')+tags.length; });
      body.appendChild(row);
    });
    item.appendChild(head); item.appendChild(body); host.appendChild(item);
  });
}

// =====================================================================
// LOCATION -> EQUIPMENT TYPE -> SPECIFIC EQUIPMENT cascade
// Each level narrows the next. Nothing dead-ends: items elsewhere in the
// plant stay reachable in a second group, and every level has "Other".
// =====================================================================
const OTHER = '__other__';
const OTHER_LOC_NAME = 'Other Area (User should Specify)';

function locTypeOf(name){
  var l = (DATA.locations||[]).find(function(x){ return x.n === name; });
  return l ? l.type : '';
}
function equipAvailable(e, loc){
  if(!loc || loc === OTHER || loc === OTHER_LOC_NAME) return true;   // unknown area: don't hide anything
  var scope = e.scope || 'All areas';
  if(scope === 'All areas') return true;
  if(scope === 'All process areas') return locTypeOf(loc) === 'Process';
  return (e.locs||[]).indexOf(loc) !== -1;
}

function _opt(sel, value, text){
  var o=document.createElement('option'); o.value=value; o.textContent=text; sel.appendChild(o); return o;
}
function _group(sel, label, items){
  if(!items.length) return;
  var g=document.createElement('optgroup'); g.label=label;
  items.forEach(function(it){ var o=document.createElement('option'); o.value=it; o.textContent=it; g.appendChild(o); });
  sel.appendChild(g);
}
function _showOther(inputId, on){
  var el=document.getElementById(inputId); if(!el) return;
  el.classList.toggle('show', !!on);
  if(!on) el.value='';
}

function populateLocations(keep){
  var sel=document.getElementById('locArea'); var prev = keep!==undefined ? keep : sel.value;
  sel.innerHTML='';
  _opt(sel,'','— select area —');
  var byType={};
  (DATA.locations||[]).forEach(function(l){
    if(l.n === OTHER_LOC_NAME) return;
    (byType[l.type||'Other']=byType[l.type||'Other']||[]).push(l.n);
  });
  [['Process','Process areas'],['Utility','Utilities'],['Site','Site & yard'],['Building','Buildings'],['Other','Other']]
    .forEach(function(p){ _group(sel, p[1], byType[p[0]]||[]); });
  _opt(sel, OTHER, 'Other area (specify)…');
  sel.value = prev || '';
  if(sel.value !== prev) sel.value='';
}

function refreshTypes(keep){
  var loc=document.getElementById('locArea').value;
  var sel=document.getElementById('equipType'); var prev = keep!==undefined ? keep : sel.value;
  sel.innerHTML='';
  _opt(sel,'','— select equipment type —');
  var here=new Set();
  DATA.equipment.forEach(function(e){ if(equipAvailable(e,loc)) here.add(e.t); });
  var inArea = DATA.equipmentTypes.filter(function(t){ return here.has(t); });
  var rest   = DATA.equipmentTypes.filter(function(t){ return !here.has(t); });
  if(loc && loc!==OTHER){ _group(sel,'Found in this area',inArea); _group(sel,'Other equipment types',rest); }
  else _group(sel,'All equipment types',DATA.equipmentTypes.slice());
  _opt(sel, OTHER, 'Other type (specify)…');
  sel.value = prev || '';
  if(sel.value !== prev) sel.value='';
  _showOther('equipTypeOther', sel.value===OTHER);
  document.getElementById('typeNote').classList.toggle('show', sel.value===OTHER);
}

function refreshEquip(keep){
  var loc=document.getElementById('locArea').value;
  var et=document.getElementById('equipType').value;
  var sel=document.getElementById('equipRef'); var prev = keep!==undefined ? keep : sel.value;
  sel.innerHTML='';
  _opt(sel,'','— select equipment —');
  var ofType = DATA.equipment.filter(function(e){ return !et || et===OTHER || e.t===et; });
  var here  = ofType.filter(function(e){ return equipAvailable(e,loc); }).map(function(e){ return e.n; });
  var other = ofType.filter(function(e){ return !equipAvailable(e,loc); }).map(function(e){ return e.n; });
  if(loc && loc!==OTHER){ _group(sel,'In this area',here); _group(sel,'Same type — other areas',other); }
  else _group(sel,'Equipment',here.concat(other));
  _opt(sel, OTHER, 'Other equipment (specify)…');
  sel.value = prev || '';
  if(sel.value !== prev) sel.value='';
  _showOther('equipRefOther', sel.value===OTHER);
}

function onLocChange(){
  _showOther('locOther', document.getElementById('locArea').value===OTHER);
  refreshTypes(); refreshEquip(); clearFieldError('locArea');
}
function onTypeChange(){ refreshTypes(); refreshEquip(); clearFieldError('equipType'); }
function onEquipChange(){
  var v=document.getElementById('equipRef').value;
  // choosing a real item keeps the type consistent with it, so the header and hazards cannot disagree
  if(v && v!==OTHER){
    var e=DATA.equipment.find(function(x){ return x.n===v; });
    if(e && document.getElementById('equipType').value!==e.t){ refreshTypes(e.t); refreshEquip(v); }
  }
  _showOther('equipRefOther', document.getElementById('equipRef').value===OTHER);
  clearFieldError('equipRef');
}

// one entry point for presets, the AI and resets
function setEquipmentContext(c){
  c = c || {};
  populateLocations(c.loc || '');
  _showOther('locOther', document.getElementById('locArea').value===OTHER);
  refreshTypes(c.et || '');
  refreshEquip(c.ref || '');
  if(c.mo) document.getElementById('equipMotion').value = c.mo;
  if(c.st) document.getElementById('equipStatus').value = c.st;
  var wa=document.getElementById('workArea'); if(wa && c.workArea!==undefined) wa.value = c.workArea;
}

// ---- resolved values (Other -> the typed text) ----
function getLocation(){
  var v=document.getElementById('locArea').value;
  return v===OTHER ? document.getElementById('locOther').value.trim() : v;
}
function getEquipType(){
  var v=document.getElementById('equipType').value;
  return v===OTHER ? document.getElementById('equipTypeOther').value.trim() : v;
}
function getEquipment(){
  var v=document.getElementById('equipRef').value;
  return v===OTHER ? document.getElementById('equipRefOther').value.trim() : v;
}
function isTypeListed(){
  var v=document.getElementById('equipType').value;
  return !!v && v!==OTHER;
}

function clearFieldError(id){
  var el=document.getElementById(id); if(!el) return;
  var w=el.closest('.field'); if(w) w.classList.remove('field-invalid');
}

// replaces the old flat populateEquip()
function populateEquip(){ setEquipmentContext({}); }


// =====================================================================
// REPORT
// =====================================================================
const REQUIRED_JOB_FIELDS = [
  ['jobType','Job Type'], ['jobDesc','Description'], ['deptName','Department / Section'],
  ['assessorName','Assessed By'], ['approverName','Approved By']
];

const REQUIRED_EQUIP_FIELDS = [
  ['locArea','Location of work','locOther'],
  ['equipType','Equipment Type','equipTypeOther'],
  ['equipRef','Specific Equipment','equipRefOther'],
  ['workArea','Specific work area',null]
];
function validateEquipment(){
  var missing=[];
  REQUIRED_EQUIP_FIELDS.forEach(function(f){
    var el=document.getElementById(f[0]); var wrap=el?el.closest('.field'):null;
    var v=el?el.value.trim():'';
    var empty = !v;
    if(!empty && v===OTHER && f[2]){ empty = !document.getElementById(f[2]).value.trim(); }
    if(wrap) wrap.classList.toggle('field-invalid', empty);
    if(empty) missing.push(f[1] + (v===OTHER ? ' (describe it)' : ''));
  });
  var b=document.getElementById('valBanner2');
  if(b){
    if(missing.length){
      b.innerHTML='<b>Complete the location and equipment.</b><br>Still needed: '+missing.map(esc).join(', ')+'.';
      b.classList.add('show');
    } else b.classList.remove('show');
  }
  return missing;
}

function validateJobDetails(){
  var missing = [];
  REQUIRED_JOB_FIELDS.forEach(function(f){
    var el = document.getElementById(f[0]);
    var wrap = el ? el.closest('.field') : null;
    var empty = !el || !el.value.trim();
    if(wrap) wrap.classList.toggle('field-invalid', empty);
    if(empty) missing.push(f[1]);
  });
  var b = document.getElementById('valBanner');
  if(b){
    if(missing.length){
      b.innerHTML = '<b>Complete the job details before generating.</b><br>Still needed: ' +
                    missing.map(esc).join(', ') +
                    '.<br>These identify the assessment and who is accountable for it, so they are recorded on the printed form.';
      b.classList.add('show');
    } else b.classList.remove('show');
  }
  return missing;
}

function generateReport(){
  var missing = validateJobDetails();
  var missing2 = validateEquipment();
  if(missing.length || missing2.length){
    var target = missing.length ? 'valBanner' : 'valBanner2';
    document.getElementById(target).scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'center'});
    return;
  }
  const hz = collectHazards();
  const et = getEquipType();
  const mo = document.getElementById('equipMotion').value;
  const st = document.getElementById('equipStatus').value;
  const ref = getEquipment();
  const loc = getLocation();
  const workArea = document.getElementById('workArea').value.trim();
  const jt = document.getElementById('jobType').value;
  const jd = document.getElementById('jobDesc').value;
  const dept = document.getElementById('deptName').value.trim();
  const as = document.getElementById('assessorName').value;
  const ap = document.getElementById('approverName').value;

  const counts={E:0,H:0,M:0,L:0,N:0};
  hz.forEach(h=>{ if(counts[h.lv]!==undefined) counts[h.lv]++; });
  const ppe = new Set();
  hz.forEach(h=>String(h.pp||'').split(',').map(s=>s.trim()).filter(s=>s&&s!=='N/A').forEach(p=>ppe.add(p)));
  const sn = makeSerial(ref, et);

  let h = '<div class="report-banner">'+
          '<div class="company">OKPELLA CEMENT LIMITED, OKPELLA</div>'+
          '<div class="form-title">RISK ASSESSMENT FORM</div></div><div class="report-body">';
  h += kv('Assessment No.', sn.serial);
  if(dept) h += kv('Department / Section', dept);
  h += kv('Location of work', loc);
  h += kv('Specific work area', workArea);
  h += kv('Specific equipment', ref);
  h += kv('Title of work', (jt||'(not set)') + (ref? ' — '+ref : ''));
  h += kv('Description', jd) + kv('Equipment Type', et + (isTypeListed() ? '' : ' (unlisted - activity hazards only)')) + kv('Motion / Status', mo+' / '+st);
  h += kv('Activities', Array.from(selectedTags).join(', ') || 'none selected');
  var hzIdx = 0;
  h += '<div class="verify-bar" id="verifyBar"><b>Verify before export.</b> Every hazard below was auto-generated. '+
       'Untick any that do not apply to this job — they stay on the record, clearly marked as reviewed and excluded, '+
       'so the assessment shows what was considered as well as what was accepted.'+
       '<div class="verify-counts" id="verifyCounts"></div></div>';
  h += '<div class="summary-strip"><span class="chip total">'+hz.length+' HAZARDS</span>'+
       ['E','H','M','L','N'].map(l=>counts[l]?'<span class="chip '+l+'">'+counts[l]+' '+({E:'EXTREME',H:'HIGH',M:'MODERATE',L:'LOW',N:'NEGLIGIBLE'})[l]+'</span>':'').join('')+'</div>';

  CATS.forEach(c=>{
    const rows = hz.filter(x=>x.cat===c);
    h += '<div class="hazard-section"><h3>'+CAT_LABEL[c]+'</h3>';
    if (!rows.length) h += '<div class="empty-note">No hazards in this category for the current selection.</div>';
    else rows.forEach(r=>{
      var hid = 'hz'+(hzIdx++);
      h += '<div class="hazard-card" id="'+hid+'" data-lv="'+esc(r.lv)+'" data-ppe="'+esc(r.pp||'')+'">'+
           '<div class="hz-check"><input type="checkbox" checked id="'+hid+'_cb" onchange="toggleHazard(\''+hid+'\')">'+
           '<div style="flex:1;">'+
           '<div class="hz-top"><div class="hz-title">'+esc(r.hz)+'</div>'+badge(r.lv)+'</div>'+
           '<div class="hz-control">'+esc(r.ct)+'</div>'+
           '<div class="hz-arrow">↓ Residual risk after controls '+badge(r.rd)+'</div>'+
           '<div class="hz-src">'+esc(r.src)+' · Responsible: '+esc(r.rp||'—')+'</div>'+
           '<div class="excl-tag">AUTO-GENERATED — REVIEWED AND EXCLUDED BY ASSESSOR AS NOT APPLICABLE</div>'+
           '</div></div></div>';
    });
    h += '</div>';
  });

  h += '<div class="hazard-section"><h3>PPE Required (auto-compiled)</h3><div class="ppe-list" id="ppeList">'+
       (ppe.size? esc(Array.from(ppe).join('; ')) : 'No PPE data for current selection.')+'</div></div>';
  h += '<div class="erp-box"><strong>Emergency Response Plan</strong>Stop whatever you are doing and call the following number:<br>'+
       'Safety Officers: 08056857344, 08152711025, 07085713676, 08056862447, 08056848737<br>'+
       'HSE Manager: 09053739601 &nbsp; Security Manager: 08110540298<br>'+
       'Extensions: HSE: 1539, 1540, 1165 &nbsp; Fire: 1539 &nbsp; Security: 1468</div>';
  h += '<div class="hazard-section"><h3>Authorization</h3><div class="sign-grid">'+
       '<div>'+kv('Assessor', as||'—')+'</div><div>'+kv('Safety Approval', ap||'—')+'</div></div></div></div>';

  document.getElementById('report').innerHTML = h;
  document.getElementById('report').classList.add('visible');
  document.getElementById('page-build').style.display='none';
  renderActionBar('report');
  recomputeReport();
  window.scrollTo(0,0);

  var methodList = Array.from(genMethods);
  var methodLabel = methodList.length === 0 ? 'From scratch'
                  : methodList.length === 1 ? methodList[0]
                  : 'Combination (' + methodList.join(' + ') + ')';
  appendLog({
    serial: sn.serial, generatedAt: sn.iso,
    generationMethod: methodLabel, methodsUsed: methodList,
    jobType: jt || '', description: jd || '',
    equipmentType: et, equipmentMotion: mo, equipmentStatus: st, equipmentRef: ref || '',
    location: loc || '', workArea: workArea || '', department: dept || '', activities: Array.from(selectedTags),
    activityCount: selectedTags.size, hazardCount: hz.length,
    extreme: counts.E, high: counts.H, moderate: counts.M, low: counts.L, negligible: counts.N,
    assessedBy: as || '', approvedBy: ap || ''
  });
  if(document.getElementById('page-admin').style.display==='block') renderLog();
}
function kv(k,v){ return '<div class="kv-row"><div class="k">'+esc(k)+'</div><div class="v">'+esc(v)+'</div></div>'; }

// ---- include / exclude a hazard, then recompute counts, PPE and the summary strip ----
function toggleHazard(id){
  var card = document.getElementById(id);
  var cb = document.getElementById(id+'_cb');
  if(!card || !cb) return;
  card.classList.toggle('excluded', !cb.checked);
  recomputeReport();
}

function recomputeReport(){
  var cards = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#report .hazard-card'));
  if(!cards.length) return;
  var inc = cards.filter(function(c){ return !c.classList.contains('excluded'); });
  var exc = cards.length - inc.length;

  // severity counts over INCLUDED hazards only
  var counts = {E:0,H:0,M:0,L:0,N:0};
  inc.forEach(function(c){ var l=c.getAttribute('data-lv'); if(counts[l]!==undefined) counts[l]++; });

  var strip = document.querySelector('#report .summary-strip');
  if(strip){
    var names={E:'EXTREME',H:'HIGH',M:'MODERATE',L:'LOW',N:'NEGLIGIBLE'};
    var html='<span class="chip total">'+inc.length+' HAZARDS</span>';
    ['E','H','M','L','N'].forEach(function(l){
      if(counts[l]) html+='<span class="chip '+l+'">'+counts[l]+' '+names[l]+'</span>';
    });
    if(exc) html+='<span class="chip" style="background:#8c98a4;">'+exc+' EXCLUDED</span>';
    strip.innerHTML = html;
  }

  var vc = document.getElementById('verifyCounts');
  if(vc){
    vc.innerHTML = '<span class="vc inc">'+inc.length+' included</span>'+
                   '<span class="vc exc">'+exc+' excluded</span>';
  }

  // PPE recompiled from included hazards only
  var ppe = [], seen = {};
  inc.forEach(function(c){
    String(c.getAttribute('data-ppe')||'').split(',').forEach(function(p){
      p=p.trim(); if(p && p!=='N/A' && !seen[p]){ seen[p]=1; ppe.push(p); }
    });
  });
  var pl = document.getElementById('ppeList');
  if(pl) pl.textContent = ppe.length ? ppe.join('; ') : 'No PPE data for the included hazards.';
}

// =====================================================================
// ACTION BAR + NAV
// =====================================================================
function renderActionBar(mode){
  const b = document.getElementById('actionBar'); b.style.display='flex';
  if (mode==='start'){ b.innerHTML=''; b.style.display='none'; return; }
  if (mode==='build'){
    b.innerHTML = '<button class="btn btn-ghost" id="bBack">Back</button><button class="btn btn-primary" id="bGen">Generate</button>';
    document.getElementById('bBack').onclick = backToPresets;
    document.getElementById('bGen').onclick = generateReport;
  } else if (mode==='report'){
    b.innerHTML = '<button class="btn btn-ghost" id="bEdit">Edit</button><button class="btn btn-primary" id="bPdf">Export PDF</button>';
    document.getElementById('bEdit').onclick = ()=>{
      document.getElementById('report').classList.remove('visible');
      document.getElementById('page-build').style.display='block';
      renderActionBar('build'); window.scrollTo(0,0);
    };
    document.getElementById('bPdf').onclick = function(){
      try{
        var cards = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#report .hazard-card'));
        var exc = cards.filter(function(c){ return c.classList.contains('excluded'); });
        var serialEl = document.querySelector('#report .kv-row .v');
        if(serialEl){
          var log = readLog();
          if(log.length && log[0].serial === serialEl.textContent.trim()){
            log[0].hazardsIncluded = cards.length - exc.length;
            log[0].hazardsExcluded = exc.length;
            log[0].exportedAt = new Date().toISOString();
            writeLog(log);
          }
        }
      }catch(e){}
      window.print();
    };
  } else { b.innerHTML=''; b.style.display='none'; }
}

function showPage(p){
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.page===p));
  var _rep = document.getElementById('report');
  const reportVisible = _rep.classList.contains('visible') && _rep.innerHTML.trim() !== '';
  document.getElementById('page-build').style.display = (p==='build' && !reportVisible)?'block':'none';
  document.getElementById('report').style.display = (p==='build')?'':'none';
  ['ai','register','admin'].forEach(x=>document.getElementById('page-'+x).style.display = (p===x)?'block':'none');
  if (p==='build') renderActionBar(reportVisible?'report':(document.getElementById('refineArea').style.display==='block'?'build':'start'));
  else renderActionBar('none');
  if (p==='register') renderReg(curTab);
  if (p==='ai') initChat();
}
document.getElementById('navbar').addEventListener('click', e=>{
  const b = e.target.closest('.nav-btn'); if (b) showPage(b.dataset.page);
});

// =====================================================================
// REGISTER
// =====================================================================
let curTab='activityHazards';
document.getElementById('regTabs').addEventListener('click', e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('.reg-tab'); if(!b) return;
  document.querySelectorAll('.reg-tab').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x===b));
  curTab=b.dataset.tab; document.getElementById('regSearch').value=''; renderReg(curTab);
});
document.getElementById('regSearch').addEventListener('input', e=>renderReg(curTab, e.target.value));

function renderReg(tab, f){
  f=(f||'').toLowerCase().trim();
  const el=document.getElementById('regContent'), cn=document.getElementById('regCount');
  el.innerHTML='';
  const src = DATA[tab]||[];
  const rows = src.filter(r=>!f||JSON.stringify(r).toLowerCase().includes(f));
  cn.textContent = rows.length+' of '+src.length;
  if(!rows.length){ el.innerHTML='<div class="empty-note">No matches.</div>'; return; }
  rows.slice(0,400).forEach(r=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='reg-item';
    if (tab==='activityHazards' || tab==='equipmentHazards'){
      d.innerHTML = '<div class="reg-title">'+esc(r.hz)+' '+badge(r.lv)+'</div><div class="reg-meta">'+
        '<b>'+esc(r.tag||r.et)+'</b> · '+esc(r.cat)+'<br>Controls: '+esc(r.ct)+
        '<br>Residual: '+esc(r.rd||'—')+' · PPE: '+esc(r.pp||'—')+
        '<br>Applies when: motion='+esc(r.mo)+', status='+esc(r.st)+'</div>';
    } else if (tab==='jobTags'){
      d.innerHTML='<div class="reg-title">'+esc(r.t)+'</div><div class="reg-meta">'+esc(r.c)+'</div>';
    } else if (tab==='equipmentTypes'){
      d.innerHTML='<div class="reg-title">'+esc(r)+'</div>';
    } else if (tab==='equipment'){
      var where = (r.scope && r.scope!=='Listed areas only') ? r.scope : ((r.locs||[]).join('; ') || '—');
      d.innerHTML='<div class="reg-title">'+esc(r.n)+'</div><div class="reg-meta">'+esc(r.t)+'<br>Found in: '+esc(where)+'</div>';
    } else if (tab==='locations'){
      var n = DATA.equipment.filter(function(e){ return equipAvailable(e, r.n); }).length;
      d.innerHTML='<div class="reg-title">'+esc(r.n)+'</div><div class="reg-meta">'+esc(r.type||'')+' · '+n+' equipment items available</div>';
    } else if (tab==='toolRecognition'){
      d.innerHTML='<div class="reg-title">'+esc(r.p)+'</div><div class="reg-meta">'+esc(r.c||'')+
        '</div><div class="chips" style="margin-top:6px;">'+(r.tags||[]).map(function(t){return '<span class="mini-chip">'+esc(t)+'</span>';}).join(' ')+'</div>';
    } else if (tab==='presets'){
      d.innerHTML='<div class="reg-title">'+esc(r.n)+'</div><div class="reg-meta">'+esc(r.d)+'<br><b>'+esc(r.et)+'</b> · '+esc(r.mo)+' · '+esc(r.st)+
        '</div><div class="chips" style="margin-top:6px;">'+r.tags.map(t=>'<span class="mini-chip">'+esc(t)+'</span>').join(' ')+'</div>';
    }
    el.appendChild(d);
  });
  if (rows.length>400) el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div class="empty-note">Showing first 400 — refine your search.</div>');
}

// =====================================================================
// AI FOLLOW-UP ENGINE
// A description is rarely complete on first pass. Rather than silently
// guessing, detect what is missing and ask for it. Tools matter because
// they carry their own hazards independent of the equipment worked on.
// =====================================================================

// tool phrase -> activity tags it implies
const TOOL_MAP_BUILTIN = {
  'angle grinder':['Grinding','Power Tool Use','Hot Work (General)'],
  'grinder':['Grinding','Power Tool Use'],
  'jack hammer':['Power Tool Use','Demolition / Breaking Concrete'],
  'jackhammer':['Power Tool Use','Demolition / Breaking Concrete'],
  'breaker':['Power Tool Use','Demolition / Breaking Concrete'],
  'welding machine':['Welding','Hot Work (General)','Fire Watch Duty'],
  'welder':['Welding','Hot Work (General)','Fire Watch Duty'],
  'welding set':['Welding','Hot Work (General)','Fire Watch Duty'],
  'cutting torch':['Cutting (Oxy-Acetylene)','Hot Work (General)','Fire Watch Duty'],
  'oxy':['Cutting (Oxy-Acetylene)','Hot Work (General)'],
  'gas axe':['Cutting (Oxy-Acetylene)','Hot Work (General)'],
  'blow torch':['Hot Work (General)'],
  'drill':['Power Tool Use','Drilling'],
  'drilling machine':['Power Tool Use','Drilling'],
  'hammer':['Hammering','Hand Tool Use'],
  'chisel':['Chiseling','Hand Tool Use'],
  'spanner':['Hand Tool Use'],
  'wrench':['Hand Tool Use'],
  'torque wrench':['Bolt Torqueing / Tensioning','Hand Tool Use'],
  'hydraulic torque':['Bolt Torqueing / Tensioning','Hydraulic System Work'],
  'tensioner':['Bolt Torqueing / Tensioning'],
  'hacksaw':['Sawing','Hand Tool Use'],
  'saw':['Sawing','Power Tool Use'],
  'shovel':['Shoveling','Manual Handling of Loads'],
  'spade':['Shoveling','Manual Handling of Loads'],
  'scraper':['Scraping','Hand Tool Use'],
  'wire brush':['Hand Tool Use'],
  'needle gun':['Power Tool Use','Painting / Surface Coating'],
  'chain block':['Use of Chain Block','Rigging/Slinging Loads'],
  'chain hoist':['Use of Chain Block','Rigging/Slinging Loads'],
  'come along':['Use of Chain Block'],
  'crane':['Crane Operation','Rigging/Slinging Loads','Signaling/Banksman Duties'],
  'overhead crane':['Use of Overhead Crane','Rigging/Slinging Loads'],
  'mobile crane':['Crane Operation','Rigging/Slinging Loads'],
  'sling':['Rigging/Slinging Loads'],
  'shackle':['Rigging/Slinging Loads'],
  'forklift':['Use of Forklift','Operating Mobile Equipment'],
  'ladder':['Ladder Use','Working at Height'],
  'scaffold':['Scaffold Use','Working at Height'],
  'harness':['Working at Height'],
  'man lift':['Man-lift/Aerial Platform Use','Working at Height'],
  'cherry picker':['Man-lift/Aerial Platform Use','Working at Height'],
  'scissor lift':['Man-lift/Aerial Platform Use','Working at Height'],
  'brokk':['Operating Mobile Equipment','Demolition / Breaking Concrete'],
  'excavator':['Operating Mobile Equipment','Excavation/Loading Operations'],
  'loader':['Operating Mobile Equipment'],
  'lathe':['Machining (Workshop Lathe/Mill)'],
  'milling machine':['Machining (Workshop Lathe/Mill)'],
  'pressure washer':['Pressure Washing','Wet Work/Slippery Surface'],
  'jetting':['Hydro-Jetting / High Pressure Cleaning'],
  'hydro jet':['Hydro-Jetting / High Pressure Cleaning'],
  'vacuum':['Housekeeping/Cleaning'],
  'air lance':['Pneumatic System Work'],
  'air hose':['Pneumatic System Work'],
  'compressor':['Pneumatic System Work'],
  'gunning machine':['Gunning / Shotcreting Refractory'],
  'grout pump':['High Pressure Grouting/Pumping'],
  'multimeter':['Instrument Loop Checking','Equipment Testing'],
  'megger':['De-energized Electrical Work','Equipment Testing'],
  'gas detector':['Gas Testing/Atmospheric Monitoring'],
  'thermal camera':['Thermographic Survey'],
  'vibration meter':['Vibration Analysis'],
  'rope':['Rope Access','Working at Height'],
  'winch':['Rigging/Slinging Loads'],
  'trolley':['Manual Handling of Loads'],
  'wheelbarrow':['Manual Handling of Loads'],
};

// Tool recognition is data-driven: the ToolRecognition sheet in the master
// workbook defines it, so HSE can add tools without touching code. The built-in
// map is only a fallback for older data files that predate that sheet.
function activeToolMap(){
  if(DATA.toolRecognition && DATA.toolRecognition.length){
    var m = {};
    DATA.toolRecognition.forEach(function(r){
      if(r.p) m[String(r.p).toLowerCase()] = (r.tags||[]).filter(Boolean);
    });
    if(Object.keys(m).length) return m;
  }
  return TOOL_MAP_BUILTIN;
}

function detectTools(text){
  const low = String(text||'').toLowerCase();
  const MAPX = activeToolMap();
  const tags = new Set(); const found = [];
  Object.keys(MAPX).forEach(function(k){
    if(low.indexOf(k) !== -1){
      found.push(k);
      MAPX[k].forEach(function(t){ tags.add(t); });
    }
  });
  return {tags: Array.from(tags), tools: found};
}

// What is still unknown, in the order worth asking
function missingFields(p){
  const m = [];
  if(!p.ref && !p.refOther) m.push('equipment');
  if(!p.loc && !p.locOther) m.push('location');
  if(!p.et  && !p.etOther)  m.push('etype');
  if(!p.mo)  m.push('motion');
  if(!p.st)  m.push('status');
  if(!p.toolsAsked) m.push('tools');
  if(!p.workArea) m.push('workarea');
  // the printed form cannot be issued without these, so collect them here too
  if(!p.jt)       m.push('jobtitle');
  if(!p.dept)     m.push('dept');
  if(!p.assessor) m.push('assessor');
  if(!p.approver) m.push('approver');
  return m;
}

const QUESTIONS = {
  equipment: 'Which <b>specific equipment</b> is this work on?',
  location:  'Which <b>area of the plant</b> is this in?',
  etype:     'What <b>type of equipment</b> is it?',
  locother:  'Please <b>name the area</b>.',
  motion:    'Will the equipment be <b>running or static</b> during the work?',
  status:    'Is the equipment <b>operational</b> or <b>shut down / isolated</b>?',
  tools:     'What <b>tools or equipment</b> will be used? (e.g. angle grinder, chain block, ladder, welding set) — tools carry their own hazards.',
  workarea:  'Where <b>exactly</b> will the work take place? (e.g. "kiln burner platform, level 2")',
  jobtitle:  'Give this job a short <b>title</b> for the assessment form (e.g. "Cement mill liner replacement").',
  dept:      'Which <b>department or section</b> owns this work? (e.g. Production, Mechanical Maintenance)',
  assessor:  'Who is <b>carrying out</b> this assessment? (full name)',
  approver:  'Who will <b>approve</b> it? (full name)'
};

// ---- location recognition: plant codes ("84A", "71-2") or distinctive name words ----
const LOC_GENERIC = new Set(['and','conveying','storage','station','system','plant','area','building',
  'loading','line','the','with','room','yard','house','additives','correctives']);
function detectLocation(text){
  const low = ' '+String(text||'').toLowerCase()+' ';
  const locs = (DATA.locations||[]).filter(function(l){ return l.n !== OTHER_LOC_NAME; });
  // 1) explicit plant code as a whole token
  for(var i=0;i<locs.length;i++){
    var code = (locs[i].code || locs[i].n.split(' ')[0]).toLowerCase();
    if(/^\d/.test(code) && new RegExp('(^|[^a-z0-9-])'+code.replace(/[-]/g,'\\-')+'([^a-z0-9-]|$)').test(low)) return [locs[i].n];
  }
  // 2) whole name
  var whole = locs.filter(function(l){
    var nm = l.n.replace(/^[0-9A-Z-]+\s/,'').toLowerCase();
    return low.indexOf(' '+nm) !== -1 || low.indexOf(nm+' ') !== -1;
  });
  if(whole.length) return whole.map(function(l){ return l.n; });
  // 3) distinctive words. A single word counts if it names at most 3 areas ("quarry",
  //    "packing", "clinker"); common words like "cement" need a second word to count.
  //    Ties are returned together so the user is asked to pick.
  var wordsOf = function(l){ return l.n.replace(/^[0-9A-Z-]+\s/,'').toLowerCase().split(/[^a-z0-9]+/)
                 .filter(function(w){ return w.length>=4 && !LOC_GENERIC.has(w); }); };
  var freq = {};
  locs.forEach(function(l){ new Set(wordsOf(l)).forEach(function(w){ freq[w]=(freq[w]||0)+1; }); });
  var best=0, hits=[];
  locs.forEach(function(l){
    var matched = wordsOf(l).filter(function(w){ return new RegExp('\\b'+w).test(low); });
    var sc = matched.length;
    var eligible = sc>=2 || matched.some(function(w){ return freq[w]<=3; });
    if(eligible){ if(sc>best){ best=sc; hits=[l.n]; } else if(sc===best) hits.push(l.n); }
  });
  return hits;
}
function locationsForEquipment(name){
  var e = DATA.equipment.find(function(x){ return x.n===name; });
  if(!e) return [];
  return (DATA.locations||[]).filter(function(l){ return l.n!==OTHER_LOC_NAME && equipAvailable(e,l.n); })
                             .map(function(l){ return l.n; });
}
function equipmentForLocation(loc){
  return DATA.equipment.filter(function(e){ return equipAvailable(e,loc); }).map(function(e){ return e.n; });
}

function chip(field, value, label){
  return '<button class="qchip" data-f="'+esc(field)+'" data-v="'+esc(value)+'" onclick="answerChip(this.dataset.f,this.dataset.v)">'+esc(label||value)+'</button>';
}
function chipRow(field, values, extra){
  return '<div class="quick-chips">'+values.map(function(v){ return chip(field,v); }).join('')+(extra||'')+'</div>';
}
function selectRow(field, values, placeholder){
  var h='<select class="chat-select" data-f="'+esc(field)+'" onchange="if(this.value) answerChip(this.dataset.f,this.value)">'+
        '<option value="">'+esc(placeholder)+'</option>';
  values.forEach(function(v){ h+='<option value="'+esc(v)+'">'+esc(v)+'</option>'; });
  return h+'<option value="'+OTHER+'">Other (type it)…</option></select>';
}

// =====================================================================
// FOLLOW-UP QUESTIONS — one card, not one bubble per question
// Every outstanding question is shown together. Anything answerable by
// tapping is answered in place; anything that has to be typed is numbered
// n/N so the user can see how many replies are still needed.
// =====================================================================
const Q_SHORT = {
  equipment:'the equipment name', location:'the area of the plant', etype:'the equipment type',
  tools:'the tools you will use', workarea:'the exact work area', jobtitle:'a short job title',
  dept:'the department', assessor:'the assessor’s name', approver:'the approver’s name'
};
const Q_LABEL = {
  equipment:'Equipment name', location:'Area of the plant', etype:'Equipment type',
  tools:'Tools to be used', workarea:'Exact work area', jobtitle:'Job title',
  dept:'Department', assessor:'Assessor', approver:'Approver'
};
const Q_OTHER_TEXT = {
  equipment:'Please <b>name the equipment</b> (e.g. its tag number).',
  location: 'Please <b>name the area</b>.',
  etype:    'Please <b>describe the equipment type</b>.'
};
let qcard = null;           // the live question card, so it updates instead of repeating
let chatPlaceholder = null; // the original placeholder, restored when nothing is outstanding

// Decide how one field is answered: by tapping, or by typing.
function buildQ(f){
  var q = {f:f, text:QUESTIONS[f], kind:'input', control:'', hint:''};
  var om = pending.otherMode || {};

  if(om[f]){                       // user chose "Other…" — now it has to be typed
    q.text = Q_OTHER_TEXT[f] || q.text;
    return q;
  }

  if(f === 'equipment'){
    var opts = pending.loc ? equipmentForLocation(pending.loc) : [];
    if(opts.length && opts.length <= 10){
      q.kind='select'; q.control = chipRow('equipment', opts, chip('equipment', OTHER, 'Other…'));
    } else if(opts.length){
      q.kind='select'; q.control = selectRow('equipment', opts, '— equipment in this area —');
    } else {
      q.hint = 'Type its name — e.g. Rotary Kiln, Cement Mill, Belt Conveyor.';
    }
  } else if(f === 'location'){
    var cands = pending.locCandidates && pending.locCandidates.length ? pending.locCandidates
              : (pending.ref ? locationsForEquipment(pending.ref) : []);
    q.kind='select';
    if(cands.length && cands.length <= 8) q.control = chipRow('location', cands, chip('location', OTHER, 'Other area…'));
    else q.control = selectRow('location', cands.length ? cands :
                    (DATA.locations||[]).filter(function(l){return l.n!==OTHER_LOC_NAME;}).map(function(l){return l.n;}),
                    '— select area —');
  } else if(f === 'etype'){
    var types = pending.loc ? Array.from(new Set(equipmentForLocation(pending.loc).map(function(n){
                  return DATA.equipment.find(function(e){return e.n===n;}).t; }))) : DATA.equipmentTypes.slice();
    q.kind='select'; q.control = selectRow('etype', types, '— equipment type —');
  } else if(f === 'motion'){
    q.kind='select'; q.control = chipRow('motion', ['Running','Static']);
  } else if(f === 'status'){
    q.kind='select';
    q.control = '<div class="quick-chips">'+chip('status','Operational')+chip('status','Shutdown/Isolated','Shut down / isolated')+'</div>';
  } else if(f === 'tools'){
    q.control = '<div class="quick-chips">'+chip('tools','__none__','No special tools')+'</div>';
  }
  return q;
}

// What has already been settled, so the card shows progress without a bubble per tap
function answeredChips(){
  var out = [];
  var add = function(l,v){ if(v) out.push('<span class="qtick">'+esc(l)+': '+esc(v)+'</span>'); };
  add('Area',      pending.loc || pending.locOther);
  add('Equipment', pending.ref || pending.refOther);
  add('Type',      pending.et  || pending.etOther);
  add('Motion',    pending.mo);
  add('Status',    pending.st);
  add('Work area', pending.workArea);
  add('Job title', pending.jt);
  add('Department',pending.dept);
  add('Assessor',  pending.assessor);
  add('Approver',  pending.approver);
  if(pending.toolsAsked && !(pending.toolsDetected||[]).length) out.push('<span class="qtick">Tools: none</span>');
  else if((pending.toolsDetected||[]).length) add('Tools', Array.from(new Set(pending.toolsDetected)).join(', '));
  return out;
}

function clearQCard(){ if(qcard && qcard.parentNode) qcard.parentNode.removeChild(qcard); qcard = null; }

function setChatPrompt(q, n, total){
  var el = document.getElementById('chatInput'); if(!el) return;
  if(chatPlaceholder === null) chatPlaceholder = el.placeholder;
  el.placeholder = q ? ('Answer '+n+'/'+total+' — '+(Q_SHORT[q.f]||'your answer')) : chatPlaceholder;
}

function askNext(){
  const miss = missingFields(pending);
  if(!miss.length){
    pending.awaiting = null; clearQCard(); setChatPrompt(null); showMatchSummary(); return;
  }
  const qs = miss.map(buildQ);
  const sels   = qs.filter(function(q){ return q.kind === 'select'; });
  const inputs = qs.filter(function(q){ return q.kind === 'input'; });

  // only the first typed question is live; the rest are queued behind it
  pending.awaiting = inputs.length ? inputs[0].f : null;
  setChatPrompt(inputs.length ? inputs[0] : null, 1, inputs.length);

  var h = '<div class="qcard-head">A few more details and this is ready to build.</div>';

  var done = answeredChips();
  if(done.length) h += '<div class="qrecap">'+done.join('')+'</div>';

  if(sels.length){
    h += '<div class="qgroup"><div class="qgroup-title"><span>'+
         (sels.length===1 ? 'Make a selection below' : 'Make a selection for each of these')+
         '</span><span class="qprog">'+sels.length+' to choose</span></div>';
    sels.forEach(function(q,ix){
      h += '<div class="qitem"><span class="qnum">'+(ix+1)+'/'+sels.length+'</span>'+
           '<span class="qtext">'+q.text+'</span>'+q.control+'</div>';
    });
    h += '</div>';
  }

  if(inputs.length){
    h += '<div class="qgroup"><div class="qgroup-title"><span>'+
         (inputs.length===1 ? 'Type your answer in the box below'
                            : 'Type these in the box below, one at a time')+
         '</span><span class="qprog">'+inputs.length+' to answer</span></div>';
    var live = inputs[0];
    h += '<div class="qitem active"><span class="qnum">1/'+inputs.length+'</span>'+
         '<span class="qtext">'+live.text+'</span><span class="qnow">answering now</span>'+
         (live.hint ? '<span class="mini-note">'+live.hint+'</span>' : '')+live.control+'</div>';
    if(inputs.length > 1){
      h += '<div class="qqueue">'+inputs.slice(1).map(function(q,ix){
        return '<span class="qmini"><span class="qnum">'+(ix+2)+'/'+inputs.length+'</span>'+
               esc(Q_LABEL[q.f] || 'Next question')+'</span>';
      }).join('')+'</div>';
    }
    h += '</div>';
  }

  // replace the previous card rather than stacking another question below it
  clearQCard();
  qcard = bubble('bot', h);
  qcard.classList.add('qcard');
}

function answerChip(field, value){
  if(value === OTHER){
    pending.otherMode = pending.otherMode || {};
    pending.otherMode[field] = true;
    askNext(); return;                     // the field becomes a typed question in the same card
  }
  if(pending.otherMode) delete pending.otherMode[field];
  if(field === 'motion'){ pending.mo = value; }
  else if(field === 'status'){ pending.st = value; }
  else if(field === 'tools'){ pending.toolsAsked = true; }
  else if(field === 'location'){ pending.loc = value; pending.locCandidates = null; }
  else if(field === 'etype'){ pending.et = value; }
  else if(field === 'equipment'){
    var e = DATA.equipment.find(function(x){ return x.n===value; });
    if(e){ pending.ref = e.n; pending.et = pending.et || e.t; }
  }
  askNext();
}

function matchEquipment(text){
  const low = text.toLowerCase();
  let best=null, bestScore=0;
  DATA.equipment.forEach(function(e){
    const n=e.n.toLowerCase();
    if(low.indexOf(n)!==-1){ best=e; bestScore=999; return; }
    const words=n.split(/[^a-z0-9]+/).filter(function(w){return w.length>=4;});
    let sc=0; words.forEach(function(w){ if(low.indexOf(w)!==-1) sc++; });
    var strong = (sc>=2) || (sc===1 && words.length===1);
    if(strong && sc>bestScore){ bestScore=sc; best=e; }
  });
  return best;
}

// Map a free-text reply to the outstanding question, then harvest anything else it contains
function applyAnswer(text){
  // only map a reply to a question that was actually asked - the opening description
  // is not an answer to anything, it is mined opportunistically below
  const f = pending.awaiting || null;
  const t = text.trim();
  const om = pending.otherMode || {};

  // "Other…" was tapped for this field, so whatever is typed is the value itself
  if(f && om[f]){
    if(f === 'equipment'){ pending.refOther = t; }
    else if(f === 'location'){ pending.locOther = t; pending.locCandidates = null; }
    else if(f === 'etype'){ pending.etOther = t; }
    delete om[f];
    pending.awaiting = null;
    return;
  }

  if(f === 'equipment_other'){ pending.refOther = t; }
  else if(f === 'location_other'){ pending.locOther = t; pending.locCandidates = null; }
  else if(f === 'etype_other'){ pending.etOther = t; }
  else if(f === 'equipment'){
    var m = matchEquipment(t);
    if(m){ pending.ref = m.n; pending.et = pending.et || m.t; }
    else pending.refOther = t;            // not in the register: keep what they said, don't guess
  }
  else if(f === 'location'){
    var ls = detectLocation(t);
    if(ls.length === 1){ pending.loc = ls[0]; pending.locCandidates = null; }
    else if(ls.length > 1){ pending.locCandidates = ls; }   // ambiguous: ask again with just these
    else pending.locOther = t;
  }
  else if(f === 'etype'){
    var ty = DATA.equipmentTypes.find(function(x){ return t.toLowerCase().indexOf(x.toLowerCase())!==-1; });
    if(ty) pending.et = ty; else pending.etOther = t;
  }
  else if(f === 'tools')    pending.toolsAsked = true;
  else if(f === 'workarea') pending.workArea = t;
  else if(f === 'jobtitle') pending.jt = t;
  else if(f === 'dept')     pending.dept = t;
  else if(f === 'assessor') pending.assessor = t;
  else if(f === 'approver') pending.approver = t;

  // opportunistic extraction, only ever filling blanks
  if(!pending.ref && !pending.refOther){
    var me = matchEquipment(t); if(me){ pending.ref = me.n; pending.et = pending.et || me.t; }
  }
  if(!pending.loc && !pending.locOther){
    var dl = detectLocation(t);
    if(dl.length === 1) pending.loc = dl[0];
    else if(dl.length > 1) pending.locCandidates = dl;
  }
  // equipment found in exactly one area settles the location
  if(pending.ref && !pending.loc && !pending.locOther){
    var only = locationsForEquipment(pending.ref);
    if(pending.locCandidates && pending.locCandidates.length){
      var inter = pending.locCandidates.filter(function(x){ return only.indexOf(x)!==-1; });
      if(inter.length === 1){ pending.loc = inter[0]; pending.locCandidates = null; }
      else if(inter.length > 1) pending.locCandidates = inter;
    } else if(only.length === 1) pending.loc = only[0];
  }
  if(!pending.mo && /\b(running|in operation|while running)\b/i.test(t)) pending.mo='Running';
  if(/shut\s*down|isolat|stopped|locked\s*out|outage/i.test(t)){ if(!pending.mo) pending.mo='Static'; if(!pending.st) pending.st='Shutdown/Isolated'; }
  if(!pending.st && /\boperational\b/i.test(t)) pending.st='Operational';
  pending.awaiting = null;
}

function showMatchSummary(){
  const chips = Array.from(pending.tags).map(function(t){
    return '<span class="mini-chip" data-t="'+esc(t)+'" onclick="togglePend(this)" style="cursor:pointer;background:var(--blue);color:#fff;">'+esc(t)+'</span>';
  }).join(' ') || '<span class="mini-note">No activities identified yet.</span>';
  let h = '<b>Ready to build:</b><br><br><b>Activities:</b><div class="chips" style="margin-top:5px;">'+chips+'</div><br>'+
    '<b>Location:</b> '+esc(pending.loc||pending.locOther||'—')+'<br>'+
    '<b>Specific work area:</b> '+esc(pending.workArea||'—')+'<br>'+
    '<b>Equipment:</b> '+esc(pending.ref||pending.refOther||'—')+
    ' &nbsp; <b>Type:</b> '+esc(pending.et||pending.etOther||'—')+'<br>'+
    '<b>Motion:</b> '+esc(pending.mo||'—')+' &nbsp; <b>Status:</b> '+esc(pending.st||'—')+'<br>';
  if(pending.toolsDetected && pending.toolsDetected.length){
    h += '<b>Tools recognised:</b> '+esc(Array.from(new Set(pending.toolsDetected)).join(', '))+'<br>';
  }
  h += '<b>Job title:</b> '+esc(pending.jt||'—')+'<br>'+
       '<b>Department:</b> '+esc(pending.dept||'—')+'<br>'+
       '<b>Assessed by:</b> '+esc(pending.assessor||'—')+' &nbsp; <b>Approved by:</b> '+esc(pending.approver||'—')+'<br>';
  if(pending.etOther) h += '<span class="mini-note">Unlisted equipment type — only activity hazards will be generated.</span>';
  h += '<br>Tap a chip to remove it, send more detail to refine, or build it.<br>'+
    '<button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top:8px;" onclick="applyPending()">Apply &amp; Build</button>'+
    '<span class="mini-note">Always review the generated assessment before export.</span>';
  bubble('bot', h);
}

// =====================================================================
// AI ASSIST
// =====================================================================
let chatInit=false, pending={tags:new Set(),et:null,mo:null,st:null,ref:null,jt:'',jd:'',dept:'',assessor:'',approver:'',toolsAsked:false,toolsDetected:[],awaiting:null,loc:'',locOther:'',locCandidates:null,etOther:'',refOther:'',workArea:'',otherMode:{}};
const getKey=()=>{try{return localStorage.getItem(AI_KEY)||''}catch(e){return''}};
function aiBadge(){ const b=document.getElementById('aiModeBadge');
  if(getKey()){b.textContent='AI-POWERED';b.classList.add('live');} else {b.textContent='BASIC';b.classList.remove('live');} }

function initChat(){
  if(chatInit) return; chatInit=true; aiBadge();
  document.getElementById('chatSend').onclick=sendChat;
  document.getElementById('chatInput').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();sendChat();} });
  bubble('bot','Describe the work you are about to do. For example: "We are entering the kiln to replace the brick lining during the shutdown." I will suggest activities and equipment settings, then you can apply them and generate the assessment.');
}
function bubble(role, html){ const s=document.getElementById('chatScroll'); const d=document.createElement('div');
  d.className='chat-bubble '+role; d.innerHTML=html; s.appendChild(d); s.scrollTop=s.scrollHeight; return d; }

const GENERIC=new Set(['work','working','worker','the','and','for','with','during','equipment','operation','operations','use','using','handling','material','materials','general','site','activity','activities','related','area','task','tasks','from','into','onto','near','over','under','while','this','that','will','need','needs','some','other','person','personnel','someone','team','job','around','we','our','are','going','have','has','been','their','they']);
const ALIAS={'confined space':'Confined Space Entry','enclosed space':'Confined Space Entry','height':'Working at Height','climb':'Climbing','ladder':'Ladder Use','scaffold':'Scaffold Use','weld':'Welding','torch':'Cutting (Oxy-Acetylene)','grind':'Grinding','spark':'Hot Work (General)','hot work':'Hot Work (General)','crane':'Crane Operation','rig':'Rigging/Slinging Loads','lifting':'Rigging/Slinging Loads','hoist':'Use of Chain Block','lockout':'Lockout-Tagout Application','loto':'Lockout-Tagout Application','isolat':'Equipment Isolation','de-energ':'De-energized Electrical Work','inspect':'Visual Inspection','check':'Visual Inspection','clean':'Housekeeping/Cleaning','sweep':'Sweeping','wash':'Pressure Washing','drill':'Drilling (Quarry Blast Holes)','blast':'Blasting Operations','explos':'Explosives Handling/Storage','lab':'Sample Preparation (Laboratory)','sample':'Sampling','chemical':'Handling Hazardous Chemicals','reagent':'Chemical/Reagent Handling (Laboratory)','acid':'Handling Corrosive Substances','electrical':'De-energized Electrical Work','live wire':'Live Electrical Work','panel':'Electrical Panel Access','fuse':'Electrical Panel Access','cable':'Cable Pulling','vehicle':'Vehicle Driving','truck':'Vehicle Driving','drive':'Vehicle Driving','forklift':'Use of Forklift','loader':'Operating Mobile Equipment','excavat':'Excavation/Loading Operations','dig':'Excavation/Loading Operations','trench':'Working in Trench/Excavation','manual handling':'Manual Handling of Loads','carry':'Carrying','maintenance':'Mechanical Maintenance/Repair','repair':'Mechanical Maintenance/Repair','fix':'Mechanical Maintenance/Repair','replace':'Mechanical Maintenance/Repair','brick':'Refractory Installation','refractory':'Refractory Installation','lining':'Refractory Installation','fire watch':'Fire Watch Duty','fuel':'Fuel/Oil Transfer','oil':'Fuel/Oil Transfer','diesel':'Fuel/Oil Transfer','tanker':'Bulk Tanker/Wagon Loading','bagging':'Bulk Tanker/Wagon Loading','lathe':'Machining (Workshop Lathe/Mill)','stacker':'Operating Stacker/Reclaimer','reclaimer':'Operating Stacker/Reclaimer','dust':'Dust-Generating Activity','dusty':'Working in Dusty Environment','noise':'Working in Noisy Environment','noisy':'Working in Noisy Environment','heat':'Working in Extreme Heat','hot':'Working in Extreme Heat','wet':'Wet Work/Slippery Surface','slippery':'Wet Work/Slippery Surface','alone':'Working Alone/Isolated','night':'Night Shift Work','gas test':'Gas Testing/Atmospheric Monitoring','calibrat':'Calibration','bolt':'Hand Tool Use','spanner':'Hand Tool Use','hydraulic':'Hydraulic System Work','compressed air':'Pneumatic System Work','paint':'Spray Painting','concrete':'Concrete Pouring','cast':'Formwork/Casting','grout':'High Pressure Grouting/Pumping','barricad':'Barricading/Signage Setup','first aid':'First Aid Response Standby'};

// Concept inference: implied activities that share no vocabulary with the text.
// e.g. "enter the kiln" implies confined space entry + gas testing + isolation.
const INFER_RULES = [
  {re:/\b(enter|entering|entry|inside|internal|go into|get into|climb into|within)/i, needsVessel:true,
   add:['Confined Space Entry','Gas Testing/Atmospheric Monitoring','Lockout-Tagout Application']},
  {re:/\b(kiln|silo|vessel|tank|cyclone|preheater|calciner|mill|hopper|bunker|duct|casing|chamber)\b/i, withEnter:true,
   add:['Confined Space Entry']},
  {re:/\b(shut ?down|outage|isolat|locked? out|de-?energi[sz]ed|stopped)\b/i,
   add:['Lockout-Tagout Application','Equipment Isolation']},
  {re:/\b(tower|platform|gantry|roof|elevated|upper level|top of|scaffold|gallery)\b/i,
   add:['Working at Height','Climbing']},
  {re:/\b(brick|refractory|lining|castable|gunning)\b/i,
   add:['Refractory Installation','Manual Handling of Loads','Working in Extreme Heat']},
  {re:/\b(hot|heat|thermal|residual heat|cool ?down|850|1400)\b/i,
   add:['Working in Extreme Heat']},
  {re:/\b(dust|dusty|powder|meal|fines)\b/i,
   add:['Dust-Generating Activity','Working in Dusty Environment']},
  {re:/\b(weld|cut|grind|torch|burn|spark|flame)\b/i,
   add:['Hot Work (General)','Fire Watch Duty']},
  {re:/\b(lift|crane|hoist|sling|rig|chain block)\b/i,
   add:['Rigging/Slinging Loads','Signaling/Banksman Duties']},
  {re:/\b(heavy|carry|move|haul|shift)\b/i,
   add:['Manual Handling of Loads']},
  {re:/\b(clean|clear|remove buildup|blockage|blocked|choke|jam)\b/i,
   add:['Housekeeping/Cleaning','Hand Tool Use']},
  {re:/\b(electric|cable|panel|motor|switchgear|mcc|transformer|fuse|wiring)\b/i,
   add:['De-energized Electrical Work','Electrical Panel Access','Lockout-Tagout Application']},
  {re:/\b(pump|bearing|gearbox|coupling|seal|valve|replace|repair|overhaul|service)\b/i,
   add:['Mechanical Maintenance/Repair','Hand Tool Use']},
  {re:/\b(truck|vehicle|loader|excavator|forklift|tipper|drive|haul)\b/i,
   add:['Operating Mobile Equipment','Vehicle Driving']},
  {re:/\b(night|dark|evening shift)\b/i, add:['Night Shift Work']},
  {re:/\b(alone|single person|by myself|one man)\b/i, add:['Working Alone/Isolated']},
  {re:/\b(noise|noisy|loud)\b/i, add:['Working in Noisy Environment']},
  {re:/\b(chemical|acid|caustic|reagent|solvent)\b/i, add:['Handling Hazardous Chemicals']},
  {re:/\b(fuel|diesel|oil|lubricant|grease)\b/i, add:['Fuel/Oil Transfer']},
  {re:/\b(confined|restricted|tight space|manhole)\b/i,
   add:['Confined Space Entry','Gas Testing/Atmospheric Monitoring']},
];
const VESSEL_RE = /\b(kiln|silo|vessel|tank|cyclone|preheater|calciner|mill|hopper|bunker|duct|casing|chamber|precipitator|filter)\b/i;
function inferTags(text, valid){
  const found = new Set();
  const hasVessel = VESSEL_RE.test(text);
  const hasEnter = /\b(enter|entering|entry|inside|internal|go into|get into|climb into|within)/i.test(text);
  INFER_RULES.forEach(function(r){
    if(!r.re.test(text)) return;
    if(r.needsVessel && !hasVessel) return;
    if(r.withEnter && !hasEnter) return;
    r.add.forEach(function(t){ if(valid.has(t)) found.add(t); });
  });
  return found;
}

function stem(w){ w=w.toLowerCase();
  if(w.length>6&&w.slice(-3)==='ing')return w.slice(0,-3);
  if(w.length>6&&w.slice(-4)==='tion')return w.slice(0,-4);
  if(w.length>5&&w.slice(-2)==='ed')return w.slice(0,-2);
  if(w.length>4&&w.slice(-1)==='s'&&w.slice(-2)!=='ss')return w.slice(0,-1); return w; }
function toks(t){ return t.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g,' ').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); }

function localMatch(text){
  const low=text.toLowerCase(); const st=new Set(toks(text).map(stem));
  const scored=[];
  DATA.jobTags.forEach(t=>{
    let s=0; const tl=t.t.toLowerCase().replace(/\s*\([^)]*\)/g,'').trim();
    if(tl&&low.includes(tl)) s+=10;
    t.t.replace(/\([^)]*\)/g,'').toLowerCase().split(/[^a-z0-9]+/)
      .filter(w=>w.length>=3&&!GENERIC.has(w)).map(stem)
      .filter(w=>w.length>=3&&!GENERIC.has(w))
      .forEach(w=>{ if(st.has(w)) s+=3; });
    Object.keys(ALIAS).forEach(a=>{ if(ALIAS[a]===t.t && low.includes(a)) s+=8; });
    if(s>=3) scored.push({t:t.t,s:s});
  });
  scored.sort((a,b)=>b.s-a.s);
  const tagSet=new Set(scored.slice(0,12).map(x=>x.t));
  const validTags=new Set(DATA.jobTags.map(t=>t.t));
  inferTags(text, validTags).forEach(t=>tagSet.add(t));
  const tags=Array.from(tagSet);

  let et=null,ref=null,best=0;
  DATA.equipment.forEach(e=>{
    const nl=e.n.toLowerCase();
    if(low.includes(nl)){ ref=e.n; et=e.t; best=999; return; }
    const ws=nl.split(/[^a-z0-9]+/).filter(w=>w.length>=4&&!GENERIC.has(w));
    let s=0; ws.forEach(w=>{ if(low.includes(w)) s++; });
    // one shared word is too weak ("chain block" must not match "Drag Chain Conveyor")
    const strong = (s>=2) || (s===1 && ws.length===1);
    if(strong && s>best){ best=s; ref=e.n; et=e.t; }
  });
  if(!et) DATA.equipmentTypes.forEach(t=>{ if(low.includes(t.toLowerCase())) et=t; });

  let mo=null,stt=null;
  if(/\b(running|operating|in operation)\b/i.test(text)){ mo='Running'; stt='Operational'; }
  if(/shut\s*down|isolat|stopped|locked\s*out|de-?energi[sz]ed|outage/i.test(text)){ mo='Static'; stt='Shutdown/Isolated'; }
  return {tags:tags,et:et,mo:mo,st:stt,ref:ref,jt:'',jd:text};
}

async function aiMatch(text){
  const key=getKey();
  const tn=DATA.jobTags.map(t=>t.t), en=DATA.equipmentTypes, rn=DATA.equipment.map(e=>e.n);
  const ln=(DATA.locations||[]).map(l=>l.n).filter(n=>n!==OTHER_LOC_NAME);
  const sys='You extract structured data for a cement plant risk assessment tool. Respond with ONLY a JSON object, no markdown fences, with keys: {"tags":[],"equipmentType":null,"equipmentMotion":null,"equipmentStatus":null,"equipmentRef":null,"location":null,"jobType":"","description":""}. '+
    '"location" ONLY an exact string from: '+JSON.stringify(ln)+' or null. '+
    '"tags" must contain ONLY exact strings from: '+JSON.stringify(tn)+'. '+
    '"equipmentType" ONLY an exact string from: '+JSON.stringify(en)+' or null. '+
    '"equipmentRef" ONLY an exact string from: '+JSON.stringify(rn)+' or null. '+
    '"equipmentMotion" exactly "Running", "Static", or null. "equipmentStatus" exactly "Operational", "Shutdown/Isolated", or null. '+
    'Think about what the work physically involves and include implied activities (e.g. entering a vessel implies confined space entry; work on a tower implies working at height). Also infer activities implied by any TOOLS mentioned (angle grinder implies grinding and hot work; chain block implies rigging; ladder implies working at height). Select 4-12 relevant tags. Never invent values outside the lists.';
  const r=await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages',{method:'POST',
    headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','x-api-key':key,'anthropic-version':'2023-06-01','anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access':'true'},
    body:JSON.stringify({model:'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001',max_tokens:800,system:sys,messages:[{role:'user',content:text}]})});
  if(!r.ok) throw new Error('API '+r.status+': '+(await r.text()).slice(0,160));
  const d=await r.json();
  const out=(d.content||[]).map(c=>c.text||'').join('');
  const m=out.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/); if(!m) throw new Error('Could not parse AI response');
  const p=JSON.parse(m[0]);
  return { tags:(p.tags||[]).filter(t=>tn.indexOf(t)!==-1),
    et:en.indexOf(p.equipmentType)!==-1?p.equipmentType:null,
    mo:['Running','Static'].indexOf(p.equipmentMotion)!==-1?p.equipmentMotion:null,
    st:['Operational','Shutdown/Isolated'].indexOf(p.equipmentStatus)!==-1?p.equipmentStatus:null,
    ref:rn.indexOf(p.equipmentRef)!==-1?p.equipmentRef:null,
    loc:ln.indexOf(p.location)!==-1?p.location:null,
    jt:'', jd:p.description||text };
}

async function sendChat(){
  const i=document.getElementById('chatInput'); const t=i.value.trim(); if(!t) return;
  bubble('user', esc(t)); i.value='';
  const useAi=!!getKey(); const think=bubble('bot', useAi?'Thinking…':'Matching…');
  let r;
  try { r = useAi ? await aiMatch(t) : localMatch(t); }
  catch(err){ think.innerHTML='AI matching failed ('+esc(err.message)+'). Using basic matching instead.<span class="mini-note">Check your key in Settings.</span>';
    showMatch(localMatch(t), false); return; }
  think.remove();

  // merge this turn's findings into the running picture
  r.tags.forEach(function(x){ pending.tags.add(x); });
  // only fill blanks - a later message about tools must not hijack the equipment
  ['et','mo','st','ref','jd'].forEach(function(k){ if(r[k] && !pending[k]) pending[k]=r[k]; });
  if(r.loc && !pending.loc && !pending.locOther) pending.loc = r.loc;

  // map the reply to the outstanding question FIRST, before any flags change
  applyAnswer(t);

  // tools mentioned anywhere in the message imply their own hazards
  var td = detectTools(t);
  if(td.tags.length){
    td.tags.forEach(function(x){
      if(DATA.jobTags.some(function(j){ return j.t===x; })) pending.tags.add(x);
    });
    pending.toolsDetected = (pending.toolsDetected||[]).concat(td.tools);
    pending.toolsAsked = true;
  }
  askNext();
}

function showMatch(r, wasAi){
  r.tags.forEach(t=>pending.tags.add(t));
  ['et','mo','st','ref','jt','jd'].forEach(k=>{ if(r[k]) pending[k]=r[k]; });
  const chips = Array.from(pending.tags).map(t=>'<span class="mini-chip" data-t="'+esc(t)+'" onclick="togglePend(this)" style="cursor:pointer;background:var(--blue);color:#fff;">'+esc(t)+'</span>').join(' ')
    || '<span class="mini-note">No activities identified — try naming the specific work.</span>';
  let h='<b>Suggested setup:</b><br><br><b>Activities:</b><div class="chips" style="margin-top:5px;">'+chips+'</div><br>'+
    '<b>Equipment Type:</b> '+esc(pending.et||'—')+'<br><b>Motion:</b> '+esc(pending.mo||'—')+
    '<br><b>Status:</b> '+esc(pending.st||'—')+'<br><b>Equipment:</b> '+esc(pending.ref||'—')+'<br><br>'+
    'Tap a chip to remove it, send more detail to refine, or apply.<br>'+
    '<button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top:8px;" onclick="applyPending()">Apply &amp; Build</button>'+
    '<span class="mini-note">'+(wasAi?'AI-powered match':'Basic keyword match')+' — always review before generating.</span>';
  bubble('bot', h);
}
function togglePend(el){ const t=el.dataset.t; pending.tags.delete(t); el.remove(); }
function applyPending(){
  noteMethod('AI assist');
  discardReport();                                   // replacing, not appending to, any earlier result
  selectedTags.clear(); pending.tags.forEach(t=>selectedTags.add(t));
  setEquipmentContext({loc: pending.loc||'', et: pending.et||'', ref: pending.ref||'',
                       mo: pending.mo, st: pending.st, workArea: pending.workArea||''});
  if(pending.locOther){ document.getElementById('locArea').value=OTHER; _showOther('locOther',true);
                        document.getElementById('locOther').value=pending.locOther; refreshTypes(); }
  if(pending.etOther){ document.getElementById('equipType').value=OTHER; _showOther('equipTypeOther',true);
                       document.getElementById('equipTypeOther').value=pending.etOther;
                       document.getElementById('typeNote').classList.add('show'); refreshEquip(); }
  if(pending.refOther){ document.getElementById('equipRef').value=OTHER; _showOther('equipRefOther',true);
                        document.getElementById('equipRefOther').value=pending.refOther; }
  if(pending.jt)       document.getElementById('jobType').value      = pending.jt;
  if(pending.jd)       document.getElementById('jobDesc').value      = pending.jd;
  if(pending.dept)     document.getElementById('deptName').value     = pending.dept;
  if(pending.assessor) document.getElementById('assessorName').value = pending.assessor;
  if(pending.approver) document.getElementById('approverName').value = pending.approver;
  showPage('build'); openRefine();
}


// The master workbook stores PPE across PPE1..PPE7 and preset activities across
// JobTag1..JobTag10 so each cell can be dropdown-controlled. Recombine them here
// into the single-field format the app uses. Older single-column files still work.
function joinPPE(r){
  if(r.RequiredPPE) return String(r.RequiredPPE);
  var out=[];
  for(var i=1;i<=12;i++){
    var v=r['PPE'+i];
    if(v!==undefined && v!==null && String(v).trim()) out.push(String(v).trim());
  }
  return out.join(', ');
}
function joinTags(r){
  if(r.JobTags) return String(r.JobTags).split('|').filter(Boolean);
  var out=[];
  for(var i=1;i<=20;i++){
    var v=r['JobTag'+i];
    if(v!==undefined && v!==null && String(v).trim()) out.push(String(v).trim());
  }
  return out;
}

// ADMIN
