document.getElementById('aiApiKey').value=getKey();
document.getElementById('aiKeySave').onclick=function(){
  try{localStorage.setItem(AI_KEY,document.getElementById('aiApiKey').value.trim())}catch(e){}
  aiBadge(); status('success', getKey()?'API key saved — AI Assist will use AI-powered matching.':'Key cleared — AI Assist stays on basic matching.');
};
document.getElementById('aiKeyClear').onclick=function(){
  try{localStorage.removeItem(AI_KEY)}catch(e){}
  document.getElementById('aiApiKey').value=''; aiBadge(); status('success','API key cleared. AI Assist back to basic offline matching.');
};
document.getElementById('gateSubmit').onclick=unlock;
document.getElementById('gatePassword').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')unlock();});

let unlockBusy=false;
async function unlock(){
  if(unlockBusy) return;
  const btn=document.getElementById('gateSubmit');
  const err=document.getElementById('gateError');
  const pw=document.getElementById('gatePassword').value;
  if(!pw){ err.textContent='Enter the admin password.'; err.style.display='block'; return; }
  unlockBusy=true; btn.textContent='Checking…'; err.style.display='none';
  let ok=false;
  try{ ok = await verifyPassword(pw); }catch(e){ ok=false; }
  unlockBusy=false; btn.textContent='Unlock';
  if(ok){
    document.getElementById('gateBox').style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('adminSection').classList.add('unlocked');
    document.getElementById('gatePassword').value='';
    summary(); renderLog();
  } else {
    err.textContent='Incorrect password.'; err.style.display='block';
  }
}

// ---- change the admin password on this device ----
const pwChangeBtn = document.getElementById('pwChangeBtn');
if(pwChangeBtn){
  pwChangeBtn.onclick = async function(){
    const a=document.getElementById('pwNew').value, b=document.getElementById('pwNew2').value;
    const st=function(t,m){ var e=document.getElementById('pwStatus'); e.className='status-msg '+t; e.textContent=m; };
    if(a.length<8){ st('error','Use at least 8 characters.'); return; }
    if(a!==b){ st('error','The two entries do not match.'); return; }
    pwChangeBtn.textContent='Saving…';
    try{
      const salt=randomSaltHex();
      const hash=await hashPassword(a, salt, PBKDF2_ITERS);
      localStorage.setItem(CRED_STORE, JSON.stringify({salt:salt, hash:hash, iters:PBKDF2_ITERS}));
      document.getElementById('pwNew').value=''; document.getElementById('pwNew2').value='';
      st('success','Admin password updated on this device.');
    }catch(e){ st('error','Could not update password: '+e.message); }
    pwChangeBtn.textContent='Change password';
  };
}
const pwResetBtn = document.getElementById('pwResetBtn');
if(pwResetBtn){
  pwResetBtn.onclick = function(){
    if(!window.confirm('Revert to the password built into this file?')) return;
    try{ localStorage.removeItem(CRED_STORE); }catch(e){}
    var e=document.getElementById('pwStatus'); e.className='status-msg success';
    e.textContent='Reverted to the built-in password.';
  };
}
function summary(){
  let custom=false; try{custom=!!localStorage.getItem(STORE)}catch(e){}
  document.getElementById('currentDataSummary').innerHTML='Loaded ('+(custom?'custom upload':'built-in')+'):<br>'+
    '<b>'+DATA.jobTags.length+'</b> activities &nbsp; <b>'+(DATA.locations||[]).length+'</b> locations &nbsp; <b>'+DATA.equipmentTypes.length+'</b> equipment types &nbsp; <b>'+DATA.equipment.length+'</b> equipment<br>'+
    '<b>'+DATA.activityHazards.length+'</b> activity hazards &nbsp; <b>'+DATA.equipmentHazards.length+'</b> equipment hazards<br><b>'+DATA.presets.length+'</b> presets &nbsp; <b>'+((DATA.toolRecognition||[]).length)+'</b> tool mappings';
}
function status(t,m){ const e=document.getElementById('uploadStatus'); e.className='status-msg '+t; e.textContent=m; }

document.getElementById('resetDataBtn').onclick=()=>{ try{localStorage.removeItem(STORE)}catch(e){}
  DATA=BUNDLED; reinit(); status('success','Reset to built-in data.'); summary(); };

document.getElementById('fileInput').addEventListener('change',function(e){
  const f=e.target.files[0]; if(!f) return;
  const rd=new FileReader();
  rd.onload=function(ev){
    try{
      const wb=XLSX.read(new Uint8Array(ev.target.result),{type:'array'});
      const need=['JobTags','EquipmentTypes','Equipment','ActivityHazards','EquipmentHazards'];
      const miss=need.filter(n=>wb.SheetNames.indexOf(n)===-1);
      if(miss.length) throw new Error('Missing sheet(s): '+miss.join(', '));
      const J=n=>XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets[n],{defval:''});
      const nd={
        jobTags:J('JobTags').filter(r=>r.JobTag).map(r=>({t:String(r.JobTag),c:String(r.Category||'Other')})),
        equipmentTypes:J('EquipmentTypes').map(r=>String(r.EquipmentType)).filter(Boolean),
        equipment:J('Equipment').filter(r=>r.EquipmentName).map(function(r){
          var ls=[]; for(var i=1;i<=20;i++){ var v=r['LocationArea'+i]; if(v&&String(v).trim()) ls.push(String(v).trim()); }
          // older files carry no location mapping: show the item everywhere rather than hide it
          var scope = r.LocationScope ? String(r.LocationScope) : 'All areas';
          return {n:String(r.EquipmentName), t:String(r.EquipmentType||''), scope:scope, locs:ls};
        }),
        locations: wb.SheetNames.indexOf('Locations')!==-1
          ? J('Locations').filter(r=>r.LocationArea).map(r=>({n:String(r.LocationArea), type:String(r.AreaType||'Other'), code:String(r.LocationCode||'')}))
          : (BUNDLED.locations||[]),
        activityHazards:J('ActivityHazards').filter(r=>r.JobTag&&r.Hazard).map(r=>({tag:String(r.JobTag),cat:String(r.HazardCategory||''),hz:String(r.Hazard),lv:String(r.LevelOfRisk||''),ct:String(r.ControlMeasures||''),rd:String(r.ReducedLevelOfRisk||''),rp:String(r.ResponsiblePerson||''),pp:joinPPE(r),mo:String(r.AppliesWhenMotion||'Any'),st:String(r.AppliesWhenStatus||'Any')})),
        equipmentHazards:J('EquipmentHazards').filter(r=>r.EquipmentType&&r.Hazard).map(r=>({et:String(r.EquipmentType),cat:String(r.HazardCategory||''),hz:String(r.Hazard),lv:String(r.LevelOfRisk||''),ct:String(r.ControlMeasures||''),rd:String(r.ReducedLevelOfRisk||''),rp:String(r.ResponsiblePerson||''),pp:joinPPE(r),mo:String(r.AppliesWhenMotion||'Any'),st:String(r.AppliesWhenStatus||'Any')})),
        toolRecognition: wb.SheetNames.indexOf('ToolRecognition')!==-1 ? J('ToolRecognition').filter(r=>r.ToolPhrase).map(function(r){
          var tg=[]; for(var i=1;i<=8;i++){ var v=r['JobTag'+i]; if(v&&String(v).trim()) tg.push(String(v).trim()); }
          return {p:String(r.ToolPhrase), c:String(r.ToolCategory||''), tags:tg};
        }) : [],
        presets: wb.SheetNames.indexOf('TaskPresets')!==-1 ? J('TaskPresets').filter(r=>r.PresetName).map(r=>({n:String(r.PresetName),cat:String(r.PresetCategory||'Other'),d:String(r.Description||''),tags:joinTags(r),et:String(r.EquipmentType||''),mo:String(r.EquipmentMotion||'Static'),st:String(r.EquipmentStatus||'Operational'),ref:String(r.EquipmentRef||''),loc:String(r.LocationArea||'')})) : []
      };
      if(!nd.activityHazards.length&&!nd.equipmentHazards.length) throw new Error('No hazard rows found.');
      DATA=nd; persist(DATA); reinit();
      status('success','Loaded: '+nd.activityHazards.length+' activity + '+nd.equipmentHazards.length+' equipment hazards, '+nd.jobTags.length+' activities, '+nd.presets.length+' presets.');
      summary();
    }catch(err){ status('error','Could not read that file: '+err.message); }
    e.target.value='';
  };
  rd.readAsArrayBuffer(f);
});

document.getElementById('downloadBtn').onclick=()=>{
  try{
    const wb=XLSX.utils.book_new();
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(DATA.jobTags.map((t,i)=>({JobTagID:'TAG'+String(i+1).padStart(3,'0'),JobTag:t.t,Category:t.c}))),'JobTags');
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(DATA.equipmentTypes.map((t,i)=>({EquipmentTypeID:'ET'+String(i+1).padStart(2,'0'),EquipmentType:t}))),'EquipmentTypes');
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet((DATA.locations||[]).map(function(l,i){
      return {LocationID:'LOC'+String(i+1).padStart(2,'0'), LocationCode:l.code||'', LocationArea:l.n, AreaType:l.type||'Other'};
    })),'Locations');
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(DATA.equipment.map(function(e,i){
      var o={EquipmentID:'EQ'+String(i+1).padStart(2,'0'),EquipmentName:e.n,EquipmentType:e.t,LocationScope:e.scope||'All areas'};
      for(var k=0;k<10;k++) o['LocationArea'+(k+1)] = (e.locs||[])[k]||'';
      return o;
    })),'Equipment');
    var splitPPE=function(o,pp){ var a=String(pp||'').split(',').map(function(s){return s.trim();}).filter(Boolean);
      for(var i=0;i<7;i++) o['PPE'+(i+1)] = a[i]||''; return o; };
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(DATA.activityHazards.map((h,i)=>splitPPE({ActivityHazardID:'AH'+String(i+1).padStart(3,'0'),JobTag:h.tag,HazardCategory:h.cat,Hazard:h.hz,LevelOfRisk:h.lv,ControlMeasures:h.ct,ReducedLevelOfRisk:h.rd,ResponsiblePerson:h.rp,AppliesWhenMotion:h.mo,AppliesWhenStatus:h.st},h.pp))),'ActivityHazards');
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(DATA.equipmentHazards.map((h,i)=>splitPPE({EquipmentHazardID:'EH'+String(i+1).padStart(3,'0'),EquipmentType:h.et,HazardCategory:h.cat,Hazard:h.hz,LevelOfRisk:h.lv,ControlMeasures:h.ct,ReducedLevelOfRisk:h.rd,ResponsiblePerson:h.rp,AppliesWhenMotion:h.mo,AppliesWhenStatus:h.st},h.pp))),'EquipmentHazards');
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(DATA.presets.map(function(p,i){
      var o={PresetID:'P'+String(i+1).padStart(2,'0'),PresetName:p.n,PresetCategory:p.cat||'Other',Description:p.d};
      for(var k=0;k<10;k++) o['JobTag'+(k+1)] = p.tags[k]||'';
      o.EquipmentType=p.et; o.EquipmentMotion=p.mo; o.EquipmentStatus=p.st; o.EquipmentRef=p.ref; o.LocationArea=p.loc||''; return o;
    })),'TaskPresets');
    if(DATA.toolRecognition && DATA.toolRecognition.length){
      XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(DATA.toolRecognition.map(function(t,i){
        var o={ToolID:'T'+String(i+1).padStart(3,'0'), ToolPhrase:t.p, ToolCategory:t.c||''};
        for(var k=0;k<5;k++) o['JobTag'+(k+1)] = (t.tags||[])[k] || '';
        return o;
      })), 'ToolRecognition');
    }
    const out=XLSX.write(wb,{type:'array',bookType:'xlsx'});
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([out],{type:'application/octet-stream'}));
    const a=document.createElement('a'); a.href=url;
    a.download='OKPELLA_Master_Workbook_'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+'.xlsx';
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    status('success','Master workbook downloaded.');
  }catch(err){ status('error','Could not generate workbook: '+err.message); }
};

// =====================================================================
// INIT
// =====================================================================
function reinit(){
  selectedTags.clear(); populateEquip(); renderPresets('');
  if(document.getElementById('refineArea').style.display==='block'){ renderChips(); renderAccordion(''); updateCount(); }
  if(document.getElementById('page-register').style.display==='block') renderReg(curTab);
}
['workArea','locOther','equipTypeOther','equipRefOther'].forEach(function(id){
  var el=document.getElementById(id);
  if(el) el.addEventListener('input', function(){ var w=el.closest('.field'); if(w) w.classList.remove('field-invalid'); });
});
['jobType','jobDesc','deptName','assessorName','approverName'].forEach(function(id){
  var el=document.getElementById(id);
  if(el) el.addEventListener('input', function(){
    var w=el.closest('.field'); if(w) w.classList.remove('field-invalid');
    var anyLeft = REQUIRED_JOB_FIELDS.some(function(f){
      var e2=document.getElementById(f[0]); return !e2 || !e2.value.trim(); });
    if(!anyLeft){ var b=document.getElementById('valBanner'); if(b) b.classList.remove('show'); }
  });
});
document.getElementById('presetSearch').addEventListener('input',e=>renderPresets(e.target.value));
document.getElementById('tagSearch').addEventListener('input',e=>renderAccordion(e.target.value));
document.getElementById('skipPresets').addEventListener('click',function(){
  noteMethod('From scratch');
  selectedTags.clear();
  ['jobType','jobDesc','deptName'].forEach(function(id){var el=document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.value='';});
  setEquipmentContext({workArea:''});
  openRefine();
});
populateEquip(); renderPresets(''); renderActionBar('start');

// =====================================================================
// ADMIN: JSON BACKUP / RESTORE + ASSESSMENT LOG
// =====================================================================
const SETTINGS_STORE = 'okpella_settings';
function readSettings(){ try{ var r=localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_STORE); return r?JSON.parse(r):{}; }catch(e){ return {}; } }
function writeSettings(s){ try{ localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_STORE, JSON.stringify(s)); }catch(e){} }

function downloadBlob(content, filename, mime){
  var url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([content], {type: mime || 'application/octet-stream'}));
  var a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}
function jstatus(t,m){ var e=document.getElementById('jsonStatus'); e.className='status-msg '+t; e.textContent=m; }
function lstatus(t,m){ var e=document.getElementById('logStatus'); e.className='status-msg '+t; e.textContent=m; }

// ---- JSON export (API key deliberately excluded) ----
document.getElementById('jsonExportBtn').onclick = function(){
  try{
    var pkg = {
      _format: 'okpella-risk-assessment-config',
      _version: 3,
      _exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      _note: 'The LLM API key is intentionally NOT included in this export, so the file can be shared safely.',
      data: DATA,
      settings: readSettings()
    };
    downloadBlob(JSON.stringify(pkg, null, 2),
      'OKPELLA_App_Config_' + new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10) + '.json', 'application/json');
    jstatus('success','Configuration exported. The API key was excluded for safety.');
  }catch(err){ jstatus('error','Export failed: ' + err.message); }
};

// ---- JSON import ----
document.getElementById('jsonImportBtn').onclick = function(){ document.getElementById('jsonFileInput').click(); };
document.getElementById('jsonFileInput').addEventListener('change', function(e){
  var f = e.target.files[0]; if(!f) return;
  var rd = new FileReader();
  rd.onload = function(ev){
    try{
      var pkg = JSON.parse(ev.target.result);
      var d = pkg && pkg.data ? pkg.data : pkg;   // accept bare data objects too
      var need = ['jobTags','equipmentTypes','equipment','activityHazards','equipmentHazards'];
      var miss = need.filter(function(k){ return !Array.isArray(d[k]); });
      if(miss.length) throw new Error('Missing or invalid section(s): ' + miss.join(', '));
      if(!d.activityHazards.length && !d.equipmentHazards.length) throw new Error('No hazard rows in file.');
      if(!Array.isArray(d.presets)) d.presets = [];
      DATA = d; persist(DATA);
      if(pkg.settings) writeSettings(pkg.settings);
      reinit(); summary();
      jstatus('success','Imported: ' + d.activityHazards.length + ' activity + ' + d.equipmentHazards.length +
        ' equipment hazards, ' + d.jobTags.length + ' activities, ' + d.presets.length + ' presets.');
    }catch(err){ jstatus('error','Could not import: ' + err.message); }
    e.target.value = '';
  };
  rd.readAsText(f);
});

// ---- Assessment log ----
function renderLog(){
  var log = readLog();
  var sum = document.getElementById('logSummary');
  var list = document.getElementById('logList');
  if(!sum || !list) return;
  if(!log.length){
    sum.innerHTML = 'No assessments generated on this device yet.';
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="log-empty">The log fills automatically each time an assessment is generated.</div>';
    return;
  }
  var tot = log.length;
  var ext = log.reduce(function(a,x){ return a + (x.extreme||0); }, 0);
  var haz = log.reduce(function(a,x){ return a + (x.hazardCount||0); }, 0);
  var first = log[log.length-1].generatedAt.slice(0,10);
  sum.innerHTML = '<b>'+tot+'</b> assessments generated since '+first+'<br>'+
                  '<b>'+haz+'</b> hazards assessed in total &nbsp; <b>'+ext+'</b> extreme-rated';
  list.innerHTML = '';
  log.slice(0,60).forEach(function(x){
    var d = document.createElement('div'); d.className='log-row';
    var when = new Date(x.generatedAt).toLocaleString('en-GB',{day:'2-digit',month:'short',year:'numeric',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
    d.innerHTML = '<div class="ls">'+esc(x.serial)+'</div>'+
      '<div class="lm">'+esc(x.jobType||'(no job type)')+'<br>'+
      esc(x.equipmentRef||x.equipmentType)+' · '+esc(x.equipmentMotion)+' / '+esc(x.equipmentStatus)+'<br>'+
      when+' · '+x.activityCount+' activities · '+x.hazardCount+' hazards'+
      (x.generationMethod? '<br>Method: '+esc(x.generationMethod):'')+
      (x.hazardsExcluded? ' · '+x.hazardsExcluded+' excluded by assessor':'')+
      (x.extreme? ' · '+x.extreme+' extreme':'')+
      (x.assessedBy? '<br>Assessed by: '+esc(x.assessedBy):'')+'</div>';
    list.appendChild(d);
  });
  if(log.length>60) list.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div class="log-empty">Showing 60 most recent of '+log.length+'. Export for the full record.</div>');
}

document.getElementById('logJsonBtn').onclick = function(){
  var log = readLog();
  if(!log.length){ lstatus('error','Log is empty — nothing to export.'); return; }
  downloadBlob(JSON.stringify(log, null, 2),
    'OKPELLA_Assessment_Log_' + new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10) + '.json', 'application/json');
  lstatus('success','Exported '+log.length+' log entries as JSON.');
};

document.getElementById('logCsvBtn').onclick = function(){
  var log = readLog();
  if(!log.length){ lstatus('error','Log is empty — nothing to export.'); return; }
  var cols = ['serial','generatedAt','jobType','equipmentType','equipmentRef','equipmentMotion','equipmentStatus',
              'location','workArea','department','generationMethod','activityCount','hazardCount','hazardsIncluded','hazardsExcluded','exportedAt','extreme','high','moderate','low','negligible',
              'assessedBy','approvedBy','activities'];
  var q = function(v){
    if(Array.isArray(v)) v = v.join('; ');
    v = (v===undefined||v===null) ? '' : String(v);
    return '"' + v.replace(/"/g,'""') + '"';
  };
  var csv = cols.join(',') + '\r\n' +
    log.map(function(r){ return cols.map(function(c){ return q(r[c]); }).join(','); }).join('\r\n');
  downloadBlob('\ufeff'+csv, 'OKPELLA_Assessment_Log_' + new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10) + '.csv', 'text/csv');
  lstatus('success','Exported '+log.length+' log entries as CSV.');
};

document.getElementById('logClearBtn').onclick = function(){
  var log = readLog();
  if(!log.length){ lstatus('error','Log is already empty.'); return; }
  if(!window.confirm('Permanently delete all '+log.length+' log entries on this device? Export first if you need the record.')) return;
  writeLog([]); renderLog(); lstatus('success','Assessment log cleared.');
};
</script>
</body>
</html>
